To improve contextual modeling in compact segmentation networks, we introduced Direction-Group Graph Selective Scan (DG-GSS), which treated scan-direction and channel-group responses as graph nodes and enabled structured information exchange before multi-directional fusion. We further incorporated DINOv3-GAD supervision, where a frozen DINOv3 teacher provided semantic guidance during training, and Gradient-Adaptive Distillation dynamically regulated the distillation strength. GAD-MambaUNet achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency balance compared with representative lightweight and general segmentation methods. Ablation studies further verify the effectiveness of DG-GSS and training-time DINOv3-GAD supervision. In future work, we will explore more flexible teacher--student alignment strategies and extend the proposed framework to more diverse medical segmentation scenarios, such as multi-class and multi-modal segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [104] [ASTRA-SR: Atmospheric Seeing and Turbulence Restoration for Astronomical Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26731)
*Xining Ge,Ziteng Cui,Shuhong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ASTRA-SR：基于物理仿真合成数据的盲单帧行星图像复原框架，联合去噪、去模糊与超分，前景PSNR较最强基线提升0.49 dB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地基行星成像受大气湍流、传感器噪声与采样不足限制，复原本质上是去噪、去模糊与超分辨的联合问题；同时真实配对HR/LR数据难以获取，因此需要物理可信的合成数据与盲复原方法。

Method: 构建物理驱动合成数据集：以高动态范围航天器RAW观测为干净源，用实测的分层积分湍流强度、传播的移动相位屏、曝光平均的空间变化PSF以及传感器噪声模型合成配对LR输入。ASTRA-SR采用两阶段盲单帧复原：先估计噪声抑制但保留模糊的LR图像，再通过多尺度处理恢复空间结构，并用串行空间-幅度细化重建HR细节。

Result: 在前景区域PSNR上比最强基线方法提升0.49 dB。

Conclusion: 将湍流物理建模与分阶段盲复原网络结合，可在缺乏真实配对数据的行星成像场景中有效实现联合去噪、去模糊与超分，为遥感/天文图像复原提供可复现的数据合成与网络设计范式。

Abstract: Ground-based planetary imaging suffers from atmospheric turbulence, sensor noise, and limited sampling, making restoration a joint denoising, deblurring, and super-resolution problem. We present ASTRA-SR, a blind single-frame restoration framework trained on a physics-grounded synthetic dataset. High-dynamic-range spacecraft RAW observations serve as clean sources, and paired LR inputs are synthesized using measured layer-integrated turbulence strengths, propagated moving phase screens, exposure-averaged spatially varying PSFs, and sensor this http URL -SR first estimates a noise-suppressed but blur-retaining LR image, then restores spatial structure through multiscale processing and reconstructs HR detail with serial spatial-amplitude refinement. It yields a 0.49 dB foreground PSNR gain over the strongest baseline approaches.

</details>


### [105] [Evaluating the Semantic-to-Geometric Gap in Adversarial Defenses Against Vision-Language Model-Based Plagiarism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26733)
*Christopher Burger,Christina Trotter,Joseph Carlisle,Charles Walter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在学术诚信场景中的脆弱性：通过人工设计的对抗性图像变换（保持人类可读性）来降低VLM对电路图、逻辑门与卡诺图的解题能力，论证视觉扰动可作为短期"止血"手段，但长期评估安全需重新设计考核形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM能力快速提升，学生可将图形化题目拍照直接提交，形成所谓"trivial plagiarism"，威胁学术诚信；教育者需要可操作的VLM能力边界数据来决定应对策略。

Method: 两阶段评估：第一阶段对电路图任务进行人工评估，测定VLM基线能力；第二阶段对拓扑结构（逻辑门）与坐标几何（卡诺图）开展自动化大规模评估，施加启发式对抗性图像变换（保持语义对人类可读）并测量性能退化。

Result: 高性能VLM表现出一定鲁棒性，但所有被测模型均对对抗性扰动存在脆弱性；人工可读的图像变换能有效降低模型解题正确率。

Conclusion: 视觉扰动只能作为短期过渡方案；随着VLM能力持续提升，长期评估安全要求教育者从根本上重新设计考核方式。

Abstract: The rapidly advancing capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) present a systemic challenge to academic integrity. VLMs now allow students to bypass meaningful engagement by capturing and submitting graphical problems as singular images, a practice we define as trivial plagiarism. To provide educators with actionable data on VLM limitations, we investigate the efficacy of heuristic adversarial image transformations designed to degrade model performance while remaining human-interpretable. Through a two-phase evaluation of introductory assessments, we manually assess baseline VLM performance on circuit diagrams, followed by an automated large-scale evaluation of topological structures (logic gates) and coordinate geometry (Karnaugh maps). We find that while highly capable VLMs can exhibit appreciable robustness, all models suffer vulnerability to adversarial perturbations. We conclude that while visual perturbations act as a viable near-term stopgap, long-term assessment security requires educators to reapproach assessment design given continually increasing VLM performance.

</details>


### [106] [FleXray: Universal Clinical X-ray Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26756)
*Victor Ion Butoi,Vivek Gopalakrishnan,John V. Guttag,Adrian V. Dalca,Neel Dey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FleXray：利用全身3D CT分割数据与生成式图像编辑模型构建物理仿真X光数据引擎，生成带完整标注的2D X光仿真数据，训练出可分割全身60个解剖结构的通用X光分割模型，并支持疾病分级量化测量与X光引导介入导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X光是使用最广但量化程度最低的影像模态：3D解剖被压缩为2D投影，结构重叠、边界模糊，导致人工标注X光分割数据库不现实，形态学与功能分析长期局限于狭窄解剖区域和应用。作者希望以可扩展的仿真数据替代人工标注，构建全身通用的X光解剖分割模型。

Method: 不依赖人工标注，而是构建基于物理的生成式X光数据引擎：复用现有3D全身CT分割数据集，结合生成式图像编辑模型，仿真出具有多样外观、生理属性与成像几何的、带完整标注的2D X光图像，并在此仿真数据上训练FleXray全身解剖分割通用模型。

Result: FleXray在未见过的研究数据集与真实场景（in-the-wild）X光上均能准确分割60个解剖结构；并进一步实现X光的可量化分析，包括疾病分级的自动测量、X光引导介入过程中的稳健导航，以及病理目标的数据高效学习。

Conclusion: 仿真数据引擎可有效替代人工标注，支撑全身通用X光分割，并使X光可用于自动化定量分析；作者开源模型、代码、全身X光分割数据集及本地浏览器工具。

Abstract: X-ray is medicine's most widely used imaging modality, yet remains among its least quantitative. Unlike volumetric modalities like CT or MRI, X-ray collapses 3D anatomy into a 2D projection, causing structures to overlap and anatomical boundaries to be ambiguous, even to experts. As a result, labeling X-ray databases for training general-purpose segmentation systems is impractical, leaving morphometric and functional X-ray analysis confined to narrow anatomical regions and applications. To this end, we present FleXray, a generalist model for anatomical segmentation across the entire body in clinical X-rays. Instead of curating large, manually annotated X-ray datasets, we build a scalable, physics-based generative X-ray data engine. Using existing 3D whole-body CT segmentation datasets and generative image-editing models, we simulate fully-annotated 2D X-rays with diverse appearances, physiological properties, and imaging geometries. Trained on these simulations, FleXray accurately segments 60 anatomical structures across unseen research datasets and in-the-wild X-rays. We further show that FleXray makes X-rays directly amenable to quantitative analysis, enabling automated measurements for disease grading, robust navigation during X-ray-guided interventions, and data-efficient learning of pathological targets. We release the model, code, a full-body X-ray segmentation dataset, and a local, easy-to-use browser-based tool at this https URL .

</details>


### [107] [StableVQ: Practical Guidelines for Stable Vector-Quantized Tokenizer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26774)
*Bao Tang,Jiahao Guo,Haoxiang Cao,Wenyu Liu,Changqian Yu,Kun Gai,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StableVQ 通过解耦编码器-解码器与码本的学习目标（Dynamic STE、Region VQ Loss、Decoupled Schedule），在不增加可学习参数的前提下提升 VQ 离散视觉 tokenizer 的训练稳定性、码本利用率与重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VQ 是现代自回归与掩码图像生成模型中离散视觉 tokenizer 的核心，近期共享投影码本（shared-projection codebook）方法虽显著提升了码本利用率，但训练稳定性仍是关键且缺乏探索的问题。作者认为根因在于 Encoder-Decoder 与 Codebook 的训练相互纠缠：两个子系统各自无法独立履行自身职责，系统只有在二者恰好协作时才能工作，这种脆弱条件在训练最紧张时（如低码本利用率、特定初始化）恰好崩溃。

Method: 提出 StableVQ，重新审视各模块的合理学习目标并分别解决独立训练时出现的问题：(1) Dynamic STE 修正编码器学习目标中的不稳定性，使其在离散正则下即使码本利用率低也能稳健优化重建空间；(2) Region VQ Loss 重新设计码本学习目标，使其能独立保证对编码器输出分布的完整跟踪，不依赖编码器振荡来驱动激活；(3) Decoupled Schedule 认为编码器-解码器与码本的不同职责需要不同的优化动力学，为二者分配独立学习率调度以保证系统级稳健性。方法构建于共享投影码本之上，轻量且不引入可学习参数。

Result: 在 ImageNet 上的实验表明，在多种码本尺寸与初始化设置下，训练稳定性、码本利用率与重建质量均获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 训练不稳定的根因是编码器-解码器与码本的训练纠缠；通过解耦二者职责并分别为其设计学习目标与优化动力学，可在零额外参数代价下显著提升 VQ tokenizer 的稳定性与重建性能。

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) is fundamental to discrete visual tokenizers that power modern autoregressive and masked image generation models. While recent shared-projection codebook methods have substantially advanced codebook utilization, training stability remains a critical and underexplored challenge. We argue that the root cause lies in the entanglement of the Encoder--Decoder and Codebook training: because neither module can reliably fulfill its own responsibility in isolation, the system can only function when the two subsystems happen to cooperate---a fragile condition that breaks down precisely when training is most stressed. We propose StableVQ, which revisits the proper learning objective of each module and resolves the problems that arise when each is trained to fulfill its own role independently. Concretely, (1) Dynamic STE corrects the instability in the Encoder's learning objective, enabling it to robustly optimize the reconstruction space under discrete regularization even when codebook utilization is low. (2) Region VQ Loss reconceives the Codebook's learning objective so that it can independently guarantee full tracking of the encoder output distribution, without relying on encoder oscillations to drive activation. (3) Decoupled Schedule recognizes that the distinct responsibilities of the Encoder--Decoder and the Codebook demand distinct optimization dynamics, and assigns each an independent learning rate schedule to ensure robust system-level behavior. Built on top of shared-projection codebooks, StableVQ is lightweight and introduces no learnable parameters. Experiments on ImageNet demonstrate consistent improvements in training stability, codebook utilization, and reconstruction quality across diverse codebook sizes and initialization settings.

</details>


### [108] [HARMONY: Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for MONocular Image-to-Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26793)
*Shufan Sun,Chen Wang,Enxin Song,Jiatao Gu,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HARMONY：分层思维链框架，结合智能体VLM推理与视觉几何基础模型，从单张单目室内图像恢复完整3D场景（布局+物体分层放置+几何精修），在合成与真实图像上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图组合式3D场景重建存在两条路线各自缺陷：智能体推理提供空间语义关系但缺乏与输入图像的精确对齐；视觉几何基础模型可预测稠密点图但重建质量有限。因此同时满足"物体间关系准确"与"高保真重建质量"的单目完整3D场景恢复仍具挑战。

Method: HARMONY 采用分层思维链：从空3D楼层平面图出发，先对相机做相对参考图像标定，建立语义化空间坐标系；再由智能体VLM推理恢复房间布局与初始放置顺序；按"墙面附着元素→独立家具→家具上的附属装饰"的分层顺序放置物体，家具采用深度优先遍历，使每次放置条件化于已解析结构；引入反思式反馈回路抑制误差累积；每次VLM放置后用点云估计做几何精修，使渲染图像与输入对齐。

Result: 在合成与真实世界图像上的实验表明，HARMONY 优于所评估的重建基线；与 GPT-6 Astra 的定性对比显示其物体排布更忠实、场景细节保持更好。

Conclusion: HARMONY 将智能体推理与视觉几何基础相结合，可生成语义一致且感知上与参考图像对齐的3D场景，把单图组合式重建拓展到复杂室内场景；后续可关注其在更开放场景、实时性与开源实现上的表现。

Abstract: Compositional 3D scene reconstruction has recently been explored from two directions: agentic reasoning that provides semantic understanding of spatial relationships but lacks precise alignment with input images; and visual geometry foundation models that predict dense point maps from input images but the reconstruction quality is limited. Therefore, recovering a complete 3D scene from a single monocular image with accurate inter-object relationships and high-fidelity reconstruction quality remains challenging. In this paper, we present HARMONY, a hierarchical chain-of-thought framework that leverages both agentic reasoning and visual geometry foundation. Given an image of an indoor scene, starting from an empty 3D floorplan, HARMONY first calibrates the camera against the reference image to establish a semantically-grounded spatial frame, then uses agentic VLM reasoning to recover the 3D room layout and an initial placement order. It then places the objects in a hierarchical order, from wall-mounted elements, free-standing furniture, to dependent decorations on top of furniture. We also use depth-first traversal for furniture so each placement conditions on previously resolved structure and a reflective feedback loop to avoid error accumulation. After each object placement by VLM, we use the point cloud estimations to perform geometry-based refinement so that the rendered image aligns better with the input. HARMONY can produce 3D scenes that are semantically consistent and perceptually aligned with the reference image, extending single-image compositional reconstruction to complex indoor scene images. Experiments on synthetic and real-world images demonstrate that HARMONY outperforms the evaluated reconstruction baselines, while qualitative comparisons with GPT-6 Astra suggest more faithful object arrangements and better preservation of scene details.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Towards Adaptive Interaction Strategies for Human Companion Robot via Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25031)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习(DRL)的人-机器人伴随策略,机器人可动态调整跟随位置;定义交互空间作为状态空间,结合MPPI与CBF构建控制器,在室内外真实环境中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HRI伴随方法将机器人限制在人后方/前方/侧方的固定相对位置,难以适应动态工作空间,牺牲了灵活性与适应性。

Method: 以"交互空间"刻画人-机器人-环境关系并作为DRL状态空间;用强化学习决策动态跟随位置;底层采用MPPI(Model Predictive Path Integral)结合控制屏障函数(CBF)实现位置与朝向的精确跟踪,兼顾避障与社交安全。

Result: 室内外真实场景实验显示,成功率提升至少24%,跟踪精度提升至少47%,人体舒适度提升;可伴随以1.7 m/s行走的人,不局限于固定跟随位置,并保持对隐私空间的尊重与有效避障。

Conclusion: DRL+MPPI+CBF的伴随框架能在真实动态环境中实现灵活、安全、社交可接受的人-机器人伴随,优于固定位置策略及对比方法。

Abstract: In the field of Human-Robot Interaction (HRI), achieving flexibility in human-accompanying within real-world environments holds great potential for various applications but also poses significant challenges. Traditional methods typically restrict robots to fixed positions relative to humans, such as tracking from behind, in front, or side-by-side, which limits robot adaptability in dynamic workspaces. This study introduces a novel human-companioning strategy that uses Reinforcement Learning (DRL) to enable mobile robots to dynamically adjust their tracking positions according to varying conditions. An interaction space is defined to capture the relationship between the human and the robot while considering the environment, which serves as the basis for state spaces in DRL to assist the robot in adapting to environmental changes. A human-robot companion controller is developed by integrating Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control with Control Barrier Functions (CBF), ensuring that the robot accurately follows the target's movement in both position and orientation while avoiding obstacles and enhancing social acceptance and safety. The proposed approach is evaluated in real-world scenarios, both indoors and outdoors, and compared with other studies. The results show that the proposed method improves the success rate and tracking accuracy by at least 24% and 47%, respectively, while enhancing human comfort. Experiments demonstrate the robot's ability to flexibly accompany a person walking at speeds of up to 1.7 m/s, dynamically adjusting its strategy without being confined to a fixed position. Additionally, the robot respects the human's intimate space to ensure safety, comfort, and effective obstacle avoidance.

</details>


### [110] [Cosserat Modeling of Trimmed Helicoid Soft Arms with a Separated-Section Constitutive Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25264)
*Zhihang Qin,Linxin Hou,Zeyu Zhong,Yuchen Sun,Wenci Xin,Yueheng Zhang,Ji Qi,Jie Wang,Muhammad Sunny Nazeer,Yu Jun Tan,Federico Renda,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对修剪螺旋臂中螺旋域分离、仅靠稀疏融合交叉点连接的几何特点，提出分离截面本构律，将各螺旋域在局部坐标系下的响应映射回主干，并结合稀疏融合力学得到有效主干刚度，嵌入几何精确动态Cosserat模型，实现约0.3 s/次的快速整臂求解与准确位置预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Cosserat杆模型假设截面刚度由同一截面上材料属性叠加得到，这一假设对修剪螺旋臂（trimmed helicoid arms）不成立：其承载螺旋域彼此分离，仅通过稀疏融合交叉点连接，导致截面刚度、尤其是各向异性柔顺性无法被准确描述，进而影响建模、规划与状态估计精度。

Method: 提出分离截面本构律：各螺旋域在自身局部坐标系中求本构响应，再拉回主干得到有效主干刚度；用稀疏融合力学刻画相邻域相对运动带来的附加柔顺，并确定弯曲、扭转、拉伸的通道折减剖面 ηc(s/L)。将所得有效截面刚度嵌入几何精确动态Cosserat模型，采用GVS离散化与走线肌腱驱动，实现快速整臂求解。

Result: 有效截面刚度呈强各向异性：弯曲与拉伸刚度约降低一个数量级，扭转刚度接近有效主干刚度。在103组实测构型上，三个数据集的汇总归一化位置误差分别为7.7%、6.7%、7.8%；单次整臂求解在单核CPU（Intel Xeon Cascade Lake, 2.8 GHz）上约需0.3 s，支持基于模型的快速规划、状态与载荷估计以及形态—控制协同设计。

Conclusion: 分离截面本构律与稀疏融合力学能更准确地描述修剪螺旋臂的柔顺行为，所得模型兼顾精度与计算效率，为架构化软体机器人的建模、规划、状态/载荷估计和形态—控制协同设计提供可用工具。

Abstract: Cosserat rod models for soft robots usually construct sectional stiffness by summing material properties over a common cross-section. This assumption becomes inaccurate for trimmed helicoid arms, where load-bearing helix domains are separated and connected only through sparse fused crossings. This paper formulates a separated-section constitutive law that evaluates each helix domain in its local frame and pulls its constitutive response back to the backbone, yielding an effective backbone stiffness. Sparse-fusion mechanics captures the additional compliance caused by relative motion between neighboring domains and determines channel-wise reduction profiles $\eta_c(s/L)$ for bending, torsion, and extension. The resulting effective sectional stiffness is strongly anisotropic: bending and extension are reduced by about one order of magnitude, whereas torsion remains close to the effective backbone stiffness. The resulting sectional law is embedded in a geometrically exact dynamic Cosserat model with GVS discretization and routed-tendon actuation. Across 103 measured configurations, the three datasets give pooled normalized position errors of \SI{7.7}{\percent}, \SI{6.7}{\percent}, and \SI{7.8}{\percent}, while each full-arm solve requires approximately \SI{0.3}{s} on one CPU core (Intel Xeon, Cascade Lake, \SI{2.8}{GHz}), enabling rapid model-based planning, state and load estimation, and morphology--control co-design for architected soft robots.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond the Flat Seafloor: A Closed-Form Two-View Constraint to Aid Sidescan Sonar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25271)
*Kalin Norman,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将多视图几何引入侧扫声呐，推导两视图公共特征的几何约束（球面与平面交线轨迹），并通过蒙特卡洛仿真刻画模糊轨迹长度的影响因素，给出水面船与水下航行器的测量规划建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧扫声呐广泛用于有人/自主海洋探测测绘，但现有方法极少利用其几何投影模型，多采用平坦海床等粗略假设；作者希望不依赖此类近似，建立严格的多视图几何框架。

Method: 基于侧扫声呐一维距离测量的投影模型，形式化两视图几何约束，证明共享特征被约束在球面与平面之交的轨迹（locus）上；用真实孔径与安装几何开展蒙特卡洛仿真，覆盖水面船与水下航行器两种平台，并仿真相对运动轨迹。

Result: 轨迹长度主要由俯仰/高度角失配（elevation misalignment）决定，在约20°的中等斜交叉角处达到峰值，在近平行与反平行航迹下轨迹最短；据此转化为具体的测绘规划准则。

Conclusion: 侧扫声呐的模糊性可通过多视图几何与航迹规划有效约束，所提约束与规划指南为水下测绘的视图配置提供理论依据。

Abstract: Sidescan sonar is a common sensor for both manned and autonomous marine exploration and mapping, yet very few methods build upon or exploit the geometric projection model of the sensor. As sidescan sonar is limited to a 1D range measurement, many approximations are frequently used, including the long-standing assumption of a flat seafloor. Rather than make similar approximations, this paper focuses on a multi-view geometry based approach and formalizes a two-view geometric constraint and proves that a shared feature is constrained to a locus within the intersection of a sphere and a plane. In addition, we characterize what governs the size of the ambiguity locus through Monte Carlo simulation that is grounded in real aperture and mounting geometry for both a surface vessel and an underwater vehicle. We translate additional simulations of relative trajectories for both vehicle platforms into concrete survey-planning guidance. Our results show that locus length is strongly governed by elevation misalignment, and peaks at a moderate oblique crossing angle of approximately 20 degrees, with minimal locus lengths obtained at near parallel and anti-parallel passes.

</details>


### [112] [Learning to Plan in Human-Robot Collaboration: Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25274)
*Afagh Mehri Shervedani,Siyu Li,Natawut Monaikul,Bahareh Abbasi,Barbara Di Eugenio,Miloš Žefran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用强化学习自动生成机器人多模态交互策略（语言+物理动作），在家庭物品寻找场景中训练并真人验证，替代手工设计的交互管理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助老年人和残障人士的机器人需与用户协作完成任务，核心是交互管理器：观察评估任务、推断人的状态与意图、选择最优动作。由于该领域数据稀疏，多模态策略多靠手工设计；随着交互复杂度上升，手工方式不可扩展。因此需要自动化的策略生成方法。

Method: 用强化学习自动生成机器人多模态策略。场景设定为机器人在家庭环境中协助用户定位物体，需联合处理语言与物理动作两类模态以选动作。与传统对话系统不同，训练使用基于人类数据构建的模拟器，可处理多模态。奖励函数为简单高层奖励、无需微调，并施加若干前置条件加速训练。最终以真实环境人因研究进行评估。

Result: 在真实世界环境下开展人类研究，结果积极：可用性高、任务完成效果良好；表明无需手工设计即可获得可用的多模态交互策略。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习的交互管理器为多模态人机协作提供了一种可扩展且可解释的替代方案。局限在于仅在单一协助找物场景验证，且依赖模拟器与人类数据，跨任务泛化性尚待考察。

Abstract: Robot assistants for older adults and people with disabilities need to perform collaborative tasks with users effectively. The core component of these systems is an interaction manager whose job is to observe and assess the task and infer the state of the human and their intent for the robot to choose the best course of action. Due to the sparseness of the data in this domain, the policy for such multimodal systems is often crafted by hand; as the complexity of interactions grows, this process is not scalable. This paper proposes a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to automatically generate the multimodal policy of the robot. Our system focuses on a realistic scenario where a robot assists a user in locating objects within a home environment, managing multimodal signals, including language and physical actions, to select the best action. In contrast to traditional dialog systems, our agent is trained with a simulator that uses human data and can deal with multiple modalities. We use a simple high-level reward function that needs no fine-tuning and enforce some preconditions to speed up the training process. A human study evaluating the system in a real-world setting demonstrates promising results, indicating high usability and effective task completion. This RL-based approach offers a scalable and interpretable alternative for designing interaction managers in multimodal human-robot collaborations.

</details>


### [113] [JAMB: Joint Action-Motion Diffusion for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25322)
*Chuyang Xiao,Peilin Meng,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JAMB：将双臂动作与未来3D点轨迹在同一Transformer中去噪，实现几何感知的协同双臂操作扩散策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂协同操作中，任一臂的运动都会改变共享3D场景并影响另一臂，但多数扩散策略不显式建模这种未来几何后果；已有的预测式变体仅把未来状态当作辅助监督或固定条件，未能让动作与几何假设相互迭代修正。

Method: 提出JAMB扩散策略，联合去噪双臂动作与未来3D点轨迹：动作假设与轨迹假设在共享Transformer内同步演化并相互引导；并将多模态表示统一grounding到共享时空坐标系，以支持联合去噪过程中的几何感知交互。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的16个仿真任务上平均成功率83.4%，比最强基线高23.9个百分点；三个真实机器人任务上分别比纯动作策略与辅助几何预测方法高50.0与21.2个百分点；在杂乱场景与分布外背景下泛化更强。

Conclusion: 联合动作-运动建模框架能有效提升协同双臂操作的成功率与泛化性，说明让动作与未来几何在去噪中互相精化是关键设计。

Abstract: Coordinated bimanual manipulation is challenging because the motion of either arm can alter the shared 3D scene and thereby affect the other arm. Yet most diffusion policies generate actions without explicitly modeling these future geometric consequences, while predictive variants typically use future state only as auxiliary supervision or fixed conditioning. We address this limitation by proposing JAMB, a diffusion policy that jointly denoises bimanual actions and future 3D point tracks. By allowing action and track hypotheses to evolve together within a shared Transformer, each can inform and refine the other throughout denoising. We further ground multimodal representations in a shared spatiotemporal coordinate system to facilitate geometry-aware interaction during joint denoising. We evaluate JAMB on diverse bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and on a real-world robot, comparing it with action-only policies and alternative future-prediction approaches spanning different state representations and learning objectives. Across 16 simulation tasks, JAMB achieves an average success rate of 83.4%, outperforming the strongest baseline by 23.9 percentage points. On three real-world tasks, it outperforms the action-only and auxiliary geometry prediction methods by 50.0 and 21.2 percentage points, respectively. Beyond these performance gains, JAMB shows stronger generalization to cluttered scenes and out-of-distribution backgrounds than the evaluated baselines. Together, these results demonstrate the effectiveness of our joint action-motion modeling framework for coordinated bimanual manipulation. Our project website is available at this https URL

</details>


### [114] [GINIO: A Geometric SO(3)-Equivariant Interface for Neural Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25338)
*Chankyo Kim,Minghan Zhu,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Avantika Rattan,Maani Ghaffari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GINIO：面向神经惯性里程计的SO(3)等变几何接口，通过张量律约束的测量与不确定性预测+Last-Frame Alignment，在TLIO/NanoBench/Fetch上大幅提升任意IMU安装旋转下的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经惯性里程计（NIO）常把网络作为滤波管线中的“学习测量”，但这类测量在任意IMU安装坐标系旋转下应满足几何一致性：均值按向量变换、协方差按二阶张量同余变换。现有方法（如EqNIO等）在非标准/未见安装姿态下会严重退化，缺乏可证明的等变保证与高效训练机制。

Method: 提出GINIO：SO(3)等变接口，在标定IMU窗口上预测满足张量变换律的运动测量与不确定性（谱协方差头替代对角头）。引入Last-Frame Alignment（LFA）确定性预处理，可证明对SO(3)等变预测器等价于世界系训练。连接估计器在传感器局部跟踪IMU偏置等状态，分离干扰量估计与几何律。将同一接口实例化到滤波连接的NIO、AirIO式循环空中预测、EqNIO式全SO(3)规范化、ResNet式时间骨干四类架构。

Result: TLIO：GINIO 2.018 m ID/SO(3) ATE，EqNIO退化至76.389 m，FLOPs少11.6倍；NanoBench：AirIO式改进ATE 5.579→1.430 m（无外部姿态输入），ResNet式0.581 m vs ResNet1D 0.645 m；Fetch：未见物理重装ATE从8.15→0.50 m且无需重训练。不确定性：谱协方差相较对角头将协方差等变误差降低三个数量级以上。

Conclusion: GINIO为NIO提供了可证明的SO(3)等变接口与高效传感器系训练范式，在多种骨干与数据集上兼顾精度、计算效率和对未见安装变换的鲁棒性，且经验上超出精确坐标框架保证的适用范围。

Abstract: Neural inertial odometry increasingly uses networks as learned measurements inside filtering pipelines. Such measurements should transform consistently under arbitrary IMU mounting conventions: their mean must transform as a vector, and their covariance must transform congruently as a second-order tensor. We present GINIO, a geometric SO(3)-equivariant interface for neural inertial odometry under arbitrary rotations of the IMU measurement frame. Given calibrated IMU windows, our framework predicts a motion measurement and uncertainty obeying these tensorial laws. To support efficient sensor-frame learning, we introduce Last-Frame Alignment (LFA), a deterministic preprocessing step that is provably equivalent to world-frame training for SO(3)-equivariant predictors. The connected estimator tracks sensor-local states such as IMU bias, separating nuisance estimation from the geometric law enforced by the learned measurement. We instantiate the same interface in filter-connected NIO, AirIO-style recurrent aerial prediction, EqNIO-style full-SO(3) canonicalization, and ResNet-style temporal backbones. On TLIO, GINIO achieves 2.018 m ID/SO(3) ATE while EqNIO degrades to 76.389 m, using 11.6x fewer FLOPs. On NanoBench, our AirIO-style instantiation improves ATE from 5.579 m to 1.430 m without external attitude input, and our ResNet-style instantiation reaches 0.581 m ATE versus 0.645 m for ResNet1D. On Fetch, GINIO empirically reduces unseen physical-remount ATE from 8.15 m to 0.50 m without retraining, demonstrating robustness beyond the exact coordinate-frame guarantee. For uncertainty, spectral covariance reduces covariance-equivariance error by over three orders of magnitude compared with a diagonal head.

</details>


### [115] [Learning from Humans for Proactive Assistance in Human-Robot Collaborative Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25351)
*Elvin Yang,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROACT：将人类协作行为预测融入顺从全身控制的人-机器人协同搬运框架，用Transformer从大规模真实双人搬运数据中预测物体未来运动，作为前馈意图信号；在9-DoF移动机械臂108次真实试验中显著降低交互功与完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同搬运要求机器人在高效移动物体的同时保持对用户输入的身体顺从性，但已有工作往往割裂二者：要么高效却抗拒人类推力，要么顺从却需持续人工引导。作者的关键洞察是：障碍约束下的协同搬运需要将人类协作行为预测与顺从控制相融合。

Method: PROACT框架在顺从式全身控制中嵌入"预期"能力：基于大规模真实双人搬运示范数据训练Transformer模型，蒸馏人类协作行为以预测物体未来运动轨迹，并将该预测作为前馈目标融入顺从全身控制器，使机器人主动分担任务而非被动响应。

Result: 在9-DoF移动机械臂上完成108次真实世界试验，相比纯顺从基线与MPC基线，平均交互功分别降低59.2%与20.4%，平均完成时间分别缩短12.9%与6.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，把学习到的人类协作行为预测与顺从机器人控制结合，可在真实障碍环境中同时提升协作效率与人机物理响应性，为协同搬运提供了一种兼顾主动性与顺从性的方案。

Abstract: We focus on human-robot collaborative transport, a challenging task of broad relevance spanning logistics, manufacturing, and the home, in which a user and a robot work together to relocate a large or heavy object. To act as an effective partner, the robot should reduce the user's effort by contributing to efficient relocation of the object while remaining physically responsive to them. Prior work often addresses these capabilities separately, producing robots that may move the object efficiently but resist user input, or accommodate the user but depend on continuous guidance. Our key insight is that obstacle-constrained collaborative transport requires integrating predictions of human collaborative behavior with compliant robot control. To this end, we introduce PROACT, a framework for human-robot collaborative transport that incorporates anticipation into compliant whole-body control through a learned model of human collaborative behavior. Trained on a large-scale, real-world dataset of dyadic human transport demonstrations, our transformer architecture distills collaborative behavior into predictions of future object motion. Across 108 real-world trials with a 9-DoF mobile manipulator, PROACT reduces mean interaction work by 59.2\% and 20.4\%, and mean completion time by 12.9\% and 6.9\%, relative to compliance-only and MPC baselines, respectively. Footage from our experiments can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [116] [HOTICE: Whole-Body Humanoid Object Transportation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25363)
*Toan Nguyen,Weiduo Yuan,Siheng Zhao,Yue Wang,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HOTICE：面向人形机器人穿越杂乱环境的全身搬运框架，通过解耦势场、双智能体RL与专家到通用蒸馏实现sim2real部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实人类环境中，人形机器人搬运物体时，机器人与所携物体周围自由空间常被杂物挤压，现有方法难以在机器人本体与载荷同时受约束时规划无碰撞动作。全身loco-manipulation动作空间大、上下肢协调难，且策略需泛化到多样杂乱场景。

Method: 1) Humanoid-Object Decoupled Potential Fields，联合编码机器人与被搬运物体的避障引导，实现两者的协调避障运动；2) 双智能体强化学习架构，解耦上肢与下肢控制，同时通过共享状态观测与奖励保持全身协调；3) 专家到通用（specialist-to-generalist）蒸馏，将特权教师策略蒸馏为单一可部署学生策略。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真与真实Unitree G1人形机器人上评估，对多种形状物体在杂乱场景中实现有效、鲁棒的搬运；可靠协调全身运动与物体感知避障，泛化到未见过的杂乱环境，sim2real部署表现良好。

Conclusion: HOTICE表明，物体解耦势场+双智能体RL+蒸馏的组合可有效解决杂乱环境下人形机器人全身搬运，并具备较强的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Object transportation is a fundamental capability for humanoid robots operating in real-world, human-centric environments, yet existing methods struggle when clutter constrains free space around both the robot and its carried payload. We present HOTICE, a whole-body humanoid learning framework for transporting objects through such cluttered environments. First, we introduce Humanoid-Object Decoupled Potential Fields, which jointly encode collision-avoidance guidance for the robot and the carried object, enabling coordinated, obstacle-aware motion for both. Second, to address the large action space inherent to whole-body loco-manipulation, we design a dual-agent reinforcement learning architecture that decouples upper- and lower-body control while preserving whole-body coordination via shared state observations and rewards. To train a policy that generalizes across diverse cluttered scenes, we further employ a specialist-to-generalist distillation strategy, in which privileged teacher policies are distilled into a single deployable student policy. We evaluate HOTICE in MuJoCo simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid, demonstrating effective and robust object transportation across cluttered scenarios for objects of varying shapes. Our results show that HOTICE reliably coordinates whole-body motion and object-aware collision avoidance, generalizing effectively to previously unseen cluttered environments while achieving strong performance in sim2real deployment.

</details>


### [117] [Capability-Aware Arbitration for Semantic Intent-Based Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25369)
*Zhaoda Du,Michael Bowman,Xiaoli Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出能力感知共享控制框架：VLM推断人类意图并提供语义置信度，VLA策略生成动作并在线估计能力置信度，通过贝叶斯滤波与sigmoid映射进行非线性权限仲裁，12人实验成功率92%，优于遥操作83%与两类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统共享控制仅依据对人类意图的置信度分配机器人权限，隐含假设自主执行可靠；当该假设失效时，高意图置信度会导致"过度帮助"（over-helping），反而降低性能。

Method: VLM进行语义意图推断并输出意图置信度；VLA策略产生随机动作轨迹，通过轨迹离散度与局部不稳定性在线估计能力置信度；将贝叶斯滤波后的意图置信度与VLA能力置信度经sigmoid映射组合成非线性仲裁策略，动态调整机器人权限。

Result: 任务成功率92%，高于手动遥操作83%、仅意图仲裁44%、固定等权融合10%；控制友好度更高，权限加权分歧更低。

Conclusion: 将VLA能力置信度纳入权限分配可缓解过度帮助问题，提升共享控制性能，为可信人机协作提供可行路径。

Abstract: Shared control often allocates robot authority based on confidence in inferred human intent, assuming reliable autonomous execution. When this assumption fails, high intent confidence can cause over-helping. We present a capability-aware shared-control framework in which a vision-language model (VLM) infers human intent and provides semantic-intent confidence, while a vision-language-action (VLA) policy generates autonomous actions. VLA capability confidence is estimated online from the dispersion and local instability of stochastic action trajectories. We design a nonlinear arbitration policy that combines Bayesian-filtered semantic-intent confidence with VLA capability confidence through a sigmoid mapping to adapt robot authority. Our evaluation combined VLM/VLA confidence assessment with a study involving 12 participants performing pick-and-place and bidirectional stacking under in-distribution and out-of-distribution conditions. The proposed method achieved the highest task success rate (92%), compared with manual teleoperation (83%), intent-only arbitration (44%), and fixed equal-weight blending (10%). It also achieved higher control friendliness and lower authority-weighted disagreement than both shared-control baselines. These results demonstrate the benefit of incorporating VLA capability into authority allocation to mitigate over-helping and improve shared-control performance.

</details>


### [118] [Angular momentum analysis on Karate roundhouse kicks: a longitudinal case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25374)
*Jan C. L. Lau,Christian Mele,Jonathan Feng-Shun Lin,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究空手道回旋踢中无目标后腿回摆动作的角动量（AM）管理机制，提出两种基于AM的新指标，通过一年纵向研究（作者本人为受试者，另邀一名教练作为专家对照）分析稳定与不稳定踢腿的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类步态不仅限于直线行走，还涉及大幅度的往返旋转，其力学机制尚不明确。空手道回旋踢是动态往返旋转的极端案例，研究其角动量管理可为动态人体运动的稳定性分析及机器人、外骨骼的平滑步态提供启示。

Method: 以第一作者作为空手道学员进行为期一年的纵向追踪，另招募一名空手道教练进行单次测试作为专家对照。提出两种基于角动量（AM）的新指标，并结合AM相关变量分析无目标可回收后腿回旋踢，比较稳定与不稳定踢腿，并考察这些变量与指标随技术进步的时间变化。

Result: 不稳定踢腿在击打前具有更高的峰值总角动量，击打后制动峰值更小。所提出的AM指标能够识别部分不稳定踢腿的成因，但由于不稳定可源于不同原因，无法在稳定与不稳定踢腿之间做出明确区分。这些指标可作为分析其他动态运动的性能度量。

Conclusion: 基于角动量的指标可用于分析动态人体运动，但若要作为稳定性判据，还需结合时间、运动学及压力中心（CoP）的协调性综合考虑。

Abstract: Human gait in reality extends beyond straight-line walking, with some situations even requiring drastic back-and-forth rotations. A smooth and stable execution may seem intuitive, but the underlying mechanics remains unknown. The Karate roundhouse kick may be an extreme case of exhibiting dynamic back-and-forth rotations, but investigating the angular momentum (AM) management can potentially inform stability analysis in dynamic human motions and smoother gait in robots and exoskeletons. This paper introduces two new AM-based measures and analyzes AM-related variables to study the target-less retractable back-leg Karate roundhouse kick. The purpose is to understand the underlying AM management, analyze the differences between stable and unstable kicks, and investigate how these variables and measures change over time with improvement. A one-year longitudinal study was conducted with the first author as a Karate student, and a Karate instructor was also recruited for one session as an expert, whose data is used for comparison. Results show that unstable kicks have higher peak total AM before Strike and smaller braking peak after Strike. The proposed AM-based measures are able to identify the cause of some unstable kicks, though no clear distinction could be made between stable and unstable kicks since kicks can be unstable for different reasons. Nonetheless, the proposed measures can be applied as performance measures to analyze other types of dynamic motions. To incorporate them as stability criteria, however, it is recommended to also consider their coordination with time, kinematics, and center of pressure.

</details>


### [119] [PARTE: Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation for Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25375)
*Abolfazl Babanazari,Carson Cramer,Tyler Summers,Carlos Nieto,Kaveh Fathian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出全局点云配准方法PARTE，将平面结构作为互补配准证据，通过平面上下文直方图(PCH)描述子与两级匹配建立可靠平面/点对应，并在置信度加权相容图上联合剔除离群点后估计刚体变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限重叠、重复几何与传感器噪声导致对应集被离群值主导；平面区域对常规点描述子尤为困难，常被抑制或丢弃，损失了有价值的几何线索。

Method: 提取平面块并用新颖的Plane Context Histogram (PCH)编码其周围几何；采用两级匹配筛选可靠平面对应；将点与平面候选对应统一置于置信度加权相容图中做联合离群剔除，再解算刚体变换；无可用平面时自然退化为纯点配准。

Result: 在密集RGB-D与稀疏LiDAR的6个室内外基准、共8,097对配准上评测，整体成功率优于13种标准与SOTA方法，且运行时间较低；提供开源C++实现及Python绑定。

Conclusion: 平面结构可作为独立且互补的配准证据被有效利用，提升了全局点云配准的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Global point-cloud registration remains challenging when limited overlap, repetitive geometry, and sensor noise produce correspondence sets dominated by outliers. Planar regions are particularly difficult for conventional point descriptors and are therefore often suppressed or discarded before matching. We present PARTE (Plane-Assisted Robust Transformation Estimation), a global registration method that instead treats planar structure as complementary registration evidence. PARTE extracts planar patches and represents them using our novel Plane Context Histogram (PCH), a descriptor that encodes the geometry surrounding each patch, while a two-level matching procedure identifies reliable plane correspondences. Candidate point and plane correspondences are combined in a confidence-weighted compatibility graph for joint outlier rejection, followed by rigid transformation estimation. When no usable plane correspondences are available, PARTE naturally reduces to point-only registration. We evaluate PARTE on 8,097 registration pairs across six indoor and outdoor benchmarks spanning dense RGB-D and sparse LiDAR measurements. Evaluations show PARTE achieves the highest overall success rate against 13 standard and state-of-the-art methods while maintaining low runtime. An open-source C++ implementation with Python bindings is provided at this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [VLAQuantBench: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Post-Training Quantization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25376)
*Jiuyi Xu,Qing Jin,Meida Chen,Song Wang,Yang Sui,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VLAQuantBench，对 VLA 模型后训练量化中「层范围—数值格式—校准方法」的交互进行受控评估（409 次运行、94,574 个仿真回合），发现精度分配强烈依赖具体量化配方而非通用层敏感度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型内存占用大，后训练量化是主流压缩手段，但现有工作缺乏对层范围、数值格式与校准三者交互的系统性受控分析。

Method: 在 LIBERO 上评测 4 个模型（X-VLA 另加三个仿真基准族）；对 π0.5 动作头子集做层数扩展实验（126→167 层）、固定观测重放验证、两回合校准、平滑加裁剪配方对比，以及 OpenVLA-OFT 中保护 28,672 参数输出投影的对比实验。

Result: 未校准 W4A4 RTN 下，π0.5 动作头子集由 126 层扩展至 167 层，成功率从 7.0% 升至 70.5%；两回合校准消除严重联合失效，但同一平滑裁剪配方降低 π0 成功率且无法端到端恢复 OpenVLA-OFT；保护单个输出投影即恢复近基线成功率，其余 441 层可保持 W3 或八位激活。

Conclusion: 量化效果是配方依赖的交互结果，应给出具体精度分配方案，而非依赖通用层敏感度规则；附真实内核与真机测量，代码与回合记录公开。

Abstract: Post-training quantization reduces the memory requirements of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but precision selection must account for the interaction between layer scope, numerical format, and calibration. We introduce \textbf{VLAQuantBench}, a controlled evaluation with 409 runs and 94,574 simulation episodes: four models on LIBERO, with X-VLA additionally evaluated on three simulation benchmark families. Under uncalibrated W4A4 round-to-nearest quantization, expanding a $\pi_{0.5}$ action-head subset from 126 to 167 layers raises success from 7.0\% to 70.5\%. Fixed-observation replay confirms a corresponding numerical recovery. Two-episode calibration removes the severe joint failures in the tested subsets, whereas the same smoothing-and-clipping recipe lowers $\pi_0$ success and does not recover OpenVLA-OFT end-to-end. For OpenVLA-OFT, protecting one 28,672-parameter output projection instead restores near-baseline success: the remaining 441 eligible linear layers retain W3 on LIBERO-Long or eight-bit activations across all four suites. Task-clustered intervals support the large failure and recovery contrasts. These results establish recipe-dependent interactions and identify concrete precision assignments, rather than universal layer-sensitivity rules. Real-kernel and physical-robot measurements complement the accuracy analysis. Code, configurations, and episode records are publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [121] [Norm2Tex: Augmenting Visuo-Tactile Simulations with Texture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25398)
*Seongjin Bien,Débora Oliveira Makowski,Roberto Calandra,Florian Walter,Wolfram Burgard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Norm2Tex 是一种即插即用方法，通过在触觉仿真渲染管线前修改目标物体的深度图，将法线贴图中的高频表面纹理细节注入视觉触觉传感器仿真，缓解仿真与真实触觉数据之间的域偏移，并在真实世界的材质分类与强化学习任务中验证了 sim-to-real 迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模数据集对通用机器人控制策略至关重要，但真实触觉数据采集成本高、耗时长，因此需要触觉仿真。现有触觉仿真器只捕获整体接触几何，忽略纹理等细节，导致仿真与真实触觉数据之间存在显著域偏移，限制了仿真数据的可用性。

Method: 提出 Norm2Tex，一种即插式增强方法：在触觉仿真器渲染管线之前修改目标物体的深度图，将法线贴图（normal map texture）中的高频表面细节融合进去，从而在不改动仿真器内部结构的前提下增强触觉仿真的纹理保真度，可无缝集成到不同视觉触觉仿真器中。

Result: 通过材质分类任务与一个强化学习任务评估 sim-to-real 迁移，结果显示 Norm2Tex 能跨域保留与材质相关的触觉信息，提升纹理识别性能，并在真实世界中产生依赖材质的控制行为。

Conclusion: Norm2Tex 以低成本、可插拔的方式弥补了触觉仿真中高频纹理细节的缺失，缩小了 sim-to-real 域差距，为触觉数据增强与触觉驱动的机器人学习提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets are essential for training generalist robot control policies. Collecting real-world tactile data is costly and time-consuming, motivating the use of tactile simulations. However, current tactile simulators capture only overall contact geometry and miss fine details like texture. This results in a significant domain shift between simulated and real tactile data. To address this gap, we introduce Norm2Tex, a plug-in method that augments simulations of vision-based tactile sensors with high-frequency surface details from normal map textures. By modifying the target object's depth map before a tactile simulator's rendering pipeline, Norm2Tex seamlessly integrates into different tactile simulators. We also evaluate sim-to-real transfer using material classification and a reinforcement learning task. Our results show that Norm2Tex preserves material-dependent tactile information across domains, improving texture recognition and producing material-dependent control behavior in the real world.

</details>


### [122] [Effects of Assistance Delay on Joint Mechanics and Energetics in Biological Torque Control of a Hip Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25417)
*Jimin An,Ryan Lee,Jingshu Peng,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究考察生物力矩控制下的助力延迟对髋外骨骼在平地、上坡、下坡行走中的影响，发现延迟影响机械卸载但不影响代谢收益，且净代谢收益具有任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物力矩控制将估计的人体关节力矩直接映射为外骨骼助力，是任务无关的策略，但固定状态-力矩映射能否在生物力学上差异显著的任务中提供有效助力尚不明确。

Method: 8名受试者完成零力矩基线和5种主动助力条件（延迟40–320 ms），在平地（LG）、上坡（RA）、下坡（RD）行走中测量净代谢率、生物髋关节正功与下肢总正功。

Result: 跨任务与延迟，助力使净代谢率降低5.24%、生物髋关节正功降低5.86%、下肢总正功降低1.68%（均p<0.05）。延迟显著影响关节功（p<0.001）但不影响净代谢率；机械卸载随延迟增加而下降，代谢收益相对稳定。相对基线，LG净代谢降9.75%、RA降7.20%，而RD升高1.23%；未检测到任务依赖的延迟响应差异。

Conclusion: 生物力矩映射应依据目标结果与被助力关节的机械角色来评估；以正功为主的助力在负功主导的下坡行走中可能无法泛化，需加以修改。

Abstract: Biological torque control directly maps an estimated human joint moment to exoskeleton assistance, providing a task-agnostic strategy for supporting diverse locomotor activities. However, it remains unclear whether a fixed state-to-torque mapping provides effective assistance across biomechanically distinct tasks. We examined how assistance delay affected hip exoskeleton performance during level-ground (LG), ramp-ascent (RA), and ramp-descent (RD) walking. Eight participants completed a zero-torque baseline condition and five active assistance conditions with delays ranging from 40 to 320 ms. Across tasks and active delays, assistance reduced net metabolic rate by 5.24%, positive biological hip joint work by 5.86%, and total lower-limb positive joint work by 1.68% (all p < 0.05). Assistance delay affected both joint-work outcomes (both p < 0.001) but not net metabolic rate. Mechanical unloading generally decreased with increasing delay, whereas metabolic benefits remained comparatively stable. Relative to the zero-torque condition, net metabolic rate decreased by 9.75% during LG and 7.20% during RA but increased by 1.23% during RD. We did not detect task-dependent differences in the delay response. Our findings indicate that biological torque mappings should be evaluated based on the target outcome and mechanical role of the assisted joint, and that predominantly positive-power assistance may not generalize to negative-work-dominant locomotion without modification.

</details>


### [123] [REDACT: Robust Perceptive Locomotion under Unseen Visual Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25450)
*Natapat Kirdwichai,Tobias Driskell-Poole,Andrei Sontea,Jadu Dash,Muhammad Burhan Hafez,Danesh Tarapore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 REDACT 师生框架：改进视觉编码器 + 持久特征掩码 + 共识门控，仅用干净仿真深度做近似保角校准，在未建模的深度损坏下保留有效视觉信息，实现优于现有跑酷基线的通过成功率与零样本真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度条件化运动策略在观测偏离训练分布时会输出不可预测动作，遮挡、无效回波、传感器噪声与视觉干扰都会造成这种偏移。现有合成传感器增强只针对预设的退化类型，无法覆盖训练中未包含的损坏族，因此需要一种在训练时覆盖率缺失情况下仍能保持行为可靠的方法。

Method: 采用 teacher-student 框架：改进视觉编码器架构增强特征提取；持久特征掩码（persistent feature masking）在时间上稳定地遮挡不可靠特征；共识门控（consensus-gating）利用仅基于干净观测的近似保角校准（approximate conformal calibration）判定特征可用性，无需事先知道损坏类型；结合深度增强的消融分析验证各模块作用。

Result: 在干净仿真深度上训练后，REDACT 在未见损坏下保留有用视觉信息，跑酷通过成功率高于现有基线；深度增强跨损坏族评估显示其在增强覆盖缺失处提升鲁棒性；真机试验实现向结构化与林地环境的零样本迁移，且场景内容陌生。

Conclusion: REDACT 说明仅凭干净观测的校准门控即可在未建模损坏下维持深度条件运动策略的可用性，为 sim-to-real 鲁棒感知控制提供了不依赖损坏先验的解决思路，并验证了野外场景的零样本迁移可行性。

Abstract: Depth-conditioned locomotion policies have demonstrated impressive agile maneuvers, but can be steered to unpredictable actions when observations are outside their training distribution. Occlusion, invalid returns, sensor noise, and visual distractors can shift deployment observations away from nominal simulated depth. While synthetic sensor augmentation targets specified degradations, it does not by itself define behavior under corruption families omitted from training. To address gaps in training-time coverage, we present REDACT (Retaining Evidence Despite Artifacts for Continued Traversal), a teacher-student framework combining an improved visual encoder architecture, persistent feature masking, and a novel consensus-gating algorithm to retain useful depth information under unmodeled corruption. The gate uses approximate conformal calibration on clean observations alone, requiring no prior knowledge of the corruption type. Trained on clean simulated depth, REDACT retains useful visual information under unseen corruption, supporting higher traversal success than existing parkour baselines. Evaluation of depth augmentation across corruption families further shows that REDACT improves robustness where augmentation coverage is missing. Real-world trials demonstrate zero-shot transfer to structured and forested environments with unfamiliar scene content.

</details>


### [124] [A bioinspired internal model-based online estimator for planar pursuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25470)
*Tengyue Liu,Xincheng Li,Sofia Morales Ferreira,Kevin Galloway,Udit Halder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仿生内部模型估计器，用庞特里亚金极大值原理与滚动时域优化，从部分机载传感观测和已知自运动重建相对运动状态，并用于两类追捕策略的实时机器人验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿生反馈控制（追捕、跟踪、集群运动）常以交互智能体间的相对构型表达，但机载传感器往往无法直接测量全部反馈量，需要对未观测量进行估计。经典状态估计器未充分利用已知的仿生内部模型结构与相对运动学约束。

Method: 将状态重构建模为优化问题：以相对运动学为约束，最小化内部模型输出与机载传感器测量之间的不一致；用庞特里亚金极大值原理推导必要最优性条件；采用前向-后向算法数值求解，并以滚动时域（moving horizon）形式在线实现。

Result: 数值实验中与经典状态估计器对比评估；在机器人硬件上实现了所提框架的实时运行，并演示了两种追捕策略。

Conclusion: 基于内部模型与相对运动学约束的估计框架可在部分观测下在线重建相对状态，并支持实时仿生追捕控制，表明模型驱动的估计与仿生反馈控制可有效结合。

Abstract: Bioinspired feedback controls for pursuit, tracking, and collective motion are often expressed in terms of the relative configuration between interacting agents. In practice, however, onboard sensors may not directly provide all quantities required for feedback control, necessitating estimation of unobserved quantities. This paper develops a bioinspired internal model-based estimator for reconstructing those quantities from partial sensory observations and known self-motion. State reconstruction is posed as an optimization problem that treats the relative kinematics as constraints and minimizes the disagreement between the internal model outputs and measurements from onboard sensors. Pontryagin's Maximum Principle is used to derive the necessary optimality conditions. A forward-backward algorithm is used to provide a numerical solution and a moving horizon formulation is employed for online implementation. The estimator is evaluated numerically against classical state estimators. Real-time implementation of the proposed framework on robotic hardware is demonstrated through two pursuit strategies.

</details>


### [125] [Brace Yourself: Task-Conditioned Environmental Bracing for Forceful Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25486)
*Zongyuan Zhang,Christopher Lehnert,Will N. Browne,Jonathan M. Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"支撑手策略"(SHS)，让类人机器人一手撑扶环境、另一手施力操作，用任务条件化的支撑构型引导两个同步强化学习策略，无需人体运动数据或在线全身轨迹规划，在Unitree G1上实现最高60 N接触力（无支撑时仅13.5 N）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类人机器人在施力操作时，交互力会破坏全身平衡，导致可施加的持续力很小（13.5 N），限制了实际作业能力。作者希望用最简单的方式（借助环境支撑）大幅提升类人机器人的施力能力，并避免依赖人体动捕数据与在线全身轨迹优化。

Method: SHS：优化一个任务条件化的支撑构型（支撑手落点/姿态），以此作为条件引导两个同步训练的强化学习策略——一臂负责撑扶支撑，另一臂负责施力操作；不依赖人体运动数据，也不做在线全身轨迹规划。在Unitree G1真机上验证，并与任务无关的支撑构型对比。

Result: 在Unitree G1上实现最高60 N的可用接触力，远高于无环境支撑时13.5 N的最大持续力；相比任务无关支撑构型显著改善力跟踪精度；同一策略无需再训练即可泛化到不同任务区域。

Conclusion: SHS以简单机制显著扩展了类人机器人的施力操作能力，任务条件化支撑构型是关键，且策略具备跨任务区域泛化性。

Abstract: Forceful manipulation is challenging for humanoid robots because interaction forces can disturb whole-body balance. We introduce the Supporting Hand Strategy (SHS), which enables a humanoid to brace against the environment with one hand while performing forceful manipulation with the other. SHS optimises a task-conditioned support configuration that guides two synchronous reinforcement-learning policies, without human motion data or online whole-body trajectory planning. On a Unitree G1, SHS achieved usable contact forces up to 60 N, compared with a maximum sustained force of 13.5 N without environmental bracing, while substantially improving force tracking over a task-independent support configuration. The same policies generalised to different task regions without retraining. SHS therefore provides a simple mechanism for substantially extending humanoid forceful-manipulation capability.

</details>


### [126] [RoboMP-DINOv2: Prompts, Not Filters for Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25506)
*Han Qi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboMP-DINOv2，把分割掩码当作空间提示而非硬过滤，从全场景提取 DINOv2 密集特征并注入区域嵌入，配合掩码区颜色随机化（MCR）提升外观鲁棒性，在七种仿真操作设置中显著优于 Diffusion Policy 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略需在视觉漂移下泛化，同时保留与动作相关的场景上下文；通用视觉编码器不适配视觉运动控制，而物体中心方法用分割掩码作硬过滤会丢弃有用上下文。

Method: 基于 DINOv2 的全场景视觉编码器：从完整观测提取密集特征，在掩码位置注入可学习的区域特定嵌入，让被提示与未被提示的 token 联合上下文建模用于动作预测；并引入掩码区颜色随机化（MCR）增强外观鲁棒性。

Result: 七个仿真操作设置中，空间位移下成功率 60.7%、场景杂乱下 59.7%，对比 DINOv2 Diffusion Policy 的 50.7% 与 41.0%；未见物体颜色下 RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR 达 72.5%，最强颜色随机化基线为 35.1%。

Conclusion: 掩码作为空间提示可在保留行为相关场景信息的同时提升视觉鲁棒性，但证据仅限仿真，缺少真机验证。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies must generalize across visual shifts while preserving scene context relevant to action. General-purpose vision encoders are not tailored to visuomotor control, while object-centric approaches often use segmentation masks as hard filters that discard potentially useful context. We propose RoboMP-DINOv2 (Robotics Mask-Prompted DINOv2), a full-scene vision encoder that treats masks as spatial prompts rather than visibility filters. It extracts dense DINOv2 features from the full observation, injects learned region-specific embeddings at masked locations, and jointly contextualizes prompted and unprompted tokens for action prediction. We further introduce masked-region color randomization (MCR) to improve appearance robustness, yielding RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR. Across seven simulated manipulation settings, RoboMP-DINOv2 achieves 60.7% success under spatial shifts and 59.7% under scene clutter, compared with 50.7% and 41.0% for a DINOv2-based Diffusion Policy. Under unseen object colors, RoboMP-DINOv2-MCR achieves 72.5% success versus 35.1% for the strongest color-randomized baseline. Additional experiments and representation analyses show improved robustness while preserving behaviorally relevant scene information. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [127] [A Deployment Study of Identity-Gated Drone Gesture Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25511)
*Diyari Mohammed Salih,Ilyes Chaabeni,Naima Ait Oufroukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IGate：一种"身份门控"手势控制栈，仅当已注册操作员通过人脸验证时才放行手势指令，在DJI Tello EDU上完成离线与飞行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉手势控制会响应视场内任意手部，在共享室内空间中存在安全隐患，因此需要将指令授权与操作员身份绑定。

Method: 20帧少样本人脸注册（无用户特定预训练），当前人脸嵌入与注册模板做余弦相似度验证，人脸跟踪用比例校正；手势识别用自建数据集训练的RBF-SVM分类手部关键点；分层有限状态机管理模式选择、默认与回退行为。

Result: 270次试验（149次飞行）：离线人脸验证EER 0.32%，飞行中升至19.3%；悬停锁定条件下RBF-SVM手势分类准确率0.850，优于几何规则0.651，差距的82%来自深度通道。

Conclusion: 身份门控可离线高精度实现，但飞行中验证显著退化；学习式手势分类明显优于几何规则，深度信息是主要增益来源。代码与日志将开源。

Abstract: Vision-based gesture control accepts commands from any hand in the camera field of view, which is unsafe in shared indoor spaces. This paper presents IGate, an identity-gated control stack that includes gesture control and face tracking, in which commands are admitted only when an enrolled operator is verified. The system performs few-shot user enrolment from 20 initial face frames, without prior user-specific training: verification compares an embedding of the current face crop against the enrolled template by cosine similarity, while face tracking uses proportional correction. Gesture control is achieved by classifying extracted hand landmarks using an RBF-SVM trained on a custom dataset. Additionally, a hierarchical finite-state machine handles mode selection, default, and fallback behaviours. The approach is tested on a DJI Tello EDU, each component evaluated offline and in-flight across 270 trials (149 flown). Face verification yields a 0.32% offline equal error rate versus 19.3% in-flight. Under hover-locked conditions, the RBF-SVM gesture classifier outperforms the geometric rule (0.850 vs. 0.651 accuracy), with 82% of this gap stemming from the depth channel. All logs and reproduction scripts will be released.

</details>


### [128] [Digital Twin-Driven VR Teleoperation with Multi-View Spatial Perception for Surgical Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25527)
*Chang Liu,Chenhao Yu,Honghao Zhao,Hao Ding,Haochen Wei,Adnan Munawar,Mathias Unberath,Peter Kazanzides*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出数字孪生驱动的VR遥操作平台，通过无标记感知构建手术场景数字孪生并在HMD上多视角渲染，15人用户研究显示相较HoloLens 2基线路径长度降低86%、抖动降低95%，深度感知达传统控制台水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMIS现有平台（固定控制台或视频透视HMD）受限于单一内窥镜视角，深度感知差，手部姿态必须严格对齐器械。作者希望用数字孪生+VR打破这一限制。

Method: 从手术环境无标记感知生成数字孪生，流式传输到头显进行多视角渲染，提供自然运动视差线索，解耦手部姿态与器械对齐；使用VR手柄（而非手势追踪）扩大遥操作工作空间并提升控制稳定性；在dVRK上开展15人用户研究，与HoloLens 2混合现实基线及传统控制台对比。

Result: 相比最先进的HoloLens 2 MR基线，路径长度减少86%，抖动减少95%；在所有条件下深度感知置信度与传统控制台相当或更优。

Conclusion: 数字孪生驱动的VR遥操作可显著优于视频透视MR方案，兼顾操作精度与深度感知，为可携带手术控制台提供可行路径；但属医疗机器人领域，非具身智能核心方向。

Abstract: Current robot-assisted minimally-invasive surgery (RMIS) platforms provide a fixed console for the surgeon to view stereo endoscopic images and teleoperate instruments inside the patient. Several researchers have proposed the use of a head-mounted display (HMD) as a portable console, with video pass-through rendering of the endoscope images which, like the fixed console, restricts the operator to a single endoscopic viewpoint and limits depth perception. We present a digital twin-driven virtual reality (VR) teleoperation platform, where the digital twin is created from markerless perception of the surgical environment and streamed for display on the HMD. This overcomes the limitations of video pass-through by providing multi-view rendering and natural motion-parallax cues, enabling decoupling of the user's hand posture from strict instrument alignment. The system utilizes VR hand controllers to increase the teleoperation workspace and to improve the robustness and stability of instrument control compared to the hand tracking approach adopted by most prior systems. A 15-participant user study on the da Vinci Research Kit (dVRK) shows that our VR platform significantly outperforms a state-of-the-art HoloLens 2 mixed reality baseline, reducing path length by 86% and jerk by 95%, while achieving depth perception confidence comparable to or exceeding the traditional console across all conditions.

</details>


### [129] [HABILIS Brain 0: Geometry-Change Supervision for Vision-Language-Action and Residual Flow Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25558)
*Jinu Pahk,Jesoon Kang,Taegeon Park,Jisu An,Soo Min Kimm,Jaejoon Kim,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GC-VLA通过预测多视角“未来-当前”几何变化token作为具身无关的视觉预训练接口，四阶段训练（VLM→ActionExpert对齐→联合微调→GCRF残差流）在LIBERO上达95.20%，加GCRF达99.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前帧几何监督虽有益于VLA策略，但无法显式描述操作过程中的几何变化；作者希望学习一个具身无关（embodiment-agnostic）的视觉接口，可先在机器人视频与自我中心视频上预训练，再与机器人动作对齐。

Method: 第一阶段用离线帧对（名义0.5秒预测时域，未来观测仅用于构造目标）训练GC-VLM预测多视角未来-当前几何变化token；第二阶段引入连续ActionExpert并与机器人动作对齐，同时在VLM接口处截断动作流梯度；第三阶段放行梯度，联合更新可训练VLM部件与ActionExpert；第四阶段冻结GC-VLA，施加几何条件残差流GCRF，由二元干预路由器与单一有界残差速度策略从闭环反馈中学习。推理仅用当前观测与学到的GC表示。

Result: LIBERO成功率95.20%；GC-VLA+GCRF达99.55%。推理时无需执行离线目标编码器。

Conclusion: 以几何变化预测作为跨具身预训练接口，并通过分阶段梯度解耦与闭环残差策略精修，可显著提升VLA操作成功率，且推理开销受控。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies benefit from geometric supervision, but current-frame geometry alone does not explicitly describe the changes associated with manipulation. This design is motivated by the goal of learning an embodiment-agnostic visual interface that can be pretrained across robot and egocentric video before robot-specific action alignment. We introduce Geometry-Change VLA (GC-VLA), which learns to predict multiview future-current geometry-change tokens from current observations. Offline frame pairs define a nominal 0.5-second prediction horizon; future observations are used only to construct training targets. Stage 1 trains a geometry-change vision-language model (GC-VLM). Stage 2 introduces a continuous ActionExpert and aligns it with robot actions while stopping action-flow gradients at the VLM interface. Stage 3 enables these gradients to update the trainable VLM components jointly with the ActionExpert. Stage 4 freezes GC-VLA and applies Geometry-Conditioned Residual Flow (GCRF), using a binary intervention router and a single bounded residual velocity policy learned from closed-loop feedback. GC-VLA achieves 95.20% success on LIBERO, and GC-VLA with GCRF achieves 99.55%. Inference uses current observations and the learned GC representation without executing the offline target encoders.

</details>


### [130] [IndustrialVLA-Bench: A Traceable Multi-Axis Evaluation of Open Robot Policy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25562)
*Yiqi Wang,Zhifeng Rao,Jiaqi Zhang,Xiaoyang Li,Zhangkai Wu,Yiqun Duan,Mingkai Zheng,Fei Wang,Shan You,Taotao Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 IndustrialVLA-Bench，一个统一证据感知评估框架，在相同报告规范下对比 6 个已发布的 VLA 与 WAM 系统：干净能力（LIBERO）、非语言鲁棒性（LIBERO-Plus）、指令敏感性（LIBERO-Para）与实测执行成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 与 WAM 面向同类操作任务但采用不同评估协议，导致二者在能力、鲁棒性、语言敏感性与部署成本上的取舍无法直接比较，缺少可追溯、统一口径的证据。

Method: 构建 IndustrialVLA-Bench：统一报告模式对 6 个已发布系统分维度评估（干净能力、非语言鲁棒性、指令改写敏感性、推理延迟/峰值显存/运行模式），固定 checkpoint 与推理配置，每个任务分数聚合三个不同随机种子的完整评估；并引入证据状态分级（protocol-faithful、near-reproduction、pending-verification），严格比较仅限 protocol-faithful 条目。

Result: 6 个系统干净 LIBERO 平均分仅相差 1.58 分，但鲁棒性与改写摘要分差分别达 14.62 与 31.08 分；仅保留 3 个 protocol-faithful 系统时结论一致（1.36、14.62、23.10 分），说明诊断性差异不依赖较弱证据层级。同时报告了推理延迟、峰值显存与运行模式。

Conclusion: 不对任一范式宣称普适优越性，IndustrialVLA-Bench 提供可追溯证据，用于在共享实际标准下比较已发布机器人策略；代码与评估记录已公开。

Abstract: Open robot policies increasingly follow two paradigms: vision-language-action models (VLAs) directly map observations and instructions to actions, whereas world-action models (WAMs) incorporate learned video or world dynamics into policy learning or action generation. Although both target the same manipulation tasks and represent alternative design choices, they are commonly reported under different evaluation protocols, leaving their capability, robustness, language sensitivity, and deployment-cost trade-offs unclear. We present IndustrialVLA-Bench, an evidence-aware evaluation of six released VLA and WAM systems under a unified reporting schema. It separately evaluates clean capability on LIBERO, non-language robustness on LIBERO-Plus, instruction sensitivity on LIBERO-Para, and observed execution cost. Reported task scores aggregate three complete evaluations with distinct random seeds under a fixed checkpoint and inference configuration. Across all six systems, clean LIBERO averages differ by only 1.58 points, whereas robustness and paraphrase summaries span 14.62 and 31.08 points. Restricting every comparison to the three protocol-faithful systems preserves the effect (1.36, 14.62 and 23.10 points), so the diagnostic separation reported here does not depend on the weaker evidence tiers. We additionally report observed inference latency, peak memory, runtime mode, and an evidence status for every system. Protocol-faithful, near-reproduction, and pending-verification entries remain visibly separated; only protocol-faithful entries support strict comparisons. Rather than claiming universal superiority of either paradigm, IndustrialVLA-Bench provides traceable evidence for comparing released robot policies on shared practical criteria. Code and evaluation records are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [131] [Recording Hand-Held Laparoscopic Instrument Motion in the Operating Room: Magnetometer-Free Fusion of Inertial, Range and Visual Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25577)
*Jiyul Lee,Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Jinseok Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种夹持式手术器械状态记录器：六轴IMU + ToF测距 + 无标记相机的误差状态卡尔曼滤波融合，在套管针远心运动约束下估计器械姿态与位移，用U-Net分割杆体轮廓替代磁力计提供航向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术仍以手持腹腔镜器械为主，仅保留内镜视频，表达手术技能的器械运动信息丢失；纯视频位姿误差达毫米至厘米级，而器械上IMU的磁力计受工具姿态与会话间磁场变化影响不可用，需一种不依赖磁场的度量级器械状态估计方案。

Method: 夹持式穿戴装置（不改变入体部分）：六轴IMU + 飞行时间测距仪 + 无标记相机，在套管针远心运动（RCM）约束下构建误差状态卡尔曼滤波器；航向由U-Net分割器械杆体轮廓得到，替代磁力计。

Result: 旋转角误差0.200°，而单用加速度计+磁力计为3.58°；对Franka Research 3机械臂且未做对齐，300次平移试验位移误差1.21mm RMS，180次旋转试验相对旋转误差0.34° RMS；连续轨迹实时跟踪显示，后验调参后绝对尖端误差为1.22mm（程控）与3.04mm（遥操作），全融合优于任一传感器子集。

Conclusion: 因不使用磁测量，精度不依赖无干扰磁场；该夹持装置可在常规手持腹腔镜中记录度量级尖端轨迹，并显示器械入体后被遮挡的插入深度与姿态，可支撑技能评估与机器人学习。

Abstract: Most minimally invasive procedures are still performed with hand-held laparoscopic instruments, yet only the endoscopic video is retained; the instrument motion that expresses surgical skill, and that could support skill assessment and robot learning, is lost. Pose from video alone remains millimeters to centimeters off, and an instrument-mounted inertial measurement unit (IMU) cannot rely on its magnetometer, whose field changed with tool pose and between sessions in our measurements. We present a surgical instrument-state logger that clips onto a conventional instrument without modifying the part that enters the patient and fuses a six-axis IMU and a time-of-flight (ToF) rangefinder with a markerless camera in an error-state Kalman filter under the remote center of motion (RCM) of the trocar. Heading comes from the shaft silhouette, segmented by a U-Net, in place of the magnetometer: the rotation-angle error is 0.200°, against 3.58° from the accelerometer and magnetometer alone. Against a Franka Research 3 manipulator, and without alignment to it, the displacement error over 300 translation trials was 1.21mm RMS and the relative-rotation error over 180 rotation trials 0.34° RMS. On continuous trajectories, tracked and displayed in real time, the absolute tip error was 1.22mm (programmed) and 3.04mm (teleoperated) after post-hoc tuning of three filter parameters, and the full fusion beat every sensor subset. Because the estimator uses no magnetic measurement, its accuracy does not rely on an undisturbed field. The same clip-on device could thus record metric tip trajectories during routine hand-held laparoscopy, while displaying the insertion depth and attitude that are hidden once the instrument is inside the patient.

</details>


### [132] [CableVLA: Simulation-Privileged Global-Local Representation Learning for Cable Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25606)
*Zhifei Teng,Bo Feng,Xiang Zou,Jinpeng Xiao,Min Li,Zhouping Yin,Yiqun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CableVLA是一个端到端多模态视觉-语言-动作框架，通过TopoHead将仿真特权监督蒸馏为因果视觉上下文中的拓扑表征，通过TacSense利用帧与触觉阵列双分支学习接触动力学，并用接触门控激活力-触觉残差来细化冻结策略的动作输出，在MuJoCo上把线缆布线成功率从62.6%提升到84.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线缆布线需要同时协调全局线缆拓扑与局部接触变化，纯视觉策略难以稳定感知拓扑演变与滑移等接触事件，且真实触觉与物理监督难以获得。作者希望把仿真中的特权信息（物理量、当前与未来拓扑、接触事件）转化为可部署的拓扑表征与触觉表征，从而弥补真实传感器信息不足、提升策略的鲁棒性与零样本迁移能力。

Method: 提出CableVLA端到端VLA框架：1) TopoHead将节点级物理与当前/未来线缆拓扑信息蒸馏为动作专家的因果视觉上下文；2) TacSense用帧分支与taxel分支互补，从电阻式触觉阵列学习接触动力学，监督信号除实测力图外还包含仿真推导的运动学与接触事件；3) 接触门控激活力-触觉残差，用于精修冻结的拓扑条件策略未来8步机械臂与夹爪动作。评估包含MuJoCo 345次试验、拓扑预测、57项触觉任务、策略适配、跨仿真器与真机零样本迁移对比。

Result: MuJoCo 345次评估中成功率从π0.5-V视觉基线的62.6%提升至84.9%；TacSense在参数量相近情况下于滑移转换识别上显著优于CNN-LSTM基线，且在冻结编码器探针下优势保持；拓扑预测与57项触觉评估用于衡量表征质量，策略适配研究评估下游控制性能；跨仿真器与真机对比进一步考察动力学与感知变化下的零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 仿真特权监督可被蒸馏为可部署的拓扑与触觉表征，接触门控式残差精修能在冻结策略上带来显著控制增益；该框架在接触丰富任务上优于视觉基线，并在跨仿真器与真机条件下展现出一定零样本迁移潜力。

Abstract: Cable routing requires coordinated control of global cable topology and changing local contacts. We present CableVLA, an end-to-end multimodal vision-language-action framework that converts simulation-privileged supervision into deployable cable-topology and tactile representations. TopoHead distills node-level physics and current and future cable-topology information into causal visual context for the action expert. TacSense uses complementary frame and taxel branches to learn contact dynamics from resistive arrays, with simulator-derived kinematics and contact events providing supervision beyond the measured force map. A contact gate activates force-tactile residuals that refine the next 8 arm-and-gripper actions of a frozen topology-conditioned policy. Across 345 MuJoCo evaluations, CableVLA improves success from 62.6% for the $\pi_{0.5}$-V visual baseline to 84.9%. TacSense achieves pronounced gains in slip-transition recognition over a CNN-LSTM baseline with a similar parameter count, and this advantage persists under frozen-encoder probes. Topology prediction and 57-task tactile evaluations assess representation quality, while policy adaptation studies evaluate downstream control performance. Cross-simulator and real-robot comparisons further examine zero-shot policy transfer under changes in dynamics and sensing.

</details>


### [133] [A Deployable Four-Finger Payload for Teleoperated Free-Flying Manipulation with Astrobee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25614)
*William Su,Jordan Kam,Yunosuke Nakamura,Yixiao Wang,Jianshu Zhou,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向自由飞行器舱内操作的双手遥操作管线与可展开四指末端执行器概念设计：17 DoF 夹爪挂载于线性导轨，VR 接口将人手动作映射到指对、间距映射到导轨、腕部动作映射到 Astrobee 平移；在 MuJoCo 自建零重力 ISS 仿真中完成 10 次刚体货物袋搬运试验，测量成功率、接触保持、完成时间与货物运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来 LEO 栖息地需要在有人/无人阶段完成货物搬运与维护等琐碎任务，需要可部署的舱内自由飞行器操作能力；同时缺乏针对多指末端执行器与自由飞行器协同的直观遥操作接口。

Method: 机械与接口设计 + 仿真验证。末端执行器为四指独立驱动 + 线性导轨，共 17 个操作自由度；遥操作采用 VR 设备，将操作员手部运动映射为手指对运动、手指间距离映射为导轨开合、腕部共同运动映射为 Astrobee 平移。在基于 MuJoCo 的自定义零重力 ISS 仿真器中做 10 次重复试验，任务为搬运刚性 ISS 货物转移袋。

Result: 给出初步结果，报告任务成功率、连续接触保持情况、完成时间以及货物运动指标；属概念验证级仿真结果，未有真实在轨或地面硬件演示。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一套可部署四指载荷 + VR 双手遥操作的完整方案框架，验证了在零重力仿真中执行货物搬运任务的可行性，为未来 LEO 舱内自由飞行器操作提供了设计与控制基线。

Abstract: This article presents a bimanual teleoperation pipeline and conceptual design of a deployable four-finger payload for intra-vehicular free-flyers. Future habitats in low-Earth orbit (LEO) will require systems to perform mundane tasks like cargo handling and maintenance during crewed and uncrewed periods. The gripper payload provides 17 manipulation degrees-of-freedom (DoF) through four independently actuated fingers on a linear rail system. To control it, a virtual reality (VR) device interface maps the human ground operator's hand motions to the finger pairs, their separation to the rail, and common wrist motion to Astrobee translation. We present the preliminary results of teleoperating Astrobee in a custom zero-gravity MuJoCo-based International Space Station (ISS) simulator through ten repeated trials of transporting a rigid ISS Cargo Transfer Bag (CTB). We measure task success, continuous contact retention, completion time, and cargo motion.

</details>


### [134] [Relative Contact Velocity-Controlled Hand-Object Mechanism for Dexterous Tool Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25619)
*Sunyu Wang,Jean Oh,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将手与工具建模为统一的"手-物体机构"(HOM)，用一组与手部运动学/几何无关的笛卡尔空间相对接触速度来描述其运动，并基于最小二乘与互补滤波构建轻量、可物理解释的运动规划与接触估计框架，在仿真中用遥操作驱动五种不同机械手完成"抓取-装载-挥动"的完整工具操作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多指机械手要完成完整工具操作（拾取、装载到合适位姿、再挥动使用），涉及抓取构型、工具在手内的相对位姿调整以及使用中的接触约束，现有方法通常针对特定手部结构、依赖大量任务专用数据或学习模型，缺乏跨手、跨工具、跨任务的统一且可解释的建模范式。作者受人类工具操作与机构学设计原理启发，希望用统一的机构学表示来消除对手部运动学与几何的依赖。

Method: 将手-工具系统定义为由子装配体组成的统一手-物体机构(HOM)，其组成包括手、物体与广义接触坐标系；用同一组笛卡尔空间相对接触速度表达HOM运动，从而不依赖手的运动学与几何；进一步把HOM的子装配体定义为手指间的相对接触速度约束与接触力约束。在此基础上构建轻量、物理可解释的运动规划与接触估计框架，使用最小二乘求解与互补滤波器进行状态/接触估计。评估方式为仿真中遥操作五种不同机械手，使用相同、简单的参考轨迹。

Result: 五种不同机械手均能完成完整工具操作流程（抓取、装载、挥动），并且仅从相同的简单参考轨迹出发即可产生灵巧行为；框架表现出对不同手、工具与任务的可适应性，这一适应性来源于其运动学与几何基础。

Conclusion: 统一的HOM表示可作为一种手部无关、几何无关的建模基础，使多指手在不依赖学习的情况下完成完整工具操作并具备跨手、跨工具、跨任务的泛化性；但验证目前限于仿真遥操作，尚缺少真实机器人上的自主执行与真实接触验证。

Abstract: This work investigates how to enable general multi-finger robotic hands to perform the complete tool manipulation process, which entails picking up a tool, loading it into a suitable pose, and then wielding it. Inspired by human tool manipulation and mechanical design principles, we model the hand and the tool as a unified hand-object mechanism (HOM) composed of sub-assemblies. Specifically, we define a HOM as consisting of the hand, the object, and the generalized contact frames, allowing the HOM's motions to be expressed with the same set of Cartesian-space relative contact velocities, irrespective of the hand's kinematics and geometry. Then, we define a HOM's sub-assemblies as relative contact velocity and contact force constraints between fingers. Building on these definitions, we developed a lightweight and physically interpretable motion planning and contact estimation framework using least squares and a complementary filter. We evaluated our framework in simulation by teleoperating five different robotic hands. The results show that our framework enabled all five hands to execute the complete tool manipulation process, achieving dexterous behaviors even from identical, simple reference trajectories. Furthermore, the results showcase our framework's adaptability to different hands, tools, and tasks, enabled by its kinematic and geometric foundation.

</details>


### [135] [From Instrument-Mounted Demonstrations to In-Vivo Execution: Learning Bimanual Laparoscopic Appendectomy Without Robot-Collected Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25625)
*Dongho Yee,Juahn Oh,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Kyu Eun Lee,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套端到端流水线：用安装在普通腹腔镜器械杆上的传感器记录器（IMU+ToF+霍尔传感器）复原器械位姿与钳口状态，把外科医生自用器械的动作转化为机器人示范，训练带 DINOv3 微调骨干的扩散策略，并在活体兔阑尾切除中完成部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术仍以手持腹腔镜器械为主，手术结束后医生的器械运动学信息丢失，只保留内窥镜视频，导致大量专家操作数据无法用于机器人学习。作者希望在无外部相机/跟踪器、机器人不采集示范的前提下，直接从医生自有器械获取示范并训练可部署的手术机器人策略。

Method: 1) 器械状态记录器：安装于标准腹腔镜器械杆，用惯性传感器+飞行时间传感器+霍尔传感器复原位姿与钳口状态，无需外部相机或跟踪器；2) 数据流水线：以机器人为真值测量各传感器通道延迟并做通道对齐，构造观测-动作对；3) 策略：基于对齐后的示范训练扩散策略，骨干为微调 DINOv3，设计选择通过由离体兔阑尾深度图重建的物理模拟器闭环 rollout 完成；4) 随后用 4 只活体兔的 849 段在体示范重训，并在另外 4 只活体兔上带电外科器械部署，由外科医生选择手术阶段。

Result: 在 4 只活体兔中，策略在医生给定手术阶段条件下于 3 只上完成了阑尾切除术；机器人仅作为传感器标定的时间参考和执行器，不采集任何示范。两套示范数据集均已公开。

Conclusion: 实验表明，从外科医生自有器械记录的示范足以支撑双臂手术策略的训练、选择与在体部署，为手术机器人学习提供了一条无需机器人采集示范的数据路径，并公开了示范语料以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Most minimally invasive surgery is still performed with hand-held laparoscopic instruments, and the surgeon's instrument kinematics are lost when the operation ends; only the endoscope video is kept. This paper presents an end-to-end pipeline that captures this motion in the operating room and uses it to train a surgical robot policy, validated on live animals. We introduce a surgical instrument-state logger that mounts on the shaft of a standard laparoscopic instrument and recovers its pose and jaw state from an inertial sensor, a time-of-flight sensor and a Hall sensor, with no external camera or tracker. A data pipeline measures the latency of every sensor channel against a robot ground truth and aligns the channels before forming observation-action pairs. On these demonstrations we train a diffusion policy with a fine-tuned DINOv3 backbone, selecting its design by closed-loop rollouts in a physics simulator reconstructed from depth maps of an ex-vivo rabbit appendix. The policy is then retrained on 849 in-vivo demonstrations from four live rabbits and deployed on four additional live rabbits with electrosurgery armed. With the surgeon selecting the surgical phase, the policy completed the appendectomy in three of the four animals. The results show that demonstrations recorded from a surgeon's own instruments are sufficient to train, select and deploy a bimanual surgical policy in vivo. The robot serves only as the timing reference for sensor calibration and as the executor, and collects no demonstrations. Both demonstration corpora are released to support future surgical robot learning research.

</details>


### [136] [MachEmbodied-U0: Unified Understanding and Generation Model for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25627)
*Haoran Wen,Wenfu Wang,Kunsong Shi,Jingke Wang,Wancheng Feng,Yiren Zhang,Yueran Zhao,Xuancheng Zhang,Nanfei Ye,Xingru Chen,Zhaohong Sun,Chengmin Yang,Zikang Yu,Penghao Bi,Jia Shi,Yu Liu,Kun Zhan,Yan Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ME-U0 是统一具身基础模型，用 Mixture-of-Transformers 连接理解与生成专家，通过子任务预测与可供性定位引导流匹配的视觉动力学（RGB/深度/法向/光流）与动作联合生成，并以多速率 RoPE 对齐粗视觉与细控制，在约 4200 小时数据上预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型有强语义先验但缺乏显式场景动力学建模，世界-动作模型虽耦合视觉预测与控制，却未显式暴露细粒度操作所需的任务语义与空间结构；需要同时具备任务意图理解、交互定位、场景演化建模与精确动作生成的统一模型。

Method: 提出 ME-U0：Mixture-of-Transformers 架构统一理解与生成专家；子任务预测与可供性定位作为语义/空间引导；用 flow matching 联合生成视觉动力学（未来 RGB、深度、表面法向、光流）与动作；多速率旋转位置编码（MRPE）对齐视觉动力学与细粒度控制；在约 4200 小时机器人演示与第一视角数据上预训练。

Result: 仅用各下游基准原生监督：RoboDojo 仿真平均分 17.66，LIBERO 平均成功率 99.0%，LIBERO-Plus 82.5%；并在真实机器人操作任务上验证有效；无对应下游监督时仍展现零样本子任务预测、可供性定位与视觉动力学能力。

Conclusion: ME-U0 在仿真与真实世界均兼具有竞争力的下游控制性能与可迁移的任务定位、视觉动力学能力，验证了理解-生成统一建模对具身操作的价值。

Abstract: General-purpose robot control requires models to understand task intent, identify where to interact, capture how the scene evolves, and generate precise actions. Vision-language-action models provide strong semantic priors but typically do not explicitly model scene dynamics, while world-action models couple visual prediction with control without necessarily exposing the task-relevant semantic and spatial structure needed for fine-grained manipulation. We present MachEmbodied-U0 (ME-U0), a unified embodied foundation model connecting understanding and generation experts through a Mixture-of-Transformers architecture. Subtask prediction and affordance grounding guide joint visual-dynamics and action generation via flow matching. Visual dynamics encompass future RGB, depth, surface normals, and optical flow, providing complementary supervision for appearance, geometry, and motion. Multi-rate Rotary Position Encoding (MRPE) aligns visual dynamics with fine-grained control. We pretrain ME-U0 on approximately 4,200 hours of curated demonstrations from robotic datasets and egocentric datasets. Using only the supervision natively available in each downstream benchmark, ME-U0 achieves an average score of 17.66 on the RoboDojo simulation benchmark and average success rates of 99.0\% and 82.5\% on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, respectively. We additionally validate ME-U0 on real-world robotic manipulation tasks, demonstrating its effectiveness beyond simulation. Without corresponding downstream supervision, ME-U0 further demonstrates zero-shot subtask prediction, affordance grounding, and visual dynamics on simulated and real-world observations. Overall, ME-U0 combines competitive downstream control performance with transferable task-grounding and visual-dynamics capabilities across simulation and the real world.

</details>


### [137] [PAKT: Physically-Aligned Kinesthetic Teaching for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25630)
*Lars Johannsmeier,Yashraj Narang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PAKT框架，将工业界常用的动觉示教（kinesthetic teaching）引入离线策略RL，通过导纳控制采集人类力指令、参考生成器施加与策略执行一致的关节/速度/加速度限幅，保证轨迹可复现；并配合"参考生成器+阻抗控制"的高频控制栈，将低频RL动作映射为高频力矩指令。在四个插装与工业装配基准（含数据中心计算托盘）上，相较HIL-SERL基线循环时间降低23%-48%，累积干预次数降低62%-86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界RL在接触密集的工业操作中仍难以满足微米级精度、>99%成功率和人类级节拍要求。利用示范与干预的off-policy方法虽有潜力，但缺少既能直观采集人类指导、又满足物理系统与策略约束的接口。遥操作不贴合工业习惯，而动觉示教的痛点是操作者给出的轨迹（速度、加速度、jerk）往往超出机器人或策略的物理可复现范围。

Method: PAKT以动觉示教为交互方式：操作者通过导纳控制施加人力，被映射为机器人运动；下游参考生成器施加与策略执行阶段相同的运动学限制，使采集轨迹天然落在可行域内。执行层为高性能控制栈，由参考生成器与阻抗控制器组成，把低频RL动作升频为高频力矩指令，在保持阻抗控制器跟踪性能的同时改善接触处理并平滑策略动作。

Result: 在四个插装与工业装配基准上（包括数据中心计算托盘任务），端到端系统相对HIL-SERL基线将循环时间缩短23%-48%，累积干预次数减少62%-86%。

Conclusion: 将工业成熟的动觉示教与RL训练闭环结合，并通过参考生成器在采集端强制策略一致的运动学约束，可显著提升接触密集工业装配的学习效率与部署节拍；高质量的高频控制层是支撑此类示教接口的必要配套。

Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) systems still struggle with the demands of contact-rich industrial manipulation, including micrometer-level precision, success rates above 99%, and human-level cycle times. Although off-policy algorithms can improve performance by leveraging demonstrations and interventions, a key bottleneck is the lack of an intuitive interface for collecting such guidance while complying with constraints of the physical system and the policy. We propose PAKT, a framework for kinesthetic teaching in RL. As opposed to teleoperation approaches, PAKT relies on kinesthetic guidance, which is widely used in industry. However, a critical weakness of kinesthetic guidance is the possibility for the operator to move the robot along trajectories (e.g., velocities, accelerations, jerk) that the robot and/or policy cannot physically reproduce. Using PAKT, operators guide the robot through admittance control, which maps human-applied forces to motion. The downstream reference generator applies the same kinematic limits used during policy execution, keeping the collected trajectories within these limits. To support this teaching interface with an appropriate execution layer, PAKT adds a high-performance control stack that maps low-frequency RL actions to high-frequency torque commands. It consists of a reference generator and subsequent impedance controller, where the reference generator preserves the tracking performance of the impedance controller while improving contact handling and producing smoother policy actions. Across the reported runs on four insertion and industrial assembly benchmarks, including a data center compute tray, the end-to-end system reduces cycle time by 23%-48% and cumulative intervention count by 62%-86% relative to the HIL-SERL baseline. Project website: this https URL }{ this https URL

</details>


### [138] [DynaForge: Planning-Guided Residual Learning for Dynamic Manipulation Demonstration Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25631)
