*Yiyang Jin,Yu Zheng,Xiao He,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaForge 通过"规划引导 + 残差策略 + 隐式课程"生成高质量动态操作演示，在仿真与真机上显著提升演示生成成功率及下游 DP3 策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态操作演示生成方法稀缺：静态任务方法难以迁移；纯规划法在接触附近易失败；DOMINO 式回放会简化动态交互，限制策略学习所需的经验多样性。

Method: 提出 DynaForge 规划引导框架：低频全局规划 + 高频以物体为中心的逆运动学按任务阶段组合，并用残差策略在动态交互阶段修正动作；隐式课程按匹配条件分组 rollout，挑选"混合成败"组，将残差强化学习聚焦于能力前沿（在 Can 与 Bottle 上比 vanilla GRPO 少用 0.73x 优化步数）。

Result: 九个仿真任务上演示生成平均成功率由规划先验 41.30% 提升至 78.37%；每任务 800 条演示下，DP3 策略基于 DynaForge 数据平均成功率 49.11%，DOMINO 数据仅 7.07%；三个真实动态任务上分别达 30–60%，DOMINO 为 0–10%。

Conclusion: DynaForge 能生成高质量动态操作演示，并具备 sim-to-real 迁移能力，是动态操作演示增强的有效方案。

Abstract: Dynamic object manipulation is essential for robots operating in real-world environments, yet methods for generating high-quality demonstrations remain limited. Methods designed for static tasks do not readily transfer to dynamic settings. Among dynamic demonstration generators, planning-based methods can fail near contact, while DOMINO-style replay simplifies dynamic interactions and may limit the experience available for policy learning. We present DynaForge, a planning-guided framework that learns residual corrections for dynamic manipulation demonstration generation. DynaForge combines low-frequency global planning with high-frequency object-centric inverse kinematics across task phases, and applies a residual policy to correct actions during dynamic interaction. An implicit curriculum groups rollouts under matched conditions and selects mixed-success groups, focusing residual reinforcement learning on the evolving competence frontier. On Can and Bottle, it uses 0.73x as many optimizer steps as vanilla GRPO at the same nominal environment-step budget, with higher observed final success rates. Across nine simulation tasks, DynaForge increases mean demonstration-generation success from 41.30% of the planning prior to 78.37%. With 800 demonstrations per task, DP3 policies trained on DynaForge data achieve 49.11% mean success, compared with 7.07% for DOMINO data. On three real-world dynamic tasks, DynaForge-trained policies achieve 30-60% success, compared with 0-10% for DOMINO-trained policies, showing the ability of DynaForge for sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [139] [RoboFollow: Unveiling the Instruction Following Mirage in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25636)
*Chang Guo,Yukun Xie,Bohan Tan,Zheng Chang,Zhaokai Yin,Qianli Ma,Yingqiao Wang,Chao Liang,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboFollow 诊断基准，指出 VLA/WAM 策略的高成功率源于低场景熵导致的“语言冗余假象”，通过多分支场景与四级扰动协议暴露指令跟随瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体报告的成功率虚高：作者将其归因于结构性属性“低场景熵”——当视觉场景只允许唯一有效任务时，语言变得冗余，策略几乎不使用语言也能获得高分。因此需要一种能强制依赖语言、并区分“理解”与“执行”的诊断性评测。

Method: 设计 RoboFollow 基准，遵循三原则：(1) 高场景熵：每个训练场景支持多个运动学上不同的任务分支，使纯视觉不足、必须依赖语言；(2) 分层诊断协议：L0–L3 四级递进扰动视觉布局与语义，检验等价指令行为一致、不同指令行为可区分（覆盖空间关系、属性、轨迹约束、逻辑）；(3) 混淆控制诊断：简化交互物体、动作限制在训练动作库内，并分阶段报告 Intent 与 Execution 分数，将语言理解与运动执行解耦。评测九种 VLA 与 WAM 策略，并测试更强 VLM 骨干、QA 协同训练、LangForce、Classifier-Free Guidance 等缓解手段。

Result: 九种 VLA/WAM 策略中，L0 上的强表现（即便达成）在微调设定下并不能可靠迁移到 L1–L3；所测试的代表性缓解手段（更强 VLM 骨干、QA 协同训练、LangForce、Classifier-Free Guidance）均无法弥合该差距。

Conclusion: RoboFollow 表明真实的指令跟随能力是当前 VLA/WAM 系统被忽视的关键瓶颈，高成功率数字具有误导性；语言条件泛化而非视觉-运动拟合才是核心短板。

Abstract: Modern embodied agents achieve impressive success rates, yet their actual instruction-following ability is far weaker than these numbers suggest. We trace this illusion to a structural property we term low scene entropy: when a visual scene admits only one valid task, language becomes redundant and a policy can score highly while barely using it. We introduce RoboFollow, a diagnostic benchmark with three principles: (1) High Scene Entropy: each training scene supports multiple kinematically distinct task branches, making vision alone insufficient and forcing reliance on language. (2) Hierarchical Diagnostic Protocol: a four-level protocol (L0--L3) progressively perturbs visual layout and semantics, probing whether equivalent instructions yield consistent behavior and distinct ones yield discriminable behavior across spatial relations, attributes, trajectory constraints, and logic. (3) Confound-Controlled Diagnosis: we simplify interaction objects, restrict actions to the trained repertoire and report stage-wise Intent and Execution scores, isolating comprehension from motor execution. Evaluation of nine VLA and WAM policies shows that strong L0 performance, where attained, does not reliably transfer to L1--L3 under our fine-tuning setup. Representative mitigations, including stronger VLM backbones, QA co-training, LangForce, and Classifier-Free Guidance, all fail to close this gap. RoboFollow exposes genuine instruction following as a critical, overlooked bottleneck. Code and dataset are available at this https URL and this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [A Reconfigurable Bidirectional Cable-Driven Hip Exoskeleton with Swappable Bench/Backpack Dual-configuration Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25639)
*YuanLong Ji,Shuhan Xiang,Qihan Ye,Xingbang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可重构双向线驱髋关节外骨骼平台，可在台式与背包式驱动间快速切换并共用同一无缆可穿戴髋接口，验证了运动跟踪、切换效率并采集了多模态步态数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室康复评估需要充足驱动与算力资源，而移动助力需要无缆便携性，将两种能力集成于同一可复用平台是核心设计难题。

Method: 模块化重构驱动配置、末端传感路径与底层控制接口；双向线驱末端执行器重0.405 kg（不含线缆与驱动单元），集成编码器与力矩传感器；台式采用基于导纳的运动跟踪，背包式采用开环力矩跟踪；三名健康受试者佩戴myoMOTION进行髋部运动感知评估，并跨30次试验验证台式-背包双向切换。

Result: 验证了台式导纳运动跟踪与背包式开环力矩跟踪；切换时间30.1±16.3 s（30次试验）；构建小规模多模态可穿戴外骨骼步态数据集，含8分钟台式跑步机记录与11分钟背包式户外步行记录。

Conclusion: 通过统一可穿戴结构、驱动接口与传感路径，该平台支持同一髋外骨骼在台式与背包式配置下的验证，为跨场景迭代开发与应用提供可复用硬件。

Abstract: Hip exoskeletons provide an important hardware basis for lower-limb rehabilitation and locomotor assistance. Laboratory rehabilitation assessment and system development require substantial actuation and computing resources, whereas mobile assistance requires untethered portability. Integrating both capabilities within one reusable platform remains a central design challenge. This paper presents a reconfigurable bidirectional cable-driven hip exoskeleton platform that rapidly switches between bench-mounted and backpack-mounted actuation while sharing one cable-free wearable hip interface. The platform modularly adapts the actuation configuration, end-effector sensing path, and low-level control interface. Each cable-driven end-effector weighs 0.405 kg, excluding the cable and actuation unit, and integrates an encoder and a torque sensor; experiments validated bench-mounted admittance-based motion tracking capability and backpack-mounted open-loop torque tracking. Human-worn experiments with three healthy participants used myoMOTION to evaluate the platform's wearable-side hip-motion sensing capability, verified bench-to-backpack and backpack-to-bench motion-ready switching across 30 trials in $30.1\pm16.3$ s, and formed a small-scale multimodal wearable-exoskeleton gait dataset for sensing validation and data-driven algorithm development, comprising 8 min bench-mounted treadmill records and 11 min backpack-mounted outdoor walking records. These results show that, by unifying the wearable structure, actuation interface, and sensing path, the proposed platform enables validation of the same hip exoskeleton in both bench-mounted and backpack-mounted configurations, providing reusable hardware for iterative development and applications across scenarios.

</details>


### [141] [Contact-Stable Deformable Tissue Simulation Using Implicit Integration and Live-Pose Grasp Constraints for Laparoscopic Surgery Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25642)
*Juahn Oh,Dongho Yee,Jinseok Lee,Jiyul Lee,Yechan Seo,Seong Jeong,Minsung Kim,Seonho Shim,Younghoon Noh,Hyuk Choi,Youngbin Kong,Hyoun-Joong Kon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 从固定视角RGB-D手术视野重建可变形组织体（十三器官、146,061四面体），去器械、封闭成水密体并四面体化；在两台Franka FR3双臂6 mm穿刺器闭环细胞内实现稳定抓取提升，核心为隐式积分、仿真/碰撞网格分离、数值守卫与活体姿态抓取约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人闭环评估缺乏既可变性、可抓取提升、又复现术区解剖的组织模型；现有体模与仿真器难以在真实记录的解剖几何上做接触与安全策略筛选。

Method: 基于固定视角RGB-D记录重建组织：器械剔除与合成（compositing）、水密封闭、四面体化，管线对两个物种三个样本保持不变；单样本放入双臂细胞，两台Franka FR3通过6 mm trocars操作电动器械；隐式积分、仿真网格与碰撞网格解耦、数值守卫、以及按活体组织姿态采集的抓取约束；45次重复抓取-提升对比摩擦抓取、约束抓取与静息姿态约束。

Result: 无反转单元（13器官，146,061四面体）；45次抓取-提升中摩擦抓取15/15失败，约束抓取各13/15成功；位移组织上静息姿态约束产生最大17.8 mm单步跳变，活体姿态捕获消除该跳变；相对录像前表面深度误差1.33–1.41 mm，器官轮廓IoU 0.80；5次视频复现抓取-提升的地标位移RMSE 11.8 mm（静态预测14.2 mm）。

Conclusion: 该环境定位于闭环可行性、安全性、接触与策略筛选，不宣称生物保真度；提供了从术区记录到可交互组织体与双臂接触稳定的完整工程路径。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of surgical robots requires tissue that deforms, can be grasped and lifted, and reproduces the anatomy in which the robot will operate. We present a simulator in which this tissue is reconstructed from a fixed-view RGB-D recording of the surgical field, composited to remove the instruments, closed into watertight volumes and tetrahedralised; the pipeline was applied unchanged to three specimens of two species (thirteen organs, 146,061 tetrahedra, no inverted elements). For one specimen, the organs are placed in a bimanual cell in which two Franka FR3 arms operate motorised instruments through 6 mm trocars. The core contribution is the numerical and contact design that keeps this cell stable: implicit integration, simulation meshes separate from collision meshes, numerical guards, and a grasp constraint captured at the live tissue pose. In 45 repeated grasp-lifts, a friction grasp held the tissue in 0 of 15 trials and each constraint grasp in 13 of 15; on displaced tissue, a rest-pose constraint produced one-step snaps of up to 17.8 mm, which live-pose capture eliminates. Against the recording, front-surface depth error is 1.33 to 1.41 mm, organ silhouette IoU is 0.80, and in five grasp-lifts reproduced from video the landmark displacement RMSE is 11.8 mm against 14.2 mm for a static prediction. Biofidelity is not claimed; the environment is intended for closed-loop feasibility, safety, contact and policy screening.

</details>


### [142] [Skill Sequence Planning for Collaborative Multi-Robot Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25649)
*Xi Wang,Bo Fu,Carol C. Menassa,Vineet R. Kamat,Min Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向多机器人协同施工的技能序列规划方法：将建筑数字模型转化为施工关系图，据此动态生成技能符号序列与无碰撞几何运动规划，并经交互式数字孪生由人类审批后执行；通过施工装配案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑机器人潜力大但行业采纳受限，主因是每类任务变体都需重新编程，适配成本高。作者希望用可复用预编程技能降低编程负担，支持多异构机器人灵活部署。

Method: 核心是分层规划：1) 中央控制器把建筑数字表示转成"施工关系图"，编码施工实体、状态及父子关系；2) 基于该图选择下一施工目标；3) 为有能力胜任的机器人成员生成符号化技能序列（抓取、钻孔、紧固等），符号规划问题随施工状态变化动态重建；4) 生成无碰撞几何运动规划以执行技能；5) 交互式数字孪生向人类协作者展示技能序列与机器人状态，审批后执行。

Result: 通过一个施工装配案例研究进行评估，展示了多异构机器人团队按复用技能协同完成装配任务的可行性，并减少了针对每类任务变体单独编程的需求。

Conclusion: 以关系图驱动的符号+几何分层规划配合人类审批，可降低机器人编程与适配成本，支持协作机器人团队在建筑场景中更灵活地部署。

Abstract: Robots have significant potential to automate construction processes. However, their industry adoption remains limited, partly because of the programming effort required to adapt robots to diverse tasks. This paper presents a skill sequence planning method that enables a heterogeneous team of multi-functional robots to collaboratively perform construction assembly work using reusable, preprogrammed skills such as grasping, drilling, and fastening. A central controller transforms the digital representation of the building into a construction relationship graph that represents construction entities, their states, and their parent-child relationships. Based on this representation, the system selects the next construction target, generates a symbolic sequence of skills for capable members of the robot team, and produces collision-free geometric motion plans for skill execution. The symbolic planning problem is dynamically regenerated as the construction state changes. An interactive digital twin presents the planned skill sequence and robot states to human co-workers for review and approval before execution. The method is evaluated through a construction assembly case study. By reducing the need to program robots separately for each task variation, the proposed approach supports more flexible deployment of collaborative robot teams in construction.

</details>


### [143] [PhyVisGen: Physically and Visually High-Fidelity Robotic Manipulation Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25653)
*Yu Zheng,Qiyu Feng,Yixin Wu,Baoquan Yang,Yixuan Zhou,Bingyang Hu,Kemeng Huang,Guansheng Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhyVisGen：基于IPC软体接触建模与真实场景重建+实时路径追踪的软体抓取操作数据生成框架，仅用合成数据训练的策略在5个真机任务上达65-95%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模操作演示数据是学习鲁棒视觉运动策略的关键，但真实数据采集成本高、难扩展；仿真数据虽可替代，却存在物理与视觉差异，尤其对软体抓手操作而言，合成数据迁移性差。作者希望构建高物理/视觉保真度的可扩展数据生成框架，实现零真机数据的sim-to-real策略迁移。

Method: 物理侧：提出基于增量势接触（IPC, Incremental Potential Contact）的机械臂-抓手耦合方法，在完整操作轨迹中实现高保真软体接触仿真；视觉侧：结合真实场景重建与实时路径追踪渲染，生成既保留采集场景外观又具视觉真实感的观测。两个模块耦合形成可扩展的软体操作数据生成流水线。

Result: 定量评估验证了PhyVisGen在物理保真度与视觉保真度两方面的优势；仅使用合成操作演示训练的策略，在五个真实机器人任务上取得65-95%的成功率，且未使用任何真机演示数据或策略微调。

Conclusion: 高保真物理+视觉的合成数据生成可以替代真实演示采集，实现软体抓手操作的零样本sim-to-real迁移，为可扩展的机器人数据生产提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation demonstrations are essential for learning robust visuomotor policies, yet real-world data collection is expensive and difficult to scale. Simulation offers a promising alternative, but physical and visual discrepancies can limit the transferability of synthetic data, particularly for manipulation with soft grippers. We present PhyVisGen, a physically and visually high-fidelity framework for scalable robotic manipulation data generation. On the physical side, PhyVisGen introduces an arm-gripper coupling method based on the Incremental Potential Contact (IPC), enabling high-fidelity soft contact throughout complete manipulation trajectories. On the visual side, it combines real-scene reconstruction with real-time path tracing to generate visually realistic observations while preserving captured scene appearance. Quantitative evaluations demonstrate the physical and visual fidelity of PhyVisGen. Policies trained exclusively on synthetic manipulation demonstrations achieve 65-95% success across five real-robot tasks, without real-robot demonstration data or policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [144] [CODA: Depth-Aligned Scene Completion and Object Decomposition from a Single RGB-D Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25654)
*Dongwon Son,Junhyek Han,Yoontae Cho,Minseok Lee,Hong-seok Choi,Jiwook Choi,Hyungjin Kim,Beomjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CODA 提出一种生成式模型，从单张未分割 RGB-D 图像直接重建完整场景几何，再将表面分解为环境与可移动物体，并用两种显式 3D 接地机制抑制生成几何与观测点云的漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需在杂乱日常环境中从局部观测推断场景几何。传统“先检测物体再独立重建”的范式存在缺陷：漏检目标永不被重建、检测合并会导致物体融合、独立重建的网格可能互相穿模或未贴合支撑面。作者希望绕过显式实例分割，直接从单张 RGB-D 图像重建完整几何后再做表面分解。

Method: CODA 是一个生成式模型：输入单张未分割 RGB-D 图像，先整体生成完整场景几何（包括未观测区域的补全），随后将表面分解为周围环境与可移动物体两类。为缓解生成几何偏离观测部分点云的问题，模型引入两种显式 3D 接地（grounding）机制，在补全不可见区域的同时保持重建几何与观测表面一致。

Result: 在 HomebrewedDB 及作者自建的杂乱场景数据集上，CODA 相比 object-first 与 scene-first 基线获得更准确的重建结果，且在模拟重力下物体保持在原位（不掉落/不漂移）的比例更高。

Conclusion: “先完整重建、后分解” 的生成式范式可规避显式检测带来的漏检、合并与几何不一致问题，配合 3D 接地机制有效提升杂乱场景几何重建精度与物理合理性，为机器人场景理解提供新思路。

Abstract: Robots operating safely in cluttered everyday environments often need to infer scene geometry from partial observations. Methods that detect objects in 2D and reconstruct them independently struggle in such scenes: a missed object is never reconstructed, a merged detection can fuse two objects, and separately reconstructed meshes may overlap or fail to touch their supporting surfaces. We introduce CODA (Complete Once, Decompose Afterward), a generative model that instead reconstructs the complete scene geometry from a single unsegmented RGB-D image, then separates the surface into the surrounding environment and movable objects. Still, generated scene geometry can drift from the observed partial point cloud. To reduce this drift, CODA uses two explicit 3D grounding mechanisms to keep reconstructed geometry consistent with observed surfaces while completing unseen regions. Experiments on HomebrewedDB and our custom cluttered-scene dataset show more accurate reconstructions and a higher fraction of objects remaining in place under simulated gravity than both object-first and scene-first baselines.

</details>


### [145] [History-Conditioned Flow Matching for Probabilistic Dynamics of Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25658)
*Hang Yang,Tingcong Liu,Junjie Xiong,Fangju Yang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出历史条件化的物理信息流匹配（flow matching）框架，对腱驱动连续体机器人进行概率动力学预测，用运动与驱动历史预测下一步完整关节构型分布，并递归采样得到未来全身运动分布。仿真与真机实验显示其Energy Score与覆盖率均优于CVAE和扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机器人存在材料行为、腱传动、摩擦与接触等不确定性，仅凭测量的关节构型与名义腱指令无法完全刻画内部力学因素，导致后续运动存在不确定性，因此需要概率化动力学建模。

Method: 构建历史条件化、物理信息引导的flow-matching概率动力学预测框架：以运动史和驱动史为条件，预测下一时刻完整关节构型的分布；在给定指令下递归采样下一步构型以滚动预测全身运动分布。并与条件变分自编码器、扩散基线及消融（去掉历史条件与结构条件）对比。

Result: 仿真中：内部摩擦变化场景五秒轨迹Energy Score为12.05 mm，未观测驱动扰动场景为9.29 mm，均低于CVAE与扩散基线且覆盖率更接近名义水平。真机中：两组未参与训练的腱指令下五秒Energy Score为11.91 mm与11.42 mm，优于基线；预测-实测离散度比分别为1.65与1.22（越接近1越好）。

Conclusion: 结果表明在机械内部状态观测不完整的情况下，历史条件化的概率动力学预测可行且有效，可作为连续体机器人不确定性运动预测的实用方案。

Abstract: Deterministic dynamics modeling of tendon-driven continuum robots remains challenging owing to uncertainties in material behavior, tendon transmission, friction, and contact. Measured joint configurations and nominal tendon commands do not fully characterize these internal mechanical factors, leaving uncertainty in the subsequent motion. We therefore develop a history-conditioned, physics-informed flow-matching framework for probabilistic dynamics prediction, using motion and actuation histories to predict the distribution of the next complete joint configuration. By recursively sampling next-step configurations under prescribed commands, the model predicts distributions of future whole-body motions. In simulation, scenario-specific models achieve five-second trajectory Energy Scores (lower is better) of 12.05 mm under internal friction variation and 9.29 mm under unobserved actuation disturbances. Relative to the conditional variational autoencoder and diffusion baselines, Flow attains lower Energy Scores and coverage closer to the nominal level in both scenarios. Ablations support history and structural conditioning in both scenarios. On the physical robot, predictions under two tendon-command profiles excluded from training capture the principal motion sequences, with five-second Energy Scores of 11.91 and 11.42 mm, lower than the compared baselines. The predicted-to-measured spread ratios are 1.65 and 1.22 (closer to 1 is better). These results support history-conditioned probabilistic dynamics prediction under incomplete mechanical observations.

</details>


### [146] [Deploying Foundation Models for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25666)
*Vishnu Sashank Dorbala,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对基础模型在具身导航部署中的两个问题——训练偏差导致的个性化不足以及上下文长度受限——分别提出TAP（利用场景中挖掘的人类习惯数据进行提示）和MemCtrl（通过新型"记忆头"增强实现主动记忆管理），并在真实机器人和多种具身任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型部署到具身导航智能体时面临两大障碍：一是模型训练偏差导致在未见环境中个性化目标查找表现差；二是有限的上下文长度限制长时程任务的成功率。作者希望系统梳理相关文献并针对这两点提出解决方案。

Method: 首先给出FM-based具身导航的文献分类体系以凸显上述局限；针对个性化问题提出Transit-Aware Planning（TAP），用从场景中挖掘的人类习惯数据对FM进行priming；针对上下文限制提出MemCtrl，通过新型"memory head"增强实现主动记忆管理。

Result: TAP在实验室环境中用Turtlebot进行真实世界个性化目标查找，较非TAP基线平均提升18%；MemCtrl在多种具身任务上平均提升6%，在长指令子集上提升20%，且上下文使用量约为基线的一半。

Conclusion: 作者基于上述结果表明对FM-based具身智能体现实部署的立场，并指出若干开放研究方向。

Abstract: We present and tackle two problems associated with deploying Foundation Models (FMs) on Embodied Agents performing navigation: 1) Training bias in FMs leading to poor personalization in unseen environments, and 2) Limited FM context length hindering success, especially on long horizon tasks. Our solution for the former involves priming the FM with human-habit data mined from the scene and our solution for the latter involves active memory management via a novel `memory head' augmentation. We first present a taxonomy of existing literature on FM-based Embodied Navigation, and highlight these limitations. We then present our approaches, Transit-Aware Planning (TAP) and MemCtrl to address the limitations. With TAP, we present real-world results in a lab environment with a Turtlebot for personalized target finding that shows an average improvement of 18% over a non-TAP baseline. On MemCtrl, we report a 6% average improvement across various embodied tasks, with 20% on long instruction subsets, all while using nearly half the context used in the baseline model. Motivated by these result, we present our stance the deployability of FM-based embodied agents in real-world environments, and highlight open research directions.

</details>


### [147] [CDKF-Track: Cluster-aware Data-Driven Kalman Filtering for Cooperative 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25668)
*Maria Damanaki,Nikos Piperigkos,Alexandros Gkillas,Aris S. Lalos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CDKF-Track：面向协同 3D 多目标跟踪的聚类感知数据驱动卡尔曼滤波框架，联合解决检测级融合冗余与可学习运动建模，在 V2V4Real 上跟踪精度最高提升 27.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体 MOT 在遮挡、传感器噪声与局部场景理解不足下性能受限；多智能体虽提升鲁棒性，却引入冗余观测导致错误数据关联，且难以刻画非线性目标运动。因此需同时解决检测级融合冗余与运动模型可学习性问题。

Method: 1) 基于图拉普拉斯的形式化方法融合多车 3D LiDAR 检测；2) 聚类感知冗余削减模块，将空间相关检测分组并选取代表性观测，减少重复输入；3) 数据驱动卡尔曼滤波，从数据中学习目标运动动力学，降低对预设线性运动假设的依赖；4) 基于小波的多分辨率时域精化模块，抑制短期位置抖动、提升轨迹连续性。

Result: 在真实世界 V2V4Real 数据集上，跟踪精度相较当前最优多智能体 MOT 方法最高提升 27.99%。

Conclusion: CDKF-Track 是首个在协同 3D MOT 中联合处理检测级融合冗余与可学习运动建模的框架，为边缘感知系统的协同跟踪提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) is essential for EdgeAI perception systems, where accurate object localization and reliable identification enable safe decision-making. Singleagent MOT suffers from occlusions, sensor noise, and partial scene understanding in complex real-world scenarios. While multi-agent systems improve robustness by exploiting shared information, they introduce redundant measurements that lead to false data associations, and still struggle to capture nonlinear object dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose CDKFTrack, a Cluster-aware Data-Driven Kalman Filtering framework for Cooperative 3D MOT. The proposed method first fuses multivehicle 3D LiDAR detections through a Graph Laplacian-based formulation. Then, a cluster-aware redundancy reduction scheme groups spatially related detections and selects representative observations to reduce duplicate inputs to the tracker. The resulting detections are processed by a data-driven Kalman filter that learns object motion dynamics from data, reducing dependence on predefined linear motion assumptions. Furthermore, a wavelet-based temporal refinement module leverages the multiresolution decomposition property of wavelets to attenuate shortterm positional fluctuations and improve trajectory continuity. To the best of our knowledge, CDKF-Track is the first framework to jointly address detection-level fusion redundancy and learnable motion modeling in cooperative 3D MOT. Experimental results on the real-world V2V4Real dataset indicate that CDKF-Track achieves up to 27.99% improvements in tracking accuracy over state-of-the-art multi-agent MOT methods.

</details>


### [148] [Teaching Reinforcement Learning and Humanoid Robotics to High-School Students: An Expert-Validated Curriculum Design on a Low-Cost Open Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25674)
*Yuanzhe Dong,Jie Cao,Shuman Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以共享机器人为核心的整合式机器人课程框架，包含并行学科轨道、依赖排序、仿真-硬件渐进整合、分层考核点与团队/个人责任平衡机制；以高中生组装开源人形机器人、仿真训练行走策略并实物部署的课程为例，经五位专家迭代评审，揭示真实性与认知负荷、系统集成与可见进展、团队建设与个人责任三大设计张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本开源机器人与RL仿真工具使中学生接触现代机器人成为可能，但完整的科研工作流（机械装配、电气搭建、仿真、策略学习、系统辨识、实物部署）难以转化为适合初学者的连贯课程。

Method: 以共享机器人制品组织课程活动的整合框架，采用平行学科轨道、基于技术依赖的排序、仿真与硬件的渐进整合、分层性能检查点、协作与个人问责平衡结构；通过迭代设计流程（含五位机器人、工程、中学STEM教育及课程设计专家的形成性评审）开发，并以高中生双人组装配开源人形机器人、仿真训练行走策略、实物部署的高中课程为例说明。

Result: 专家反馈提炼出三大核心设计张力：真实性与认知负荷、系统集成与及时可见进展、团队构建与个人问责，并据此形成最终框架。

Conclusion: 为将机器人科研工作流转化为跨学科预科课程提供了结构化方法；未来需开展课堂研究以检验实施效果与学生学习成效。

Abstract: Lower cost open source robots and reinforcement learning (RL) simulation tools create new opportunities for precollege students to engage with contemporary robotics. However, translating a complete research workflow, spanning mechanical assembly, electrical setup, simulation, policy learning, system identification, and physical deployment, into a coherent course for novice learners remains challenging. We present an integrated robotics course framework that organizes these activities around a shared robotic artifact. The framework combines parallel disciplinary tracks, sequencing based on technical dependencies, progressive integration of simulation and hardware, layered performance checkpoints, and structures for balancing collaborative work with individual accountability. We illustrate the framework through a high school curriculum organized around a robot project in which pairs of students assemble an open source humanoid robot, train a walking policy in simulation, and deploy it on the physical platform. The framework was developed through an iterative design process that included formative review by five experts in robotics research, engineering, secondary STEM education, and curriculum design. Expert feedback highlighted three central design tensions: authenticity versus cognitive load, system integration versus timely visible progress, and team construction versus individual accountability. These tensions informed the final framework presented in this paper. This work offers a structured approach for adapting robotics research workflows into interdisciplinary precollege courses; future classroom studies are needed to examine implementation and student learning.

</details>


### [149] [SG-CPG: Severity-Gated Central Pattern Generators for Adaptive Quadruped Locomotion under Continuous Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25687)
*Adarsh Kumar Kosta,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受动物肢体无力时渐进卸载而非切换步态的启发，提出SG-CPG：保留冻结的健康CPG策略，加入严重度驱动的残差门与幅值门，实现执行器渐进退化下的四足稳健运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物在肢体力量部分丧失时不会离散地切换步态，而是卸载患肢、重组其余肢体并随损伤严重度缩放响应。现有四足控制策略多将故障视为离散失效或依赖健康假设，难以在连续执行器退化下保持运动能力。

Method: 在冻结的健康CPG策略上引入两个严重度门：残差门重新协调四条腿的动作，幅值门随退化程度逐渐缩短弱化腿的步幅。用两种机制模拟渐进退化——降低关节力矩上限（ceiling机制）与缩放低层控制器增益（gain机制）。

Result: Unitree Go2仿真：全向指令序列下可维持trot步态，95%关节力量损失时存活率100%，指令跟踪误差在8%内。在80%损失、降低力矩上限条件下，移除残差严重度观测或幅值门会将弱化关节的力矩饱和从4.4%升至13.6%与26.3%。真实Go2上，小腿扭矩退化最高93%时29次前进与转向试验成功28次。

Conclusion: 严重度门控的适应机制可将健康运动策略扩展至渐进执行器退化场景，无需将故障当作离散失效处理，为容错四足运动提供连续退化下的可行范式。

Abstract: An animal with a weakened limb does not necessarily switch its gait, instead it unloads the affected limb, re-coordinates the remaining limbs, and scales its response with injury severity. This graded adaptation allows locomotion to persist despite partial loss of limb strength, rather than requiring a discrete transition between healthy and failed. Inspired by this behavior, we propose SG-CPG, a central pattern generator (CPG) for quadruped locomotion under continuous actuator degradation. SG-CPG preserves a frozen healthy CPG policy and introduces two severity-driven gates: a residual gate that re-coordinates all four legs and an amplitude gate that progressively shortens the weakened leg's stride as degradation increases. We emulate progressive degradation through two mechanisms: lowering the joint torque ceiling (ceiling mechanism) and scaling its low-level controller gains (gain mechanism), representing distinct forms of actuator weakening. Our simulations on a Unitree Go2 show that SG-CPG maintains a trot gait with 100% survival across an omnidirectional command schedule under 95% joint strength loss while tracking commands within 8%. Under a lowered torque ceiling, removing either severity path, the residual's severity observation or the amplitude gate, raises clipping at the weakened joint from 4.4% to 13.6% and 26.3% of steps at an 80% loss. On a real Go2, SG-CPG survives 28 of 29 forward and turning trials with up to 93% calf torque degradation. These results show that severity-gated adaptation can extend a healthy locomotion policy to progressive actuator degradation without treating the fault as a discrete failure.

</details>


### [150] [MatcherCompass: A Deployment-Aware Benchmark to Guide Image Matcher Selection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25688)
*Hyunwoo Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MatcherCompass——一个部署感知的局部特征匹配器选型基准，在统一输入与位姿评估流程下比较 9 种经典与学习型匹配流水线，覆盖 4 种分辨率、多种数值精度、4 种视觉条件（视角变化、昼夜可见光、昼夜热成像、白天可见光-热）与 4 个 GPU 平台，输出满足时延/显存/能耗约束的配置选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外机器人需在昼夜、跨模态条件下做图像对应，且受机载时间与资源预算限制。但已有方法多只在单一设备上报准确率与运行时，无法指导在目标平台上选择匹配器及其配置（分辨率、精度）。

Method: 在统一输入与位姿评估流程下，对 9 个经典与学习型匹配流水线进行系统评测；变量包括 4 种图像分辨率、支持的数值精度、4 种视觉条件；指标为 AUC@5°/10°/20° 位姿精度，以及每图像对的运行时、GPU 内存与能耗；硬件覆盖 4 个从工作站到机载的 GPU 平台；将测量结果整理为选型指南，返回满足用户指定时间与资源限制的全部配置及对应精度。

Result: 硬件、输入分辨率与数值精度的变化会使匹配器跨过运行时预算边界，从而改变可行选择集合；据此给出受限条件下的可行配置与精度查询指南。

Conclusion: MatcherCompass 为按机器人感知条件与计算硬件选择匹配流水线提供了实测依据，但主要是基准与选型工具，不含新匹配方法。

Abstract: Field robots operating across time of day and sensing modalities require accurate image correspondences within onboard time and resource budgets. However, accuracy and runtime reported for individual methods on a single device provide limited guidance for choosing a matcher and its configuration on a target platform. We present MatcherCompass, a deployment-aware benchmark for choosing local feature matchers in field robotics. Under common input and pose-evaluation procedures, we compare nine classical and learned matching pipelines across four image resolutions and supported numerical precisions. Four visual conditions cover viewpoint variation, day--night matching in visible and thermal imagery, and daytime visible--thermal matching. We evaluate pose accuracy using the area under the error--recall curve (AUC) at $5^\circ$, $10^\circ$, and $20^\circ$, and measure runtime, GPU memory, and energy per image pair on four GPU platforms spanning workstation and onboard computers. The results show that changes in hardware, input resolution, and numerical precision can move a matcher across a runtime budget boundary, altering the feasible choices. We organize the measurements into a selection guide that returns all configurations satisfying user-specified time and resource limits, together with their accuracy under the selected visual condition. MatcherCompass provides measured evidence for choosing matching pipelines that fit a robot's sensing conditions and computing hardware. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [151] [MotionForge: A Data Generation Pipeline and Large-Scale Benchmark for Long-Horizon Manipulation of Dynamic Objects with Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25689)
*Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Haotian Xian,Ruyang Han,Jiayi Sun,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Luxi Li,Kaimin Mao,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MotionForge：面向动态操作的大规模仿真基准与数据生成管线，涵盖40个动态交互任务（11种运动模式，17个长时程任务），提出单因子/联合域偏移评估协议与解耦、延迟感知的实时执行协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型机器人策略主要在静态或准静态环境中评估；动态操作中物体与场景在感知-推理-动作过程中持续演化，而已有动态仿真基准多为短时程、反应式交互，运动模式简单，且缺乏对域偏移下系统评估与模型无关实时执行协议的支持。

Method: 构建MotionForge基准与数据生成管线：40个动态交互任务、11种运动模式、17个长时程任务；引入两项机制：(1) 单因子与联合域偏移（物体、背景、光照、速度同时变化）的系统鲁棒性评估协议；(2) 解耦的延迟感知执行协议，使环境演化独立于策略推理时间。

Result: 对代表性通用机器人策略的广泛评估显示，在联合域偏移下存在显著性能局限，暴露了当前策略能力与鲁棒长时程动态操作需求之间的关键差距。

Conclusion: MotionForge可作为具身智能中动态操作鲁棒性研究的综合测试平台，但仍以仿真评估为主，缺少真实机器人验证与具体策略方法创新。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot policies have demonstrated promising progress, yet they are predom- inantly evaluated in static or quasi-static environments. In dynamic manipulation, objects and scenes continuously evolve while the robot perceives, reasons, and acts. However, recent dynamic simulation benchmarks largely focus on short-horizon, reactive interactions with simple motion patterns and offer limited support for both systematic evaluation under domain shifts and model-agnostic real-time execution protocols. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MotionForge, the first large- scale simulation benchmark and data-generation pipeline tailored to jointly evaluate domain shifts and long-horizon interaction in dynamic manipulation. MotionForge comprises 40 dynamic interaction tasks spanning 11 distinct motion patterns, with dedicated support for 17 long-horizon tasks. Our benchmark introduces two key novelties: (1) a systematic evaluation protocol for assessing policy robustness under both single-factor (e.g., only backgrounds shift) and joint domain shifts (e.g., simultaneous shifts of objects, backgrounds, lighting, and speed); and (2) a decoupled, latency-aware execution protocol where the environ- ment continuously evolves independently of policy inference time. Extensive evaluations of representative general-purpose robot policies on our benchmark reveal substantial limitations under joint domain shifts. These findings expose a critical gap between current policy capabilities and the requirements of robust long- horizon manipulation of dynamic objects under domain shifts, establishing MotionForge as a comprehensive testbed for future research in embodied AI.

</details>


### [152] [Induced Riemannian Metrics for Motion Planning with Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25695)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Thomas Cohn,Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作指出约束运动规划中隐式与显式表示应统一采用子流形从配置空间黎曼度量继承的诱导度量，从而解耦度量选择与表示选择，并将黎曼度量下的规划扩展到约束子流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束运动规划中，任务与闭环约束将运动限制在配置空间的低维弯曲子流形上。现有方法多将隐式表示（约束水平集）与欧氏度量绑定、显式表示（参数化）与参数域度量绑定，导致规划器最小化的路径长度依赖于所选取的表示形式，缺乏几何一致性。

Method: 理论上证明隐式与显式表示所诱导的度量是同一诱导度量在不同坐标下的表达，因此几何一致，且该结论对任意配置空间黎曼度量成立（不限于欧氏）。基于此，在采样规划器与轨迹优化器中同时应用诱导度量，将黎曼度量下的规划从无约束空间推广到约束子流形；对显式表示，诱导度量还计入了参数化引入的畸变。

Result: 在双臂Franka操作平台、末端执行器任务约束的双臂操作实验中，比较了欧氏度量与动能度量的表现，验证了所提框架的可行性与表示无关性。

Conclusion: 度量选择与表示选择相互独立，采用诱导度量可给出表示无关的一致几何，为约束流形上的黎曼度量规划提供了统一的基础，并在采样规划与轨迹优化中均可应用。

Abstract: In constrained motion planning problems, task and loop-closure constraints restrict a robot's motion to a curved, lower-dimensional submanifold of its configuration space. Planners measure path length with a metric, which sets the cost of moving in each direction. Under the Euclidean metric, this cost is the same everywhere, whereas under a general Riemannian metric, such as the kinetic-energy metric, the cost can vary with direction and configuration. Existing methods often describe the submanifold either implicitly, as a constraint level set, or explicitly, through a parameterization. The implicit representation is typically combined with the Euclidean metric of the configuration space, and the explicit representation with the parameter domain, so the path length that a planner minimizes depends on the representation. Instead, we measure path length with the induced metric, which the submanifold inherits from a Riemannian metric on the configuration space. The implicit and explicit representations yield the same induced metric, expressed in different coordinates, and hence the same geometry. This result holds for any Riemannian metric on the configuration space, not only the Euclidean one. The choice of metric is therefore independent of the choice of representation. Using this result, we extend planning under a Riemannian metric from unconstrained spaces to constraint submanifolds by applying the induced metric in both a sampling-based planner and a trajectory optimizer. For an explicit representation, the induced metric also accounts for the distortion that the parameterization introduces. In experiments on a bimanual manipulation setup with two Franka arms under end-effector task constraints, we compare the Euclidean and kinetic-energy metrics.

</details>


### [153] [The Cartesian Hand: In-Hand Manipulation with All-Linear Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25696)
*Boxi Xia,Bokuan Li,Ryan Shin,Zijiang Yang,Jiaxun Liu,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Cartesian Hand——一种7自由度末端执行器，通过双平行夹爪独立抓取+四个平移指尖产生相对运动，仅用线性运动实现抓取中的相对操作，在35个物体上演示开盖、移液、拧螺丝等任务并迁移到人形机器人双臂操作，将开源软硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多自由度灵巧手能力强但机械与控制复杂，平行夹爪简单鲁棒却难以在抓取后操作物体；操作螺纹、枢轴、滑轨、柱塞、扳机等关节类物体常需第二夹爪、外部夹具或臂部协同运动。作者希望用机械简单的架构同时获得抓取与抓取内相对操作能力。

Method: 设计7-DoF末端执行器：两个独立驱动的平行夹爪分别夹持物体不同部位，四个平移指尖在已抓取部件间产生相对运动；仅依赖线性运动，指尖运动学与构型无关，操作可由线性运动基元组合而成。面向螺纹、枢轴、线性导向、柱塞、扳机等机构化物体设计。软硬件将开源。

Result: 在实验室、制造、家用场景共35个物体上完成开合瓶盖、移液、泵送、双把手操作、拧螺丝、扳机触发、抓取内重定向等任务；同一操作流程可从固定基座机械臂迁移到人形机器人，用两个Cartesian Hand实现双臂实验室操作。

Conclusion: 当把独立抓取与相对运动直接设计进末端执行器时，机械简单的架构即可涌现出多样的抓取内操作能力；该方法通用、可迁移，将开源软硬件设计。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has increasingly pursued human-like dexterous hands with many articulated degrees of freedom, offering rich manipulation capabilities at the cost of mechanical and control complexity. At the other extreme, parallel grippers are simple and robust, but provide little ability to manipulate an object after grasping it. Operating articulated objects such as threaded containers, manufacturing tools, and laboratory instruments often requires a second gripper, an external fixture, or coordinated arm motion. We introduce the Cartesian Hand, a 7-DoF end-effector that rethinks dexterous manipulation by combining independent grasping and relative manipulation within a single end-effector using only linear motion. Two independently actuated parallel grippers hold different parts of an object, while four translating fingertips generate relative motion between the grasped parts. Its configuration-independent fingertip kinematics allow manipulation to be composed from simple linear motion primitives. The Cartesian Hand is particularly suited to objects structured around common mechanisms such as threads, pivots, linear guides, plungers, and triggers. We demonstrate cap opening and closing, pipetting, pumping, two-handle manipulation, screwdriving, trigger actuation, and in-grasp reorientation across 35 objects spanning laboratory, manufacturing, and household settings. The same manipulation procedures transfer from a fixed-base robot arm to a humanoid, where we demonstrate bimanual laboratory manipulation using two Cartesian Hands. These results show that versatile in-hand manipulation capability can emerge from a mechanically simple architecture when independent grasping and relative motion are designed directly into the end-effector. We will open-source all software and hardware design. Our website is this https URL .

</details>


### [154] [Zephyron: Integrated Design and Analytical Evaluation of a Solar-Assisted Mobile Manipulator for Multimodal Environmental Reconnaissance and Distributed Visual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25709)
*Sabik Bin Sultan,Shafi Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Zephyron 四轮巡视机器人的文献驱动工程设计基线，用显式组件与几何参数替代无依据数值假设，并给出可复现的能耗/爬坡解析估算与机器学习评估路径，但尚无论文实测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境侦察需要兼顾传感搭载、测量语境保留、可解释证据回传与有限能量/通信约束的移动平台；已有原型存在缺乏依据的数值假设，需要可追溯的设计与评估基线。

Method: 可复现检索获得 5000 条记录（4858 条去重）并筛选，结合一手文献与厂商资料做定向综述；建立组件与几何基线（165 mm 轮径、12 kg 质量预算、72 Wh 电池能量、20 W 光伏、前置机械臂、分布式视觉与后置太阳能板）；以滚动阻力系数 0.04 计算 10° 坡道等负载下每轮约 0.517 N·m 稳态需求；用驾驶占空比与可用电量做续航情景推算；传感器模型纳入积分时间、标定、温度与通信延迟；提出质量感知的停走采样策略、轻量检测器与参考式传感器学习、可执行数据完整性检查。

Result: 给出可编辑 3D 模型、子系统框图与可复现解析数据的设计-评估框架；示例情景：40 W 运动负载下 57.6 Wh 可用能量续航 1.44 h，25% 驱动占空比无太阳能输入时 4.19 h。作者明确这些为计算结果而非实测性能，实验验证前不应赋予载荷、续航、检测或外场运行等级。

Conclusion: 贡献在于可追溯、可编辑、可复现的工程设计与评估框架，而非提出新算法或实测系统；后续需实机实验验证。

Abstract: Environmental reconnaissance needs mobile platforms that carry sensors, preserve measurement context, and return interpretable evidence under limited energy and communication. We present a literature-informed engineering design for Zephyron, a four-wheel rover with a front manipulator, environmental sensors, distributed computer vision, local recording, and a raised rear solar module. The design keeps the prototype layout but replaces unsupported numerical assumptions with an explicit component and geometry baseline. A reproducible search retrieved 5,000 records (4,858 unique) for screening, followed by targeted review of primary literature and manufacturer documentation. The baseline uses 165 mm wheels, a 12 kg mass budget, a 72 Wh battery-energy basis, and a 20 W photovoltaic module. With rolling-resistance coefficient 0.04, steady ascent of a 10 degree grade needs about 0.517 N m per wheel under equal load sharing. An illustrative 40 W motion load gives 1.44 h from 57.6 Wh usable energy, and a 25 percent driving duty gives 4.19 h without solar input; these are calculated scenarios, not measured performance. Sensor models show how integration time, calibration, temperature, and communication delay constrain interpretation, and a quality-aware stop-and-sample policy links these constraints to mission execution. Lightweight detectors, reference-based sensor learning, and executable data-integrity checks define a reproducible machine-learning evaluation pathway. The contribution is a traceable design and evaluation framework with editable 3D models, subsystem diagrams, and reproducible analytical data. Experimental validation is required before assigning payload, endurance, detection, or field-operating ratings.

</details>


### [155] [Designing an Efficient Excavator Bucket for Lunar ISRU: A Comparative Study with Vision-Based Fill and Displacement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25724)
*Abdulla Hil Kafi,Tomoki Koshi,Jorge Casir,Kenji Nagaoka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出螺旋腔轮式月壤挖掘装置及传感-光照评估栈，联合估计填充率、沉陷量与比能耗；台架实验显示挖掘率提升2.2–3.0倍、比能耗降低29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球原位资源利用需要挖掘月壤，但传统多执行器挖掘机构质量大、结构复杂、能耗高；作者希望用更轻量、简单的轮式机构替代，并配套可量化的评估方法。

Method: 设计螺旋腔轮（spiral-cavity wheel）；构建传感器-光照评估栈，用视觉估计填充率与沉陷量，用执行器日志推算比能耗；在台架上以5、10、15 RPM各旋转四圈，与两个文献基线（含bucket-drum）对比；提出保留指数η并建立DEM仿真流程。

Result: 相比bucket-drum基线，挖掘质量与填充率更高，挖掘率提升2.2–3.0倍，比能耗降低29%；归一化沉陷量(mm/kg)更低，牵引稳定不打滑下陷；力矩包络平稳，cut-carry-dump循环可重复；η与填充率相关，DEM仿真以低误差复现实验趋势。

Conclusion: 螺旋腔轮在质量、结构简单性和能效为任务驱动因素时，可替代更重的多执行器挖掘机；评估栈与η、DEM为后续设计比较提供工具。

Abstract: This paper present a spiral-cavity wheel for lunar regolith excavation and a sensor-light evaluation stack that jointly estimates fill ratio (vision), sinkage (vision), and specific energy from actuator logs. In benchtop tests (four revolutions at 5, 10, and 15~RPM) against two literature baselines, the proposed wheel achieved higher excavated mass and fill ratio, delivering 2.2-3.0 times higher excavation rate while reducing specific energy by 29 % relative to a bucket-drum baseline. Normalized sinkage (mm/kg) was also lower, indicating stable traction without bogging. Effort-time traces show a steady torque envelope with repeatable cut-carry-dump cycles across speeds. We provide a retention index $\eta$ that correlates with fill ratio and a DEM setup that reproduces experimental trends with low error. Results suggest spiral-cavity wheels can replace heavier multi-actuator diggers when mass, simplicity, and energy efficiency are mission drivers.

</details>


### [156] [AgriGen: Large-Scale Scene Generation Framework for Photorealistic Agricultural Robotics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25725)
*Utkarsh Bajpai,Serge Tleiji,Cédric Pradalier,Stéphanie Aravecchia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于 Isaac Sim 的 ROS 集成框架，实现农业环境的大规模程序化生成，支持真实感渲染、物理仿真与域随机化，覆盖行播作物、果园与葡萄园。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人研究受限于实地准入困难、田间条件不可控、地理差异大与季节性作物周期，导致数据集获取成本高、多样性不足，评估受限、系统鲁棒性下降；其他机器人领域已通过高保真仿真解决该问题，农业机器人缺乏同等能力的仿真工具。

Method: 在 Isaac Sim 之上构建 ROS 集成的农业环境程序化生成框架，支持照片级渲染、物理仿真与域随机化，内置行播作物、果园、葡萄园场景，并可扩展至其他作物类别。

Result: 摘要层面未给出定量结果；主要产出为可扩展的仿真框架与项目主页，宣称支持面向机器人研究规模的环境生成。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了农业机器人领域高保真、可扩展仿真工具的空白，有望降低数据获取成本并提升评估与系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: Agricultural robotics is advancing rapidly, yet progress remains constrained by limited field access, lack of control over field conditions, geographic variability, and seasonal crop cycles. These factors make it difficult and costly to acquire diverse agricultural datasets, resulting in limited evaluation and reduced system robustness. While other robotics domains have scaled learning and evaluation through high-fidelity simulation, agricultural robotics still lacks comparably capable tools. In this paper, we present a ROS-integrated framework, built on Isaac Sim, for large-scale procedural generation of agricultural environments. The framework supports photorealistic rendering, physics simulation, and domain randomization at scales relevant to robotics research, with built-in support for row crops, orchards, and vineyards and straightforward extensibility to additional crop categories. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [157] [Dual Covariance Gaussian Splatting SLAM: Decoupling Rendering and Registration for Robust Real-Time Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25746)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双协方差参数化的3DGS SLAM：每个高斯保留单一均值但拥有两个协方差（渲染协方差用于光度优化，跟踪协方差源自RGB-D噪声模型），并将跟踪协方差作为图像角点锚点以补充弱深度方向约束，在多个数据集上实现鲁棒跟踪与更低漂移，约60 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于ICP的3DGS SLAM在配准新帧与地图高斯时，渲染与配准共用同一协方差，存在冲突：mapper为使光度误差最小常将其压扁贴合表面，而稳健配准更需要体现测量不确定性，二者需求矛盾。

Method: 双协方差参数化：每个高斯仅有一个均值，但维护两个协方差——由mapper优化的渲染协方差，以及由RGB-D传感器噪声模型推导的跟踪协方差；同时将跟踪协方差用作图像角点的高斯锚点，在深度几何较弱的方向上提供约束。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D、ScanNet、Replica及两段用RealSense D435i在轮式与手持平台采集的室外序列上评估，多场景下跟踪鲁棒、里程计漂移下降，跟踪速度约60 FPS。

Conclusion: 解耦渲染与跟踪的协方差需求可同时提升跟踪鲁棒性与精度，且保持实时性能；该方法为3DGS SLAM中几何/光度优化与配准不确定性的失衡问题提供了简洁有效的解决方案。

Abstract: ICP-based 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM tracks in real time by registering incoming frames against map Gaussians, using each primitive's covariance for both rendering and registration. These two uses place conflicting demands on one covariance. The mapper shapes it to minimize photometric error, often flattening it against surfaces, while robust registration typically benefits from measurement uncertainty. We propose a dual-covariance parameterization. Each Gaussian keeps a single mean but holds two covariances: a rendering covariance optimized by the mapper, and a tracking covariance derived from an RGB-D sensor noise model. We further use the tracking covariances as Gaussian anchors for image corners, providing constraints in directions where depth geometry is weak. We evaluate on TUM RGB-D, ScanNet, Replica, and two outdoor sequences recorded with a RealSense D435i on wheeled and handheld platforms. We achieve robust tracking performance across multiple scenes and reduced odometry drift, while tracking at $\sim$ 60 FPS.

</details>


### [158] [Fisheye-VLA: Decoupling Coverage and Acuity for Manipulation with a Single Fisheye Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25750)
*Ziang Ren,Zike Yan,Raymond Zhang,Xuguo He,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fisheye-VLA 用单个被动鱼眼相机同时提供全局场景感知与局部细节反馈，通过重渲染研究确定局部裁剪应分配给末端执行器方向，替代传统前视+腕部相机配置，在扩展桌面区域达84%/82%成功率并支持货架与传送带操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作任务既需要广域场景感知，也需要局部细节反馈，但传统相机配置需分别依赖前置相机与腕部相机，增加了硬件复杂度与标定负担。作者希望用单一被动鱼眼相机兼顾这两种能力，并回答"局部视觉预算应投向何处"这一关键设计问题。

Method: 提出 Fisheye-VLA 视觉接口：单个被动鱼眼提供保持工作空间的全局视图，并生成指向交互细节的局部透视裁剪。通过受控重渲染实验，在相同录制观测上比较不同裁剪方向，评估各候选方向的收益。局部裁剪采用标定的末端执行器投影与运动前导进行跟踪，并用共享射线编码保持裁剪移动时的空间语义。整体与预训练 VLA 集成。

Result: 以末端执行器为中心的视图捕获了远大于候选池中大部分方向的估计收益，支持围绕双手的紧凑分配。在两个扩展桌面区域成功率分别为84%和82%，其中部分目标放置超出前置相机覆盖范围，并支持货架与传送带操作。消融实验表明，在工作空间较大的区域，局部裁剪及其观察方向更为重要。

Conclusion: 单个鱼眼相机无需物理腕部相机即可支持此类操作任务，为VLA的视觉接口设计提供了一种紧凑、可标定且全局-局部兼顾的替代方案。

Abstract: Manipulation requires both broad scene awareness and detailed local feedback, yet conventional camera rigs provide them through separate front and wrist cameras. We present Fisheye-VLA, a visual interface that brings these capabilities together using a single passive fisheye. A global view preserves the workspace, while local perspective crops direct detail toward the interaction. The key design question is where this local visual budget should go. We answer it through a controlled re-rendering study, comparing alternative crop directions on the same recorded observations. The study finds that end-effector-centered views capture most of the estimated benefit of a much larger candidate pool, motivating a compact allocation around both hands. Our interface uses calibrated end-effector projection and motion lead to track the crops, while a shared ray encoding preserves their spatial meaning as they move. Integrated with a pretrained VLA, it achieves 84% and 82% success in the two expanded tabletop regions, where some target placements extend beyond the front-camera coverage, and supports shelf and conveyor manipulation. Ablations show that local crops and their viewing directions become more important in the larger workspace regions. The results demonstrate that a single fisheye can support these manipulation tasks without physical wrist cameras.

</details>


### [159] [PLAT: Sparse Timed Keyframe Motion Tracking for Humanoid Control via Privileged Latent Transition Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25754)
*Zepeng Wang,Jiangxing Wang,Chao Ma,Xiaochuan Shi,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PLAT三阶段框架，通过特权潜在转移学习实现稀疏定时关键帧的人形动作跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形运动跟踪策略依赖稠密逐帧参考，难以作为高层运动控制器用于规划与交互式运动生成，需要仅凭稀疏未来关键帧及到达时间即可执行稳定全身运动。

Method: PLAT三阶段：先用预训练稠密跟踪专家提供运动先验；再通过DAgger式模仿学习特权潜在先验；最后用潜在残差强化学习细化潜在转移而非直接优化动作。训练时利用稠密目标序列作为特权监督，部署时仅需稀疏定时关键帧指令。

Result: 仿真实验表明PLAT在不同规划时域下保持准确稳定的稀疏定时关键帧跟踪，长时域指令下表现尤为突出；在Unitree G1人形机器人上成功部署，验证了有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: PLAT桥接了稠密运动跟踪与稀疏目标条件控制，为稀疏人形运动控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking policies rely on dense frame-by-frame references, limiting their use as high-level motion controllers for planning and interactive motion generation. We study \emph{Sparse Timed Keyframe Motion Tracking}, where a policy receives only sparse future keyframes and their desired arrival times, and must execute stable whole-body motions that reach successive goals. We propose \textbf{PLAT}, a three-stage sparse timed keyframe motion tracking policy learning framework with \textbf{P}rivileged \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{T}ransition learning. PLAT bridges dense motion tracking and sparse goal-conditioned control by exploiting dense goal sequences as privileged supervision during training while requiring only sparse timed keyframe commands at deployment. A pretrained dense tracking expert first provides robust motion priors. A privileged latent prior is then learned through DAgger-style imitation, followed by latent residual reinforcement learning that refines latent transitions instead of directly optimizing actions. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that PLAT maintains accurate and stable sparse timed keyframe tracking across varying planning horizons, with particularly strong performance under long-horizon commands. Successful deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot further demonstrates the effectiveness and practicality of PLAT for sparse humanoid motion control.

</details>


### [160] [MedVLA: A Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Framework for Closed-Loop Precision Medical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25756)
*Junjie Xie,Chuxuan He,Angen Ye,Yujia Song,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MedVLA：面向精准医疗机器人的分层VLA框架，高层多模态推理+低层函数约束执行，配合多智能体流水线生成技能导向CoT数据，柔性电极植入闭环实验成功率达95.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的连续动作生成范式不适配精准医疗任务：这类任务对安全性、可解释性和执行约束要求严格，且可靠闭环操作往往还依赖非动作类的系统函数调用，因此需要新的决策与执行架构。

Method: 提出MedVLA分层框架，将高层多模态推理与低层函数约束执行解耦；并设计可扩展的多智能体流水线，自动生成技能导向的思维链（CoT）数据用于结构化训练；在多种多模态大模型骨干上微调验证。

Result: 在不同多模态大模型骨干上微调后性能一致提升；相同初始条件下完成100次闭环柔性电极植入试验，任务成功率95.0%，显著优于OpenVLA（8%）与π0（15%），在准确性、稳定性和安全性上均占优。

Conclusion: 结构化推理结合受限函数级执行是通往可部署精准医疗机器人的可行技术路径，为受约束的医疗操作场景提供了VLA落地范式。

Abstract: Precision medical robotics demands adaptive decision-making under strict safety, interpretability, and execution constraints. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show strong multimodal reasoning ability, their continuous action generation paradigm is not well suited for precision medical tasks, where reliable closed-loop operation may also depend on non-action system function calls. To address this gap, we propose MedVLA, a hierarchical framework that couples high-level multimodal reasoning with low-level function-constrained execution. We further introduce a scalable multi-agent pipeline to generate skill-oriented chain-of-thought(CoT) data for structured training. Built on different multimodal large-model backbones, MedVLA consistently improves performance after fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework across model variants. Under identical initial conditions, we perform 100 closed-loop flexible electrode implantation trials. The results show that MedVLA achieves a 95.0\% task success rate, substantially outperforming representative VLA baselines, including OpenVLA (8\%) and $\pi_0$ (15\%), in accuracy, stability, and safety. These results indicate that structured reasoning with constrained function-level execution is a practical route toward deployable precision medical robotics.

</details>


### [161] [VisForce: Visual Grounding of Current and Desired Forces for Goal-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25785)
*Jung-Woo Lee,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VisForce，将当前与目标力线索以视觉形式直接渲染在腕部图像与目标图像的指尖位置上，通过目标条件交叉注意力生成力感知动作，在真实 UR10+RH56F1 灵巧手上验证力条件抓取与三项多阶段操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型已能作为通用操作策略，但在灵巧手操作中接触力通常以独立状态或力专用表示输入，无法显式建立"力—对应视觉位置"的空间对应关系，导致力信息与视觉观测解耦，难以精细调控抓握力与滑移等接触丰富行为。

Method: VisForce 将当前力与期望力渲染为视觉力线索，分别叠加在当前腕部相机图像和任务特定的目标图像上；两者通过目标条件交叉注意力（goal-conditioned cross-attention）融合，输出力感知动作。指尖对齐的渲染使力线索天然携带空间落点，无需额外的力状态编码。

Result: 真实 UR10 + RH56F1 平台：力条件抓取中抓握力随期望力单调一致响应，鸡蛋与牙膏管抓取—提升成功率 70% 与 80%；多阶段任务最终成功率分别为杯子插入/倒瓶 70%、镊子辅助面包转移 55%、滑移调制轴孔装配 40%。

Conclusion: 指尖对齐的视觉力表示可作为 VLA 灵巧手操作中有效的力感知条件，证明将力信息"视觉落地"比独立力状态输入更利于空间对应与接触调控；但高精度滑移调制任务成功率偏低（40%），细粒度力控制仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as general-purpose robotic manipulation policies. However, in dexterous hand manipulation, contact forces are typically provided as separate states or force-specific representations, making it difficult to explicitly represent the spatial correspondence between force and their corresponding visual locations. In this work, we propose VisForce, which visually grounds the current and desired forces at their corresponding fingertip locations. VisForce renders current and desired visual force cues on the current wrist image and a task-specific goal image, and combines the two representations through goal-conditioned cross-attention to generate force-aware actions. We evaluate VisForce using a real UR10 robot equipped with an RH56F1 dexterous hand through force-conditioned grasping and three multi-stage manipulation tasks. In force-conditioned grasping experiments, VisForce exhibited a consistent grip-force response as the desired force increased, and achieved grasp-and-lift success rates of 70% and 80% for an egg and a toothpaste tube, respectively. It further achieved final success rates of 70%, 55%, and 40% on cup insertion/bottle pouring, tong-assisted bread transfer, and slip-modulated peg-in-hole, respectively. These results show that fingertip-aligned visual force representations can be effectively used for force-aware conditioning in VLA-based dexterous hand manipulation.

</details>


### [162] [MOLA LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (MOLA-LIO) on the COMFORT Localization Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25813)
*Jose Luis Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将 MOLA-LO 扩展为融合 IMU 与可选腿部运动学里程计的 LiDAR-惯性系统，作为 COMFORT 定位基准（IROS 2026）参赛方案，报告架构、输入流、选型协议与实时性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当地团队参加 COMFORT Localization Benchmark，需要在 GrandTour 数据集（Boxi 载荷）上取得可实时运行的定位方案，故以已有的 MOLA-LO 为基础扩展多模态输入。

Method: 在 MOLA-LO 上增加 IMU 与可选 legged kinematic odometry 融合，形成 LiDAR-inertial 架构；本地协议筛选提交配置；以实测指标支持实时性声明。

Result: 摘要未给出具体精度或速度数值，仅陈述有实时性测量证据与一套本地选型流程；基准评测结果未量化披露。

Conclusion: 属短期（short report）型参赛技术报告，聚焦系统集成与基准提交，方法增量对通用 LiDAR-inertial SLAM 的启示有限。

Abstract: This short report documents our entry to the COMFORT Localization Benchmark (IROS 2026), evaluated on the GrandTour dataset recorded with the Boxi payload. It extends MOLA-LO into a LiDAR-inertial system that also ingests IMU and, optionally, legged kinematic odometry. We describe the architecture, the streams consumed, the local protocol that selected the submitted configuration, and the measurements backing our real-time claim.

</details>


### [163] [Beyond Reconstruction Error: Analytical and Data-Driven Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25820)
*Yuxin Yang,Gaohan He,Changxue Guan,Hangming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文质疑以重建保真度评估VLA动作表示的传统做法，在统一tokenization接口下比较解析、线性数据驱动与非线性神经表示，发现表示排名随评价指标变化，重建误差低并不代表闭环控制性能好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散动作tokenization是自回归VLA模型的核心，但动作表示通常仅以重建保真度来评估。作者希望弄清哪种表示性质真正决定闭环控制性能。

Method: 在统一的tokenization接口下，对比固定的解析表示、数据驱动的线性表示（如PCA、Temporal-DCT）与非线性神经表示（自编码器）。评估手段包括率失真分析、序列建模诊断以及3,500次LIBERO rollout实验，并做种子对比与匹配种子消融。

Result: PCA的标称重建误差低于Temporal-DCT，但token序列可预测性更差，且三个策略训练种子下平均已见任务成功率低3.0个百分点，其中一个种子出现策略排序反转。种子42匹配消融中，自编码器进一步降低重建误差，却未获得最强策略，且对离散token扰动的敏感性更高。

Conclusion: 单一重建保真度无法可靠地选择自回归控制所需的动作表示，应联合评估几何保真度、序列可预测性、解码器稳定性与闭环性能。

Abstract: Discrete action tokenization is central to autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, yet action representations are often evaluated primarily through reconstruction fidelity. We ask which representation properties actually matter for closed-loop control by comparing fixed analytical, data-driven linear, and nonlinear neural representations under a unified tokenization interface. Across rate-distortion analysis, sequence-modeling diagnostics, and 3,500 LIBERO rollouts, representation rankings change with the evaluation criterion. PCA achieves lower nominal reconstruction error than Temporal-DCT, but produces less predictable token sequences and 3.0 percentage points lower mean seen-task success across three policy-training seeds, with the policy ordering reversing in one seed. In a matched seed-42 ablation, an autoencoder further reduces reconstruction error yet does not yield the strongest policy and exhibits greater sensitivity to discrete token perturbations. These findings show that reconstruction fidelity alone cannot reliably select action representations for autoregressive control, motivating joint evaluation of geometric fidelity, sequence predictability, decoder stability, and closed-loop performance.

</details>


### [164] [Sometimes You Gotta Run Before You Can Walk: Run-then-Walk Scheduling Strategy for VLM Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25831)
*Yuqi Ye,Shangkun Sun,Junhong Lin,Jiayi Zhao,Changhao Peng,Wei Zheng,Guoqing Liu,Tiesong Zhao,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Run-then-Walk 两阶段 GRPO 奖励调度策略，先探索高进度、再修复安全，在多个驾驶基准上提升性能并减少 40–50% 训练轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLM 的自动驾驶规划器在 GRPO 强化学习中出现两难：追求效率会带来不安全行为，而早期强加安全约束又导致过度保守，且均需较长训练。作者希望揭示不同 RL 阶段的行为规律并据此设计更优的奖励调度。

Method: 揭示两种 RL 机制——Run-GRPO（激进探索高进度）与 Walk-GRPO（在稳定进度下恢复安全）；据此提出 Run-then-Walk 两阶段奖励调度：Run 阶段专注进度以摆脱保守偏置、发现高进度模式，Walk 阶段引入端点与安全策略修复不安全行为。

Result: 在 NAVSIMv1、NAVSIMv2、Navhard、nuScenes 多个基准上，配合多种 VLM 规划器验证，驾驶性能提升且 RL 训练轮次比基线减少 40–50%。

Conclusion: 反向的“先进度后安全”调度同时克服了 Walk-first 的保守性与联合优化的不安全激进性，实现性能与收敛速度的双赢，为 VLM 驾驶规划器的 GRPO 训练提供了简单有效的范式。

Abstract: Recent VLM-based autonomous driving planners adopt GRPO-style reinforcement learning to optimize driving performance. However, existing GRPO recipes either optimize driving efficiency, risking progress-seeking but unsafe behavior, or enforce early safety constraints, leading to overly conservative behavior; both require lengthy training. To solve these problems, we first reveal two distinct RL regimes: a progress regime (Run-GRPO) that aggressively explores high progress, and a safety regime (Walk-GRPO) that restores safety under stable progress. Based on this finding, we propose $\textit{Run-then-Walk}$, a simple yet effective two-stage reward scheduling strategy for GRPO, achieving both better performance and faster convergence. Unlike one-stage RL, which may focus on progress, safety, or a mixture of both within a single training phase, this schedule explicitly separates progress discovery from safety repair. In the $\textit{Run}$ phase, we focus on progress, allowing the policy to escape the conservative bias and discover high-progress modes. In the subsequent $\textit{Walk}$ phase, we introduce endpoint and safety strategy to repair unsafe behaviors from the Run phase. This reversed schedule overcomes the conservatism of Walk-first methods and the unsafe progress-seeking of joint optimization. We validate it with various VLM-based planners on multiple benchmarks: NAVSIMv1, NAVSIMv2, Navhard, and nuScenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved driving performance while requiring 40--50\% fewer RL training epochs than the baselines.

</details>


### [165] [What is the Better Curriculum: Controller-Shaped Grasping Behavior for Contact Force-Sensitive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25887)
*Ziyan Feng,Zizhao Yuan,Yulong Fu,Yuxin He,Zhiyuan Zhang,Zhengjie Zhang,Jinni Zhou,Renjing Xu,Qiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出把触觉反馈用作"采集期教师"：用25Hz确定性触觉反射控制器生成力敏感抓取示范，再训练无触觉策略（ACT、π0.5），实现95%稳定抓取，但抗扰动仍依赖部署期控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚牛顿级接触力下易碎物体抓取中，人类手动遥操作存在延迟大、粒度粗的问题，难以稳定维持在极窄力区间；现有视觉-触觉策略学习多把触觉作为策略输入，未解决数据采集阶段的瓶颈。

Method: 以25Hz确定性触觉反射控制器作为采集期教师（collection-time teacher / reflex-shaped demonstrations），生成具有控制器塑造抓取行为特征的示范数据；在此数据上训练无触觉输入的策略（ACT、π0.5），并与视觉筛选的人工示范对比；另设部署期反射仲裁器（reflex arbiter）作为对照条件。

Result: ACT在标称塑料杯任务上复现教师的抓取力曲线，稳定抓取率95%，显著优于人工示范；π0.5在分布内稳定性提升，对未见纸质杯变体呈正向探索趋势；随机外力扰动下，仅策略的π0.5失败率45%，而部署期反射仲裁器保持全部抓取成功。

Conclusion: 触觉在力敏感操作中的新角色是"采集期教师"而非策略输入：它塑造示范中的抓取行为以服务无触觉策略学习；但扰动抑制仍依赖控制器，界定了无触觉策略的能力边界。

Abstract: How should a robot learn to manipulate objects so fragile that sub-Newton contact forces can cause irreversible damage? Existing visuo-tactile policy learning typically treats tactile sensing as an additional policy input. In direct-contact force-sensitive manipulation, however, the bottleneck can arise earlier, during data collection: manual gripper control is too delayed and coarse-grained to reliably maintain the narrow force range required for stable grasping. We therefore use a deterministic 25 Hz tactile reflex controller as a collection-time teacher, producing demonstrations with controller-shaped grasping behavior for tactile-free policy learning. On Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), policies trained from reflex-shaped demonstrations recover the teacher's grasping profile and achieve 95% stable grasps on the nominal plastic-cup task, substantially outperforming visually screened manual demonstrations. The same intervention improves in-distribution stability on $\pi_{0.5}$ and shows a favorable exploratory trend on an unseen paper-cup variant. Under randomized external disturbance, however, the reflex-data $\pi_{0.5}$ policy still fails in 45% of policy-only trials, whereas a deployment-time reflex arbiter retains all grasps. These results reveal a new role for tactile feedback in force-sensitive manipulation: rather than integrating tactile into the policy, we use it as a collection-time teacher that shapes grasping behavior in demonstrations for policy learning, while disturbance rejection remains controller-dependent, revealing the boundary of tactile-free policy.

</details>


### [166] [Robust Active-Perception Control for Global-State-Free Aerial-Ground Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25898)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Jiajun Yu,Baozhe Zhang,Pengxiang Zhou,Wentao Liu,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COPA框架：用单轴云台解耦相机光轴与无人机俯仰，结合TCN预测UGV短时运动与MPC联合优化，实现无全局状态的空地协同主动视觉跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地协同需实时相对状态信息，但依赖全局定位不可靠；固定相机在无人机姿态与视场约束冲突时易丢失移动UGV目标。因此需要一种无需全局状态、能主动保持目标可见性的相对位姿估计与控制方案。

Method: 在UGV附着的非惯性系中直接控制；采用单轴云台解耦相机光轴与UAV俯仰姿态；推导关联UAV运动、云台角与UGV运动的主动感知模型，映射到目标像平面状态；用TCN基于近期运动历史预测短时UGV加速度与角速度（无需全局状态测量）；MPC利用预测联合优化UAV与云台控制。

Result: 仿真表明COPA能持续保持目标可见性；消融实验证实TCN降低UGV运动切换期的峰值误差；真机实验中UGV加速度达3 m/s²、偏航角速度达1.0 rad/s时仍实现鲁棒跟踪。

Conclusion: COPA通过主动感知与预测式联合控制，在无全局定位条件下实现了鲁棒的空地协同相对位姿跟踪，具备仿真与真机验证。

Abstract: Aerial-ground cooperation requires real-time UAV--UGV relative-state information. Instead of maintaining global estimates for both robots, direct control in a UGV-attached non-inertial frame avoids reliance on global localization. Vision-based relative pose estimation with a passive marker offers a low-cost and effective solution. However, a fixed camera may lose sight of the moving UGV when the required UAV attitude conflicts with the field-of-view (FOV) constraint. To address this, we propose COPA, a robust active-perception framework for global-state-free aerial-ground cooperation. We use a single-axis gimbal to decouple the camera optical axis from the UAV pitch attitude. We derive an active-perception model that relates UAV motion, gimbal angle, and UGV motion to the target image-plane state.A Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) predicts short-horizon UGV acceleration and angular velocity from recent motion history without global-state measurements. The model predictive control (MPC) uses these predictions to jointly optimize UAV and gimbal control. Simulations show that COPA maintains continuous target visibility, while ablation studies confirm that the TCN reduces peak errors during UGV motion transitions. Real-world experiments with UGV accelerations up to 3m/s^2 and yaw rates up to 1.0rad/s demonstrate robust tracking.

</details>


### [167] [You Should Be Properly Scoring Your Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25900)
*Ola Rønning,Usama Saqib,Andrzej Wąsowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用严格适当评分规则（strictly proper scoring rules）替代RMSE等点度量来评估里程计的协方差质量，并给出无需真值的成对检验以诊断过度自信，发布开源框架smfeval，在LiDAR-惯性里程计四类滤波器上发现严重过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有里程计评估普遍采用RMSE等点度量，直接丢弃滤波器/平滑器已输出的协方差矩阵，既无法反映估计器是否"信任自己的输出"（过度自信者不会报告自身丢失），也会在不确定方向上对高误差施加不当惩罚。因此需要能同时评估估计值与不确定性的评分工具。

Method: 用严格适当评分规则（如负对数似然、能量分数）对"估计+协方差"联合评分：无协方差报告时退化为点度量，有协方差报告时可诊断协方差一致性；提出单边成对检验，无需真值即可暴露两估计器中至少一方存在过度自信；实现开源框架smfeval。

Result: 在四类地基LiDAR-惯性里程计滤波器上评估平移分量不确定性质量，全部存在过度自信，最差情形为"厘米级置信度对应公里级误差"；机理分析表明过度自信源于滤波器高估了LiDAR测量所携带的新信息量。

Conclusion: 里程计评估应从点度量转向严格适当评分规则，以纳入并检验协方差；smfeval及成对检验提供了可用的开源工具；LiDAR-惯性里程计的协方差标定（尤其对LiDAR新息信息量的建模）是待修复的关键问题。

Abstract: When we evaluate the performance of our odometry, it is common practice to score the estimated track against a ground truth. Unfortunately, scoring uses point metrics, such as the root mean square error, that ignore the covariance matrix which estimators like filters and smoothers already report. Using the covariance matters for two reasons. First, the covariance encodes the estimator's uncertainty, so it tells us whether the estimator trusts its own output. An overconfident estimator will not report itself lost. Second, the covariance weights the error in each direction of the estimate. Without the covariance, an estimator is unduly penalized for a high error in an uncertain direction. Instead of point metrics, we should use strictly proper scoring rules. These rules score the estimate together with its reported uncertainty. Strictly proper scoring rules recover the point metrics when no covariance is reported, and they diagnose covariance inconsistency when covariance is reported. Using a one-sided pairwise test, we show that two estimators can expose overconfidence in at least one of them without a ground truth. Strictly proper scoring rules and our pairwise test are available in our open-source framework smfeval. As a case study, we use smfeval to assess the uncertainty quality of the translational component of ground-based LiDAR-inertial odometry. Across four filters we find overconfidence - the worst case reports centimeter certainty with kilometer error. Knowing the filters are overconfident, we investigate the mechanism. The investigation traces overconfidence to filters crediting LiDAR measurements with more new information than they carry.

</details>


### [168] [Control Barrier Functions for Safe Free-Flying Robotic Spacecraft Operations in Tumbling Target Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25905)
*Alexander Meinert,Peter Stadler,Niklas Baldauf,Alen Turnwald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向翻滚目标捕获的自由飞行机器人航天器安全操作，提出模块化控制屏障函数（CBF）框架，将13-DoF系统分解为平移、姿态、机械臂子系统，各配轻量QP安全滤波器，在Basilisk仿真中验证接近走廊、碰撞避免、抓取等任务的安全性与实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESA最新安全近距离操作指南给出了安全区域与要求，但翻滚目标捕获涉及高自由度、动态耦合及多类约束（接近走廊、避碰、视线指向、角速度、关节限幅、执行器饱和），现有控制方法难以在保持模块化的同时提供系统级安全保证，需要将安全规范形式化为可计算约束。

Method: 将ESA安全区域/要求翻译为专用CBF；把13-DoF系统分解为平移、姿态、机械臂三个子系统，每个子系统配置一个安全滤波器，在轻量二次规划中以最小改动修正标称控制输入；通过把上游安全控制指令视为已知互联输入来处理子系统间动态耦合，从而在不破坏模块化的前提下支持系统级安全。

Result: 约束覆盖锥形接近走廊、碰撞避免区、姿态视线指向、角速度上限、机械臂关节限、连杆-基座避碰与执行器约束均被滤波器强制执行；在高保真天体动力学仿真器Basilisk中对在轨服务场景（最终接近、角速率同步、翻滚目标抓取）验证，蒙特卡洛仿真显示在不同翻滚角速率下兼具运行效率与操作安全性。

Conclusion: 模块化CBF安全滤波器可在多子系统耦合的高维航天器操作中实现系统级安全，且QP开销轻量、满足实时性，适合翻滚目标捕获这类时变高风险任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a modular control barrier function (CBF) framework for safe free-flying robotic spacecraft operations during tumbling target capture. Motivated by latest ESA guidelines for safe close proximity operations, safety zones and requirements are translated into dedicated CBFs. The 13-DoF system is decomposed into translational, attitude, and robotic subsystems, each equipped with a safety filter that minimally modifies nominal control inputs in a lightweight quadratic program. The filters enforce a conical approach corridor, collision avoidance zone, attitude line-of-sight pointing, angular velocity limits, robotic joint limits, link-base collision avoidance, and actuator constraints. Dynamic coupling between subsystems is handled by treating upstream safe control commands as known interconnection inputs in the downstream safety filters, preserving modularity while supporting system-level safety. The framework is validated in an on-orbit servicing scenario, including final approach, angular rate synchronization, and tumbling target grasping, using the high-fidelity astrodynamics simulator Basilisk. Monte Carlo simulation results demonstrate runtime efficiency and operational safety for various tumbling rates.

</details>


### [169] [Vision-based Underwater Formation Control With Input Saturations via Barrier Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25917)
*Nicola De Carli,João Zenário,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无通信的视觉编队控制框架，用重定中心障碍Lyapunov函数编码感知与避碰约束，结合指令滤波反步法扩展到二阶动力学，并通过二次规划处理执行器饱和，在Gazebo的SITL仿真中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动水下机器人编队控制需在无通信条件下依赖视觉，同时满足感知受限、避碰与输入饱和等多重约束，现有方法难以统一处理这些约束并保证安全性与可行性。

Method: 重定中心障碍Lyapunov函数（recentered BLF）编码感知域与避碰约束；指令滤波反步法（command-filtered backstepping）将设计扩展到二阶车辆动力学并应对输入饱和；约束控制目标由二次规划求解，显式考虑执行器限幅；保守感知域提供物理极限裕度并可按需自适应放宽，允许暂时越界。

Result: 在Gazebo中进行逼真的软件在环（SITL）仿真验证，展示了在感知约束、避碰与输入饱和下编队控制目标的可实现性与约束满足。

Conclusion: 该通信无关框架为受多约束的水下机器人视觉编队提供了可执行的约束控制方案；但仅由SITL仿真验证，缺乏实物实验，实际水动力与感知噪声下的鲁棒性仍待检验。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a communication-free framework for vision-based formation control of fully actuated underwater robots subject to sensing constraints, collision-avoidance requirements, and input saturations. Recentered barrier Lyapunov functions encode sensing and collision-avoidance constraints, while command-filtered backstepping extends the design to the second-order vehicle dynamics. The resulting control objective is enforced through a quadratic program that explicitly accounts for actuator limits. Conservative sensing domains provide margins from the physical limits and are adaptively relaxed when necessary, allowing temporary violation of the conservative bounds. The proposed approach is validated through realistic Software-in-the-Loop (SITL) simulations in Gazebo.

</details>


### [170] [Unsigned Distance Maps on 2D Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25932)
*Ricardo B. Sousa,Giorgio Grisetti,Héber Miguel Sobreira,Carlos André Silva,António Paulo Moreira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于无符号距离图（UDF）的2D点云配准方法，用预计算的距离场与空间导数将ICP的逐次最近邻搜索替换为O(1)查表，并在SE(2)流形上推导点到点、点到面误差，用Gauss-Newton求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D点云配准是移动机器人激光里程计与SLAM的核心环节，ICP虽广泛应用，但每次迭代都需通过最近邻搜索重算对应关系，计算开销大；而免对应方法多局限于scan-to-map对齐，缺乏通用的高效替代方案。

Method: 在离散网格上预计算到最近参考点的欧氏距离及其空间导数，构建无符号距离图，从而以O(1)查表取代逐迭代的最近邻搜索；在SE(2)流形上分别推导点到点与点到面误差形式，并采用Gauss-Newton优化求解。

Result: 在合成基准与真实IILABS 3D数据集上，预计算的点到点变体优于其解析对应版本，激光里程计漂移与点到面形式相当；预计算梯度在传感器噪声下起到对应关系正则化作用。

Conclusion: 距离图预计算可在保持精度的同时显著降低2D配准的迭代搜索成本，预计算梯度对噪声鲁棒，是激光里程计/SLAM中ICP的有效替代。

Abstract: 2D point cloud registration arises in laser odometry and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) for mobile robots. Iterative Closest Point (ICP) is one of the most widely used approaches. Still, its iterative procedure recomputes correspondences via nearest-neighbor search at every iteration, whereas correspondence-free alternatives focus on scan-to-map alignment. This paper proposes a 2D point cloud registration approach based on unsigned distance maps, precomputing the Euclidean distance to the nearest reference point, along with its spatial derivatives, over a discrete grid, replacing the per-iteration search with O(1) lookups. Moreover, point-to-point and point-to-plane error formulations are derived on the SE(2) manifold and solved via Gauss-Newton optimization. On a synthetic benchmark and the real-world IILABS 3D dataset, the precomputed point-to-point variant outperforms its analytical counterparts, achieving competitive laser-odometry drift compared to point-to-plane formulations, as the precomputed gradient regularizes correspondences in the presence of sensor noise.

</details>


### [171] [Destination Support Restoration for Finite-Set Multimodal Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25942)
*Fengrui Liu,Jiajun Peng,Duo Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DSR：一种后选择因果算子，通过整数目标计数与模态保护/再分配修复多模态行人轨迹预测中的目的地支持，保持集合大小与接口不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人围绕行人运行时常依赖有限的人类未来轨迹假设集，反复在线更新会使预测预算向主导目的地集中，导致合理替代目的地表示不足甚至缺失，从而在下游决策可用的有限表示中丢失多样性。

Method: 提出Destination Support Restoration（DSR），一种因果后选择算子：在每一步从观测前缀构建临时的目的地分层候选库，将候选证据转换为整数目标计数，保护活跃模态的代表，并把冗余的富余假设重新分配给缺失模态；保持恰好N个假设且最多替换⌈ρN⌉个条目；保护代表维持当前类别支持，沿袭感知粒子滤波同时保护存活的重采样祖先。

Result: 在完整3719条轨迹的Edinburgh协议、三种子设置下，N=64时MIF加权ADE与FDE分别降低13.36%与13.30%；与CLiFF、PPT、causal GDTS、Social Informer、PECNet配对集成时，所有评估对的两个指标均提升。

Conclusion: 有限集合的支持分配是预测侧有效的控制点，尤其当固定假设集作为下游系统接口时；DSR无需重训练主机预测器或增大集合规模即可修复目的地支持。

Abstract: Robots operating around pedestrians often reason over a finite set of predicted human futures. Repeated online updates can concentrate this limited prediction budget on dominant destinations and leave plausible alternatives underrepresented or absent, removing those alternatives from the finite representation available to downstream decision making. We introduce Destination Support Restoration (DSR), a causal post-selection operator that repairs destination support without retraining the host predictor or increasing the maintained set size. At a repair step, DSR evaluates a temporary destination-stratified candidate bank from the observed prefix, converts candidate evidence into integer target counts, protects representatives of active modes, and reallocates redundant surplus hypotheses to deficient modes. The maintained and returned sets retain exactly $N$ hypotheses, and DSR replaces at most $\lceil\rho N\rceil$ entries. Protected representatives preserve current categorical support; lineage-aware particle filters also preserve surviving resampling ancestors. Each replacement reduces the allocation mismatch to the evidence-driven target by one. On the complete 3,719-trajectory Edinburgh protocol over three seeds, DSR reduces MIF weighted ADE and FDE by 13.36% and 13.30% at $N=64$. Paired integrations with CLiFF, PPT, causal GDTS, Social Informer, and PECNet improve both metrics in every evaluated pair. These results show that finite-set support allocation is a useful prediction-side control point when a fixed hypothesis set serves as the interface to downstream systems.

</details>


### [172] [An Action Is Worth One Patch: Unified World-Action Modeling with PatchWAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25961)
*Tianheng Wang,Zhou Xie,Heng Jia,Jianhua Xu,Tong Zhang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PatchWAM，将连续动作通过固定映射Action-as-Patch表达为patches，使单一生成式视觉主干同时完成视觉预测与动作生成，无需独立动作头或专家模块，在LIBERO-Plus达91.8%、RoboTwin 2.0达96.12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型/视觉生成模型用于连续控制时，通常需额外引入可训练动作头或独立动作专家来桥接低维状态与高维视觉表征。作者质疑视觉预测与动作生成是否必须走分离计算路径，探索视觉主干既有容量能否在兼容表征下直接承担控制。

Method: 提出PatchWAM（Patch World-Action Model），核心是Action-as-Patch：用固定映射把连续动作视作另一类patch，与视觉patch统一在同一生成过程中建模，由单一模型同时预测机器人动作与后续场景，不设专用动作头或独立动作专家。

Result: 子采样训练窗口实验相比匹配的双专家控制方案取得提升；全数据设置并附加增强示范下，LIBERO-Plus成功率91.8%，RoboTwin 2.0成功率96.12%。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式新增能力并非必要，可通过既有主干继承；扩展生成式模型的约束在于新信号的接口表征形式，而非模型建模容量。

Abstract: Generative visual models offer a foundation for learning representations of physical dynamics, yet their extension to continuous control raises a fundamental question: do visual prediction and action generation require separate computational pathways? Existing approaches usually introduce trainable action heads or separate action experts to bridge low-dimensional states and high-dimensional visual representations. In this work, we explore whether the visual backbone's existing capacity can also support control when actions are expressed in a compatible representation. Thus, we introduce PatchWAM (Patch World-Action Model), which treats continuous actions as another type of patch through a fixed mapping called Action-as-Patch. This allows a single model to predict both how the robot should move and what the scene may look like afterward. Visual prediction and action generation become parts of the same generative process, without a dedicated action head or separate action expert. Experiments with subsampled training windows show gains over a matched dual-expert control, while benchmark evaluations reach 91.8% success rate on LIBERO-Plus and 96.12% on RoboTwin 2.0 in a full-data setting with additional augmented demonstrations. More broadly, the result suggests that capability need not be added where it can be inherited: the constraint on extending a generative backbone is the interface a new signal is written in, not the capacity to model it.

</details>


### [173] [Predict Before You Step: Auditable Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance under Sparse Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25969)
*Yuhui Mao,Fen Liu,Shenghai Yuan,Tianxin Hu,Ruimeng Liu,Rong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOOP：用循环占用预测（学习流场+可见性门warp）为腿部机器人提供50Hz局部避障策略，仿真头对头成功率57.1%，并在Unitree Go2上14.5ms/步实时运行、16次实机穿越零碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏路点导航下的腿足机器人需在部分、快速变化的LiDAR观测下避让动态障碍；现有反应式策略缺乏对未来的显式预测，且难以与冻结的运动控制器高频对接。

Method: LOOP（Latent-recurrent Occupancy rollOut Policy）以占用与自车速度历史为输入，用循环预测器通过学习的流场与可见性门对当前地图做warp，预测1秒未来的占用；再由地图导出特征与几何风险估计驱动速度选择，形成可检视、可替换的显式预测接口；适配器以50Hz连接冻结的低层运动控制器。

Result: 在encounter-synchronised Isaac Lab评测中，障碍速度2.5–3.2 m/s时头对头成功率57.1%，比重训练的反应式基线高8.2个百分点；与无rollout的BEV策略相比，预测分支增益较小且依赖场景，但在最高头对头速度下改善穿越成功率并降低跨训练种子方差；适配器在Unitree Go2上每步14.5 ms，16次实机穿越试验全部无碰撞。

Conclusion: 显式未来占用预测与速度选择的结合可提升动态避障性能并支持真实部署，但预测分支的收益有限且具场景依赖性。

Abstract: Legged robots under sparse waypoint guidance must avoid moving obstacles using partial, rapidly changing LiDAR observations. We present LOOP (Latent-recurrent Occupancy rollOut Policy), a local avoidance policy that connects sparse waypoint guidance to a frozen locomotion controller at 50 Hz.
