From occupancy and ego-velocity histories, a recurrent predictor forecasts future occupancy over a 1 s horizon by warping the current map with learned flow and visibility gates. These maps guide velocity selection through map-derived features and geometric risk estimates, providing an explicit interface for inspecting and replacing predictions. In encounter-synchronised Isaac Lab evaluations, LOOP achieves 57.1% head-on success at obstacle speeds of 2.5-3.2 m/s, exceeding a retrained reactive baseline by 8.2 percentage points. Comparisons with a rollout-free BEV policy show smaller, scenario-dependent gains from the prediction branch, including improved crossing success and reduced variability across training seeds at the highest head-on speeds. The adapter runs onboard a Unitree Go2 in 14.5 ms per step and completes all 16 real-world crossing trials without collision, demonstrating deployment feasibility.

</details>


### [174] [Safety-Constrained Model Predictive Control for an Omnidirectional Walking Assistive Robot Using Control Barrier Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25994)
*Andrea Fortuna,Marta Lorenzini,Elisa Motta,Alberto Ranavolo,Elena De Momi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将控制障碍函数(CBF)嵌入模型预测控制(MPC)的全向助行机器人(I-WANDER)控制框架，在优化能耗与柔顺人机协作的同时显式约束碰撞安全，并通过12人实验验证其优于变导纳控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 助行机器人需在提供物理柔顺助力、保障用户安全与支持之间取得平衡，传统导纳控制缺乏显式安全约束与能耗优化能力，故需一种兼顾安全性、能效与平滑协作的控制架构。

Method: 在全向助行机器人I-WANDER上，将CBF安全约束整合进MPC框架构成CB-MPC控制器；与变导纳控制器(AC)对照，12名健康受试者完成两类任务：U形走廊结构化导航，以及蒙眼单障碍避障（确保障碍突发）。对比能耗、机械功、运动平滑性与碰撞次数。

Result: CB-MPC在p<0.01水平下显著降低能耗与机械功，且不牺牲运动平滑性，同时减少障碍物碰撞次数。

Conclusion: CBF约束的MPC架构可同时提升助行机器人辅助的安全性与能效，表明其在机器人行走辅助中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Providing safe and effective mobility assistance plays a crucial role in restoring independence and enhancing the quality of life for individuals with motor impairments. In this context, robotic walking assistive devices have recently emerged as promising solutions to provide physically compliant interaction while ensuring user safety and support. This paper presents a novel control framework for an omnidirectional Walking Assistive Robot (I-WANDER) that integrates a Control Barrier Function (CBF) formulation into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) scheme to explicitly enforce collision-avoidance safety constraints while optimizing for energy efficiency and smooth human-robot collaboration. The method was experimentally evaluated with 12 healthy participants performing two different walking tasks using both the proposed CBF-based MPC controller (CB-MPC) and a variable admittance controller (AC). The first task involved structured navigation through a U-shaped corridor, whereas the second consisted of a single-obstacle avoidance task performed blindfolded to ensure the obstacle was unexpected. Comparative results show that the CB-MPC architecture significantly reduces energy consumption and mechanical work (p < 0.01) without compromising motion smoothness, while also decreasing the number of obstacle collisions. Overall, the findings highlight the potential of the proposed control architecture to enhance both safety and efficiency in robotic walking assistance.

</details>


### [175] [Manipulation with Stability Guarantees: Linear Deformable Objects with Non-negligible Physical Response Grasped at Multiple Location](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26004)
*Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出考虑物体动力学的闭环控制架构，将线性可变形物体（软管、线束）的操作建模为形状调节问题，通过离散应变参数化建立全动力学模型，将驱动坐标扩展到SE(3)形成欠驱动架构，并给出非线性反馈的稳态收敛条件；仿真与实时闭环实验验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作研究多局限于准静态、机械响应可忽略的轻量系统，忽视了软管、管道、线束等动力学不可忽略的一大类实际物体，缺乏能显式处理动力学的控制方法。

Method: 三项方法贡献：1）基于离散应变参数化的线性可变形物体全动力学模型；2）将驱动坐标概念扩展到SE(3)，构建结构化且本质欠驱动的控制架构；3）非线性反馈策略，给出收敛到目标构型的稳态显式条件；通过沿物体多个固定点施加力与力矩实现闭环控制。

Result: 在代表性操作任务上的大量仿真显示基于模型的表述带来性能提升；进一步通过带在线形状估计的实时闭环实现完成实验验证，证实了方法的实际可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模物体动力学并将操作重构为形状调节问题，为动力学不可忽略的线性可变形物体提供了可行的闭环控制框架，仿真与实物实验均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Most research on the manipulation of deformable objects focuses on lightweight systems with negligible mechanical response, effectively restricting attention to quasi-static regimes. This assumption excludes a broad class of practically relevant objects, such as hoses, pipes, and wiring harnesses, whose dynamics cannot be ignored during manipulation. In this work, we address this limitation by introducing a closed-loop control architecture that explicitly accounts for object dynamics and recasts manipulation as a shape-regulation problem. Control is achieved by modulating forces and torques applied at multiple fixed points along the object. This approach builds on three methodological contributions: a fully dynamic model of linear deformable objects based on discrete strain parameterizations; an extension of the notion of actuation coordinates to SE(3), yielding a structured and inherently underactuated control architecture; and nonlinear feedback strategies providing explicit conditions for steady-state convergence to desired configurations. Extensive simulations on representative manipulation tasks demonstrate the performance gains enabled by the proposed modelbased formulation. We finally validate the approach experimentally through a real-time closed-loop implementation with online shape estimation, confirming its practical feasibility and effectiveness

</details>


### [176] [Skytopia: Monocular Drone Navigation with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26007)
*Yuhang Zhang,Rangya Zhang,Yujing Shang,Zhuoyuan Yu,Weiying Wang,Steven Yang,Qingsong Yan,Chao Yan,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: skytopia：基于动作条件隐世界模型表示的单目无人机导航策略，配合3DGS训练平台，丢弃预测器后单一策略支持点目标、图像目标、无目标导航，并零微调部署真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目无人机导航在未知环境中缺乏深度与尺度线索；现有世界模型在每个控制步都执行预测并回馈到动作生成，推理开销大，且其价值被误置于"预测"而非"表示"。

Method: 提出动作条件隐世界模型：前向目标从意图运动预测下一观测的表示，逆向目标从预测的转移中恢复该运动；训练依托3D高斯泼溅平台。由于预测不进入动作生成，预测器可被丢弃，仅保留策略。

Result: 仿真中三类导航设定均超越所有基线，成功率57.8%/66.0%/49.0%；丢弃预测器节省59.4%推理成本；同一策略无需微调部署于真机，在室内、开阔室外与林地环境达成目标。

Conclusion: 策略真正需要的是产生预测所需的表示而非预测本身；该解耦设计在性能、推理效率与真机泛化上同时获益。

Abstract: Monocular drone navigation requires reaching a goal in an unseen environment from a single forward-facing camera, which offers few cues for depth and scale. World models address this by modelling how observations evolve under actions, but they are built to be executed: the prediction is produced at deployment and fed back into action generation at every control step. We argue that what a policy needs from a world model is not the prediction but the representation required to produce it: in flight the executed action explains almost all of the change between observations, so prediction reduces to reprojecting a static scene under a known displacement. We therefore introduce skytopia, a policy built on an action-conditioned latent world model, and the 3D Gaussian Splatting platform on which it is trained. A forward objective predicts the representation of the next observation from the intended motion, and an inverse objective recovers that motion from the predicted transition. Because the prediction never reaches action generation, the predictor is discarded and one policy serves point-goal, image-goal, and goal-free navigation. Simulation experiments show that skytopia outperforms every baseline under all three specifications, attaining 57.8%, 66.0%, and 49.0% success rate, while discarding the predictor removes 59.4% of the inference cost. The same policy is subsequently deployed on a physical drone without fine-tuning and reaches goals in indoor, open outdoor, and woodland environments.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Intent-Aware Human-Robot Teaming: A Platform for Search-and-Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26051)
*Rohith Prem Maben,Ayesha Jena,Björn Olofsson,Stefan Reitmann,Jacek Malec,Rogier Woltjer,Elin Anna Topp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一个人机异构自主智能体交互平台，用于研究操作员行为、意图推断与决策，并以搜救任务为例验证自主系统对操作员感知、决策与行动的支持程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂动态环境下，人机协作中操作员行为建模与意图推断困难；异构自主智能体与人的协同缺乏可实验、可复现的交互研究平台。

Method: 构建交互平台，集成state-of-the-art的意图推断与决策框架，支持对操作员行为的观测研究；在搜救场景中开展人机协作实验。

Result: 展示了自主系统在搜救操作中能够在多大程度上支持操作员的感知、决策与行动（摘要未给出量化指标、用户研究规模或基线对比）。

Conclusion: 该平台可作为研究人机协作、操作员行为与意图推断的实验载体；但摘要层面缺乏方法细节与实证数据。

Abstract: We investigate the challenges of enabling effective collaboration between human operators and heterogeneous autonomous agents in complex, dynamic environments by developing an interaction platform that allows study of operator behavior and supports intent inference and decision-making using state-of-the-art frameworks. We demonstrate the extent to which the operator's perception, decisions, and actions could be supported by autonomous systems during search-and-rescue operations with our platform.

</details>


### [178] [StrataVLA: Hierarchical and Efficient 3D Geometric Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26071)
*Jin Cui,Zhaoyu Pu,Botao Cai,Jun Ye,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StrataVLA：插件式分层几何接地框架，用冻结几何基础模型+稀疏层间Geometry Adapter，通过跨注意力向VLA骨干注入几何证据，并用任务感知路由与LRU特征缓存降低几何模型调用达88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽继承大规模视觉-语言预训练的语义先验，但3D空间感知不足，限制机器人操作能力。现有方案要么依赖显式深度/点云输入，要么把几何压缩为训练期监督，或仅在模型输入或动作专家处注入几何，导致视觉-语言骨干无法持续访问任务相关空间信息。

Method: （1）冻结的几何基础模型从RGB观测中提取共享几何特征；（2）稀疏的、层特定的Geometry Adapter让选定骨干深度的视觉表征通过跨注意力检索相关几何证据；（3）为便于推理期使用几何，引入任务感知路由与LRU特征缓存，利用操作任务中的时间冗余。

Result: 在LIBERO、SimplerEnv及真机操作上相较强VLA基线取得一致性提升；LIBERO各套件平均成功率98.53%，几何模型调用减少最多88%。

Conclusion: 分层几何注入是实现空间接地机器人控制的有效且高效途径，可在不改变VLA主干的前提下低成本提升3D空间感知能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit strong semantic priors from large-scale vision-language pretraining, yet remain limited in robotic manipulation by insufficient 3D spatial awareness. Existing approaches either require explicit depth or point-cloud inputs, compress geometry into training-time supervision, or inject it only at the model input or action expert, leaving the vision-language backbone without persistent access to task-relevant spatial information. We introduce StrataVLA, a plug-and-play framework for hierarchical geometric grounding. A frozen geometry foundation model extracts shared geometric features from RGB observations, while sparse, layer-specific Geometry Adapters allow visual representations at selected backbone depths to retrieve relevant geometric evidence through cross-attention. To make inference-time geometry practical, StrataVLA further combines task-aware routing with an LRU feature cache that exploits temporal redundancy during task manipulation. Experiments on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over strong VLA baselines. StrataVLA achieves 98.53% average success on LIBERO suites while reducing geometry-model invocations by up to 88%, establishing hierarchical geometry injection as an effective and efficient way to achieve spatially grounded robotic control.

</details>


### [179] [Situation Aware Locomotion for Dual Mobile Cobots in Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26083)
*William Moraes,Igor Nunes,Ahilen Mazondo,Sebastian Barcelona,Hiago Sodre,Pablo Moraes,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向共享工业环境中两台协作移动机器人的情境感知（situation aware）运动决策框架，以感知-理解-预测三层情境感知选择安全运动动作，仿真中达成100%任务成功率并消除共享区冲突与安全停机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双机器人共享工业环境中，独立决策与固定优先级策略难以处理共享区占用、载荷与机械臂状态变化及短时域冲突，易产生冲突与安全停机，因此需要将情境感知引入运动决策。

Method: 将情境感知解构为感知、理解、预测三层；以机器人位姿、载荷状态、机械臂状态、共享区占用、障碍状态及预测的机器人间冲突作为决策输入，据此选择安全运动动作。在仿真中实现，场景匹配4m×4m物理测试区，并与独立策略、固定优先级两个基线在多次试验与随机种子下对比。

Result: 情境感知方法在所有场景下任务成功率100%，两个基线总体成功率均为33.3%；该方法完全消除共享区冲突与安全停机，保持最大平均最小机器人间距，并取得最低平均完成时间。

Conclusion: 结合载荷状态、机械臂状态、共享区推理与短时域冲突预测的情境感知可有效提升双机器人运动性能，但结论目前仅基于仿真，尚需物理平台验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a situation aware locomotion framework for two mobile collaborative robots operating in shared industrial environments. The proposed method models situation awareness through perception, comprehension, and projection to support locomotion decisions. Robot pose, load state, manipulator state, shared zone occupancy, obstacle state, and predicted inter robot conflict were used to select safe locomotion actions. The framework was implemented in simulation and evaluated in simulated industrial scenarios designed to match a feasible 4 m by 4 m physical test area. The proposed method was compared with two other baselines over multiple trials and randomized seeds. The results show that the situation aware method achieved 100% task success across all scenarios, while the independent and fixed priority baselines each achieved 33.3% overall success. The proposed method eliminated shared zone conflicts and safety stops, maintained the largest average minimum inter robot distance, and completed the tasks with the lowest average completion time. These results indicate that situational awareness can improve the locomotion of dual robots by combining load state, manipulator state, reasoning about the shared zone, and prediction of short-horizon conflicts.

</details>


### [180] [Vision-Language Models as copilots for Autonomous UAV Navigation: Analysis of Latency and Reliability in Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26084)
*Hiago Sodre,Sebastian Barcelona,Vincent Sandin,Pablo Moraes,Ahilen Mazondo,Igor Nunes,William Moraes,André Kelbouscas,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FSM-VLM混合控制架构用于无GPS环境下的无人机自主导航，FSM负责底层物理控制，VLM异步担任高层语义寻路；在SITL仿真中测评三个不同参数规模模型，区分格式层语法错误与逻辑层语义幻觉，结论是参数规模而非纯延迟是VLM安全集成的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM虽赋予无人机语义推理能力，但实时闭环导航同时要求低推理延迟与对结构化飞行指令的严格遵循，二者存在冲突；需要厘清VLM集成中的失败模式究竟源于延迟还是模型能力本身。

Method: 混合FSM-VLM控制架构：确定性有限状态机承担低层物理控制，VLM作为异步副驾驶执行高层语义寻路；在软件在环（SITL）仿真中对比三种参数规模模型，在正常与退化两种场景下量化格式层语法错误与逻辑层语义幻觉。

Result: 实验表明参数规模扩展（而非单纯推理延迟）是VLM安全且兼容地集成到自主飞行中的首要瓶颈；同时呈现出格式合规性与语义正确性两类错误的不同分布特征。

Conclusion: VLM接入无人机自主导航的关键约束在于模型容量与结构化指令遵循能力，异步架构可缓解延迟但无法替代参数规模带来的语义可靠性；为后续VLM-UAV闭环系统的设计提供了错误分类与评估范式。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offers unprecedented semantic reasoning capabilities. However, real-time closed-loop navigation requires not only low inference latency but also obedience to structured flight commands. This paper proposes a hybrid FSM-VLM control architecture for UAVs in GPS-free environments. The system combines a deterministic Finite State Machine (FSM) for low-level physical control with an asynchronous VLM copilot for high-level semantic pathfinding. We evaluate three models with different parameter scales in a Software-In-The-Loop (SITL) simulation. The framework isolates and measures syntax errors at the format level versus semantic hallucinations at the logic level in a normal and degraded scenario. This study demonstrates that parameter scaling, and not pure latency, remains the primary bottleneck for the safe and compatible integration of VLM into autonomous flights.

</details>


### [181] [Acoustic Ellipses: Bio-Inspired Omnidirectional Echolocation in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems using Frequency Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26085)
*Petras Swissler,Lindsay Burke,Julia Hyland Bruno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出仿鸟类鸣叫的协作智能体环境障碍物测绘方法：静止源智能体发射调频chirp，运动听者智能体观测直射与反射信号，利用"频率间隙"定义候选反射椭圆并结合2D累积滤波定位障碍物，经仿真与户外实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受鸟类的飞行与鸣叫启发，探索一种低成本、可用于群体机器人系统的环境测绘方法，使协作智能体能够有效识别环境中的障碍物。

Method: 静止源智能体发射调频chirp信号，一个或多个运动听者智能体观测直射与反射信号；利用直射与反射chirp信号间的"频率间隙"定义候选反射椭圆，输入2D累积滤波器以找出潜在反射密度最高的位置；用定制音频仿真器研究障碍物数量、采样率、路径曲率的影响，并比较双听者配置下的协作运动策略。

Result: 仿真分析了单听者场景下障碍物数量、采样率与路径曲率对障碍物识别精度的影响，评估了双听者的不同协作运动策略；并在户外公园环境中用现成硬件完成实地实验，验证了利用频率间隙识别障碍物的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法为群体机器人系统提供了一种低成本环境测绘的基础方案，具备实际可部署的初步可行性。

Abstract: Inspired by the flight and song of birds, we propose an approach that enables cooperating agents to effectively identify obstacles in their environment by having a stationary source agent emit a frequency-modulated chirp while one or more listener agents observe the direct and reflected signals while in motion. We present a novel mapping approach that exploits the ``frequency gap'' between direct and reflected chirp signals to define candidate reflection ellipses, which are then fed into a 2D accumulation filter to identify locations with the highest density of potential reflections. We demonstrate this work first with simulation results derived from an efficient, bespoke audio simulator, examining the effect of obstacle count, sampling rate, and path curvature on the ability to accurately identify environmental obstacles for a one-listener scenario. We then examine different cooperative motion strategies for two-listener configurations. Finally, we validate the real-world viability of this approach through field experiments in an outdoor park setting to demonstrate the ability to identify frequency gaps using off-the-shelf hardware. Our results provide a foundation for a low-cost approach to environmental mapping in swarm robotic systems.

</details>


### [182] [GDLAM: Group-Disentangled Latent Action Model for Highly Disentangled Embodied Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26118)
*Jiarui Yang,Jiawei Li,Jiale Zhang,Hang Guo,Wen Huang,Maowei Hu,Tao Dai,Shu-Tao Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分组解耦潜在动作模型 GDLAM，将潜在动作码按构造分解为 N 组（独立变分瓶颈 + 空间门控路由），配合互斥性、组/门稀疏性、静动正交等信息几何目标，使各组因果独立而非仅去相关，并迁移到世界模型与 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作模型（LAM）把相机运动、物体动力学、交互事件等异质视觉变化源压缩进单一潜在向量，导致表征纠缠、语义结构弱，从而限制世界模型的可控性与 VLA 策略泛化能力。作者希望在不依赖动作标签的视频预训练中获得结构化解耦的潜在动作码。

Method: GDLAM 在构造上把潜在动作码因子化为 N 个组，每组有独立变分瓶颈与空间门控路由通路；训练目标为一组信息几何约束：组间互斥性、组稀疏与门稀疏、静态-动态正交性，目标是让各组的因果效应相互独立而非仅仅统计去相关。通过单组干预实验与无标签解耦指标（Modularity、MIG、DCI）评估，并用三种互信息估计器与线性探针检验动作信息量，最后迁移到世界模型与 VLA 策略两类下游任务。

Result: 单组干预仅改变该组、其余组保持不变，验证因果独立性；无标签解耦指标 Modularity、MIG、DCI 大幅优于强非结构化 LAM 基线。分组编码在分布内与分布外均比单体基线更具动作信息量（三种互信息估计器 + 线性探针）。世界模型预训练取得更优的 rollout 保真度与动作跟随能力；VLA 策略在多个仿真基准与真实机器人操作任务上显著提升成功率。

Conclusion: 结构化分组解耦不会牺牲动作信息，反而提升信息量，可作为可复用的预训练表征迁移至世界模型与 VLA 策略，说明解耦潜在动作是具身智能预训练的有价值范式。

Abstract: Latent action models (LAMs) learn action-related representations from action-free videos via self-supervised future prediction, offering a scalable paradigm for embodied intelligence pretraining. However, existing LAMs collapse heterogeneous sources of visual change, including camera motion, object dynamics, and interaction events, into a single latent vector, resulting in entangled representations with limited semantic structure and consequently restricting world model controllability and VLA policy generalization. We introduce the Group-Disentangled Latent Action Model (GDLAM), a latent action model whose code is factorized by construction into N groups, each with an independent variational bottleneck and a spatially gated routing pathway, and trained with a set of information-geometric objectives: mutual exclusivity, group and gate sparsity, and static-dynamic orthogonality, that make the groups mutually causally distinct rather than merely decorrelated. Quantitatively, intervening on any single group changes only that group and leaves the others intact, and GDLAM improves label-free disentanglement metrics, including Modularity, MIG, and DCI, by wide margins over a strong unstructured LAM. Notably, this factorization is not at the expense of action information: across three mutual-information estimators and a linear probe, the grouped code is more informative than monolithic baselines both in- and out-of-distribution. As supporting evidence that the disentangled code is a reusable pretraining currency, we further transfer it to two downstream regimes: (1) World Modeling: World models pretrained with GDLAM achieve superior rollout fidelity and action-following capability compared with SOTA baselines. (2) VLA Policies: Pretraining with GDLAM substantially improves task success rates over previous methods across multiple simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic manipulation tasks

</details>


### [183] [Design and Implementation of an Ultra-Low-Cost Wall-Climbing Robot for Infrastructure Crack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26130)
*Mrinmoy Modak,Supreyo Chakravorty Pretom,Shourv Tarafder,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 低成本爬壁机器人+两阶段深度学习裂缝检测（YOLOv8定位、CNN/EfficientNet-B0分类），约25美元，Web应用手机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混凝土基础设施裂缝检测常受预算、人工强度与安全风险限制，尤其发展中国家缺乏可负担的自动化检测手段，因此需要低成本、可扩展的机器人方案。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段YOLOv8从视频帧检测并定位墙体区域，裁剪边界框；第二阶段在裁剪区域上用CNN或EfficientNet-B0做二分类裂缝检测。硬件采用风扇负压吸附、四轮滑移转向、ESP32-CAM实时采集，轻量3D打印底盘，漏斗形机身增强负压并降低44%占空比。

Result: 系统可爬行墙面与天花板并实时采图分析，整体成本约25美元，含轻量Web应用支持手机端控制；漏斗形机身提升负压生成、占空比降低44%，显著降低功耗。

Conclusion: 低成本硬件与深度学习流水线结合，提供了面向实时基础设施巡检的可扩展、可及方案，适合预算受限地区。

Abstract: Crack detection is a crucial process to ensure the safety and longevity of buildings and other infrastructure. In this paper, we developed a low-cost, automated crack detection robot that leverages CNN, EfficientNet-B0, and YOLOv8 for efficient identification of cracks in concrete surfaces with a curated crack image dataset introduced to support training and evaluation. YOLOv8's real-time object detection enhances crack localization, while CNN and EfficientNet-B0 provide binary classification, ensuring high precision and recall. The system consists of two stages. In the first stage, YOLOv8 detects and localizes wall regions from the video frame, and the bounding boxes are cropped. The second stage performs crack detection using one of three models by analyzing the cropped regions. Cost-effective approaches are also taken for robot design. The robot features a fan-based negative pressure adhesion system, a 4-wheeled skid-steering drive, and an ESP32-CAM for real-time image capture. Its lightweight 3D-printed chassis ensures stability, allowing it to navigate both walls and ceilings while capturing images for crack analysis. Unlike conventional wall-climbing robot designs, this robot incorporates a funnel-shaped body that enhances negative pressure generation and achieves a 44% reduction in duty cycle, significantly lowering power consumption. By combining low-cost hardware with a deep learning pipeline, our system provides a scalable, efficient, and accessible solution for real-time infrastructure inspection at an approximate total cost of $25, with a lightweight web application enabling smartphone-based control. This affordability makes the system more suitable for the developing world, where infrastructure inspection is often limited by budget constraints, labor intensity, and safety risks.

</details>


### [184] [StepTrigger: Contact-State-Triggered Backdoor Attacks on VLM-Powered Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26131)
*Jiageng Zhang,Doniyorkhon Obidov,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StepTrigger 后门攻击：以 Unitree Go1 四足机器人足地压力/接触模式为触发信号，攻击 VLM 高层规划器，并通过多模态机器人状态学习选择性后门策略以区分良性压力异常与密集地形接触触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM/VLM 作为机器人高层规划器时存在新的后门面：被污染规划器平时表现正常，仅在隐藏触发下改变目标选择。已有攻击依赖语言提示、可见物体、场景语义或动作历史序列等触发，而本体感知与接触通道（压力、足地接触）这一模态尚被忽视，现有防御也主要针对语言、视觉或动作历史。

Method: StepTrigger 以四足机器人走过密集地形块时产生的压力与足-地接触模式作为触发，而非提示词或可视标记；针对接触信号的噪声性和良性运动中同样出现压力异常的问题，从多模态机器人状态中学习选择性后门策略，将偶发压力事件作为良性样本、密集地形接触作为投毒样本进行分类式条件触发。

Result: 分层离线评测中：干净行为保持 98.75%、误触发拒绝 92.50%、真触发激活 76.25%、整体解析行为准确率 89.17%，表明触发具有选择性（良性压力事件不被激活）。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了本体感知与接触通道中一类未被语言、视觉或动作历史防御覆盖的后门攻击面；真触发激活率（76.25%）仍明显低于干净保持率，说明该通道触发可靠性有限，也提示需要跨模态（含接触/本体感知）的后门检测与防御。

Abstract: Large language models and vision-language models are increasingly used as high-level planners in robotic systems, using task goals and sensor summaries to select navigation or manipulation actions. This creates a new backdoor surface: a compromised planner can behave normally in most runs, yet change its target selection when a hidden trigger is present. Prior attacks on LLM-powered or embodied agents mainly rely on triggers that appear in language, camera-visible objects, scene semantics, or specific sequences of past actions. This paper presents StepTrigger, a contact-state-triggered backdoor attack for VLM-powered legged robots. The trigger is not a prompt token or a visible marker. It is produced by pressure and foot-ground contact patterns that arise when a Unitree Go1 quadruped walks across a dense terrain patch. Unlike conventional visual or textual triggers, contact signals are inherently noisy and may also arise during benign locomotion. To avoid treating every pressure anomaly as a trigger, StepTrigger learns a selective backdoor policy from multimodal robot state, using incidental pressure events as benign examples and dense-patch contacts as poisoned examples. In a stratified offline evaluation, the trained planner achieved 98.75% clean behavior preservation, 92.50% false-trigger rejection, 76.25% true-trigger activation, and 89.17% overall parsed behavior accuracy. These results reveal a backdoor surface in proprioceptive and contact channels that is not captured by defenses focused only on language, vision, or action history.

</details>


### [185] [Toward Self-Repairing Ubiquitous Robots Using Goal-Oriented Agentic AI in Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26155)
*Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向机器人维护维修的目标导向智能体架构，分离目标分解、持久状态跟踪、战略目标管理与实时对话执行，在真实硬件维修任务中对20名用户验证，19人完成任务（95%），系统被评为有用且胜任，但对物理交互相比在线基线的社会临场感与信任/胜任感显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无传统视觉界面的泛在机器人系统依赖自然语言交互，非专家用户需要完成技术性维修任务，现有系统缺乏对目标分解、状态持久化与对话策略的统一支持。

Method: goal-oriented agentic AI architecture，模块化拆分为预交互目标分解、持久状态跟踪、战略目标管理、实时对话执行；在物理硬件维修场景中开展20人用户实验，并与先前在线基线对比（统计检验）。

Result: 19/20完成维修任务（95%完成率）；用户评价系统有帮助且胜任；智能体对元查询、语码转换等对话偏移保持鲁棒；与在线基线相比，物理交互显著降低社会临场感（p=.0005）及信任与胜任感（p=.037），但感知有用性仍高。

Conclusion: 该智能体架构可支持非专家用户完成真实机器人维修任务，但对物理情境下的社会临场感与信任构建提出额外设计挑战，需针对性改进人机信任与交互体验。

Abstract: Ubiquitous robotic systems often lack traditional visual interfaces, making natural language interaction important for maintenance and repair. This paper presents a goal-oriented agentic AI architecture that enables non-expert users to complete technical repair tasks through situated dialogue. The architecture separates pre-interaction goal decomposition, persistent state tracking, strategic goal management, and real-time conversational execution. We evaluated the system in a physical hardware repair task with twenty participants. Nineteen participants completed the task, corresponding to a 95% completion rate. Participants rated the system as helpful and competent, and the agent remained robust to conversational diversions such as meta-queries and code-switching. A comparison with a prior online baseline showed that physical interaction significantly reduced perceived social presence, (p=.0005), and trust and competence, (p=.037), while perceived helpfulness remained high.

</details>


### [186] [Silent Sabotage: Internal State Triggered Backdoor Attacks on LLM-Powered Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26184)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Shivayogi Akki,Tan Chen,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首次系统研究LLM驱动机器人控制器的"历史触发"后门攻击：触发器是机器人自身过去动作的罕见序列，而非外部刺激，攻击成功率接近100%且极难检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被整合进机器人控制系统带来复杂推理与规划能力，但也引入未被充分探索的安全风险。现有LLM后门攻击均依赖外部触发（特定词、视觉目标、环境状态），忽略了触发器内生于智能体自身运行逻辑这一更隐蔽的漏洞类别。

Method: 攻击者通过操纵LLM机器人控制器的指令，植入隐蔽后门；触发条件为机器人自身历史动作的特定稀有序列（history-based trigger）。后门在常规运行中保持休眠以维持任务效用，一旦历史动作序列满足条件即激活，诱导恶意行为（如完全停止或碰撞）。在仿真环境中用多种机器人本体与多种LLM进行验证。

Result: 该基于历史的攻击高度有效，攻击成功率接近完美，同时异常难以被检测；休眠期不影响机器人正常任务性能。

Conclusion: 揭示了自主系统中一个关键且此前未被处理的漏洞——内部状态可被用作后门触发器，强调安全机制必须考虑智能体的内部状态与历史轨迹。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into robotic control systems is enabling a new generation of autonomous agents capable of complex reasoning and planning. While this paradigm shift accelerates progress, it also introduces novel security risks that remain largely unexplored. Current research into LLM backdoors has focused on attacks triggered by external stimuli, such as specific words, visual objects, or environmental states. These attacks, while potent, overlook a more insidious class of vulnerability where the trigger is internal to the agent's own operational logic. This paper presents the first comprehensive study of history-based backdoor attacks on LLM-powered robotic systems. We demonstrate that an attacker can embed a stealthy backdoor into an LLM-based robot controller by manipulating its instructions. This backdoor is triggered not by an external cue, but by a specific, rare sequence of the robot's own past actions. It remains dormant during normal operation, preserving the robot's utility, but can be activated to induce a malicious behavior, such as a complete stop or a collision. Our experiments, conducted in a simulated environment with a variety of robots and LLMs, show that this history-based attack is highly effective, achieving a near-perfect attack success rate while remaining exceptionally difficult to detect. These findings reveal a critical and previously unaddressed vulnerability in autonomous systems and underscore the urgent need for security measures that account for an agent's internal state.

</details>


### [187] [Manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects Using Model Predictive Path Integral Control with Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26238)
*Lukas Zeh,Johannes Meiwaldt,Zexu Zhou,Armin Lechler,Alexander Verl*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于biLSTM学习的DLO动力学模型与MPPI模型预测控制的DLO操作框架，仿真与实验验证了操作精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（电缆等）具有无穷自由度与非线性动力学，自动化操作难度大，需要数据驱动的建模与优化控制方案。

Method: 用MuJoCo生成的合成数据训练双向LSTM网络建模DLO动力学，并采用Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）模型预测控制策略实现操作。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明该方法能实现准确且高效的DLO操作。

Conclusion: 学习式动力学建模结合MPPI控制是DLO操作的有效途径，具备向真实场景迁移的潜力。

Abstract: The manipulation of Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as cables poses a significant challenge for automation due to their infinite degrees of freedom and non-linear dynamics. In this paper we present a machine learning based optimal control approach for the manipulation of DLOs. This approach is divided into two main components: modeling and control. For modeling the dynamics of the DLO, we propose a learning based approach using a bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (biLSTM) network. The biLSTM network is trained on synthetic data generated by the MuJoCo physics engine. For manipulating the DLO, a model predictive control strategy that employs Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is selected. The proposed approach is evaluated through simulation and experiments. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in achieving accurate and efficient manipulation of DLOs.

</details>


### [188] [High-Bandwidth Biomimetic Finger for Tactile-Transparent Remote Texture Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26256)
*Shuang Yang,Fuyuan Liu,Yitian Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 比较仿生指尖（多层力学梯度+指纹形态+IMU振动采集）三种变体与人手指尖，建立"指尖设计→信号特征→触觉透明性"传递链，给出面向遥操作的应用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人遥操作需要高保真触觉反馈，但仿生指尖的机械设计如何影响远程振动触觉渲染的"透明性"（即还原真实触感的能力）尚不清楚，缺乏设计指导。

Method: 设计复现人手指多层力学梯度与指纹形貌的仿生指尖，内置高灵敏IMU采集纹理诱发的振动信号，用于远端振动触觉渲染；对三种变体与人手指尖进行时域/频域分析，并开展覆盖三个感知维度的用户实验。

Result: 多层力学梯度在信号强度与纹理特征表征上提升最明显；较硬的表皮层虽放大振动强度却牺牲特征表征。各感知维度依赖不同信号属性：粗糙度对应能量尺度、颗粒感对应频谱形状、重复性对应特征峰表征。

Conclusion: 所搭建的"指尖设计—信号特征—触觉透明性"传递链为遥操作中面向具体任务的指尖选型提供了设计原则。

Abstract: High-fidelity tactile feedback is essential for robotic teleoperation, enabling precise manipulation and critical decision-making. While biomimetic fingertip sensors can capture surface-texture features, how their design shapes the tactile transparency of rendered feedback remains poorly understood. This paper presents a biomimetic fingertip replicating the human finger's multilayer mechanical gradient and fingerprint morphology, with an embedded high-sensitivity inertial measurement unit capturing texture-induced vibrations for remote vibrotactile rendering. Three variants are compared with the human fingertip through temporal and spectral analyses and a user study spanning three perceptual dimensions, establishing a transmission chain from fingertip design through signal characteristics to tactile transparency. The multilayer mechanical gradient yields the clearest improvements in signal intensity and texture-feature representation, whereas a stiffer skin layer amplifies vibration intensity at the expense of feature representation. The perceptual dimensions draw on distinct signal attributes: roughness on energy scaling, granularity on spectral shape, and repetitivity on characteristic-peak representation. These findings offer design principles for application-specific fingertips in robotic teleoperation.

</details>


### [189] [Multi-Axis Selective Decoupling Framework for Free-Floating Space Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26267)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多轴选择性解耦框架，仅沿任务关键方向消除动量传递，放宽零空间约束，在保证基座不受扰的同时保留工作空间自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由漂浮空间机械臂存在动力学耦合，传统无扰操作需对所有旋转轴施加零空间投影约束，导致约束过强、工作空间大幅缩减。

Method: 构建方向约束的子耦合矩阵，仅在任务关键方向上零化动量传递，实现多轴选择性解耦，从而放宽零空间投影约束。

Result: 数值仿真表明，该框架在数学上消除了目标轴方向的扰动，同时保留其余自由度，机械臂可成功完成任务空间操作。

Conclusion: 按方向选择性施加解耦约束可在基座无扰与工作空间保留之间取得更优折衷，为自由漂浮空间机械臂提供更灵活的控制方案。

Abstract: Free-floating space manipulator systems face operational challenges due to dynamic coupling. While traditional reactionless manipulation eliminates base disturbances, enforcing the null-space projections across all rotational axes imposes excessive constraints on the system and drastically reduces the available workspace. To address this limitation, this work proposes a multi-axis selective decoupling framework that nullifies momentum transfer exclusively along mission-critical directions. By leveraging a directionally constrained sub-coupling matrix, the framework relaxes the null-space constraints. Numerical simulations confirm that this approach mathematically eliminates constrained directional disturbances along the targeted axes while preserving the remaining degrees of freedom, enabling the manipulator to execute task-space operations successfully.

</details>


### [190] [RoboTwin-Phys: Do WAMs and VLAs Understand the Physical World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26292)
*Jiaqi Zhang,Feng Ye,Mingjia Yang,Zhihong Chen,Mingkang Xiang,Xinglin Yao,Yanbin Li,Siwei Ma,Chuanmin Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboTwin-Phys 物理条件多样化机器人操作基准，连续变化 13 种物理属性并开源 5000+ 专家演示与真值物理参数，揭示 WAM/VLA 在物理条件扰动下存在显著鲁棒性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模仿真基准虽在物体外观、场景布局、视觉观测上做了随机化，但底层物理参数（质量、摩擦、关节动力学等）固定不变，导致真实世界中重要的物理变异性未被评测，策略鲁棒性被高估。

Method: 构建 RoboTwin-Phys 基准，将物理条件多样性作为显式评测维度，在物理合理范围内连续变化 13 种物理属性，提供统一评测设置；同时发布超 5000 条带真值物理参数的专家演示，支持物理属性估计、条件感知建模与物理条件化策略训练。

Result: 对代表性 WAM 与 VLA 模型的评测显示明显鲁棒性缺口：在既有视觉与布局随机化下仍有效的模型，在物理条件变化下性能可显著退化。

Conclusion: RoboTwin-Phys 为系统度量并改进机器人操作对物理条件多样性的鲁棒性提供了基准、数据与评测协议，指出物理条件维度是当前策略评测中被忽视的关键方向。

Abstract: Physical-condition diversity is largely missing from current benchmarks for robot manipulation. While large-scale simulation benchmarks increasingly incorporate variations in object appearance, scene layout, and visual observations, they typically keep the underlying physical parameters fixed. As a result, important sources of real-world variability, such as changes in mass, friction, and joint dynamics, remain largely untested. We introduce RoboTwin-Phys, a physics-diverse benchmark that treats physical-condition diversity as an explicit dimension of robot manipulation evaluation. The benchmark continuously varies 13 physical attributes within physically plausible ranges, providing a unified setting for evaluating policies across diverse physical operating conditions. We further release more than 5,000 expert demonstrations with ground-truth physical parameters, enabling physical-attribute estimation, condition-aware modeling, and physics-conditioned policy training. Evaluations of representative WAMs and VLAs reveal a substantial robustness gap: models that remain effective under existing visual and layout randomization can degrade markedly under changes in physical conditions. RoboTwin-Phys provides the benchmark, data, and evaluation protocol needed to systematically measure and improve robustness to physical-condition diversity in robot manipulation.

</details>


### [191] [Shaft-Configuration-Adaptive Catheter Tip Position Estimation via Motor-History Conditioned Residual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26304)
*Peihan Zhang,Michael C. Yip,Ankur Kapoor,Young-Ho Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于电机历史条件化的GRU残差估计器，在无直接杆体构型传感条件下实现心脏内超声导管三维尖端定位，开环RMSE 3.3mm，较恒定曲率基线降低59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机械臂在医疗导航中需要精确的尖端位置估计，但患者解剖与术式布置导致杆体构型任务相关且未知，摩擦、松弛与柔顺性引入迟滞，使尖端估计困难；目标是仅依赖电机测量实现无杆体传感的三维尖端估计。

Method: 先用多方向扫掠策略标定几何导管模型主干，并将电机角度与驱动力矩响应编码为杆体构型上下文向量；运动过程中该上下文条件化一个GRU预测任务空间残差以校正主干模型，上下文在当前杆体构型下固定，循环状态捕捉演化的驱动历史。

Result: 在4根一次性心内超声心动图导管、16种弯曲杆体构型上，开环尖端RMSE达3.3mm，相对恒定曲率基线降低59%。

Conclusion: 电机历史条件化的残差学习可在无直接杆体构型传感下有效补偿迟滞与柔顺误差，为连续体机器人导管尖端定位提供可部署的仅电机测量方案。

Abstract: Tendon-driven continuum manipulators are widely used in medical applications, where accurate tip-position estimation is essential for precise navigation and instrument positioning. However, patient anatomy and procedural setup impose task-dependent unknown shaft configurations, while friction, slack, and compliance introduce hysteresis, making tip estimation challenging. This paper presents a motor-history-conditioned gated recurrent unit (GRU) residual estimator for three-dimensional catheter tip estimation without direct shaft-configuration sensing. First, an initial multidirectional sweep strategy is applied to calibrate a geometric catheter model backbone, and encode the motor-angle and drive-torque response into a shaft-configuration context vector. During subsequent motion, the context conditions a GRU that predicts a task-space residual correcting this backbone, relying on motor measurements alone. The context remains fixed for the current shaft configuration, while the recurrent state captures the evolving actuation history. Across four disposable intra-cardiac echocardiography catheters and 16 bent shaft configurations, the method achieves 3.3mm open-loop tip RMSE, a 59% reduction relative to the constant-curvature baseline.

</details>


### [192] [SafeLoop: Risk-Aware Rollback for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26313)
*Zeyu Lou,Tianran Zhang,Xinquan Yue,Ya Jing,Chenyang Si*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SafeLoop，一种不改变 VLA 模型参数的外挂式安全机制：用视觉+本体感知训练风险预测器输出碰撞与物体失败的概率和时间，再用轻量控制器选择继续、记录检查点或关节空间回退，仿真与真机实验将危险案例降低约 70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽具备通用操作能力，但长时序执行脆弱，状态估计或控制的小误差可能累积为不可逆失败（碰撞、掉物），需要主动预判风险的机制。

Method: SafeLoop 作为非侵入式外包裹模块，不改动基础策略参数：从视觉和本体感知训练风险预测器，输出身体碰撞与物体失败的"发生概率 + 到危险时间"四个值；轻量控制器据预测风险在三种动作中选择——noop（继续执行）、record（保存安全检查点）、rollback（在关节空间后退到最近安全路点并重新查询基础策略以获取替代续行方案）。

Result: 在 24 个 LIBERO 任务（每任务 16 个随机种子）和 3 个真机任务（各 25 次 rollout）上，SafeLoop 的安全-成功权衡优于对比方法，危险案例减少约 70%，同时保持任务成功率与基础策略控制率。

Conclusion: 在长时序 VLA 执行中，外挂式风险预测与回退恢复可在不牺牲任务性能的前提下显著提升安全性，为通用操作策略提供可插拔的安全层。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models are promising for general-purpose manipulation, but long-horizon execution remains fragile. Small state-estimation or control errors can lead to irreversible failures (e.g., collisions and object drops). Avoiding these risks requires a proactive safety mechanism capable of anticipating hazards. In this paper, we introduce SafeLoop, a non-invasive external wrapper that adds hazard prediction and rollback-based recovery to a VLA model without changing its parameters. SafeLoop trains a risk predictor from vision and proprioception to output four values: the probability and time-to-hazard for body collisions and for object failures. A lightweight controller then chooses one of three actions based on the predicted risk: continue execution (noop), save a safety checkpoint (record), or retreat in joint space (rollback). Rollback moves the robot back to a recent safe waypoint and queries the base policy again, which may yield an alternative continuation. Across 24 LIBERO tasks (16 random seeds each) and three real-robot tasks (25 rollouts each), SafeLoop achieves a stronger overall safety-success trade-off than alternative methods, reducing hazard cases by roughly 70% while preserving task success and the base-policy control rate. Project code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [193] [TriWorldBench: A Tri-View Consistency Perspective on Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26314)
*Xuanyi Liu,Haofeng Wang,Ruiqi Li,Danni Yu,Rui Wan,Ruixu Zhang,Siyu Tao,Xue Yang,Shaofeng Zhang,Zicheng Zhang,Jiaqi Zhang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRIWORLDBENCH基准，用同步的头、左腕、右腕三视角视频评估具身世界模型，含50个双臂任务500条episode、19项指标，并给出TWB-Score。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型评估多基于单视角视觉质量，独立评估头视角与腕视角无法判断它们是否描述同一动作与物体状态，因此需要多视角一致性评估。

Method: 构建TRIWORLDBENCH：500条episode、50个双臂操作任务、三路同步视频（头+左腕+右腕）；定义19项指标，覆盖三视角一致性、任务对齐、物理与3D一致性、运动质量、时序一致性与视觉质量；结合跨视角检查与单视角度量，汇总为TWB-Score并保留各视角结果。

Result: 提供可复用的代码、数据与指标定义，能定位预测在哪个视角失败，并将世界模型评估从单视角视觉质量扩展到多视角一致性与任务一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作为具身世界模型提供多视角一致性评测范式，弥补单视角评估的不足。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the outcomes of robot actions to support learning and planning. For robots equipped with head and wrist cameras, this requires complementary views: the head view captures the overall task, while wrist views reveal local gripper-object interactions. However, evaluating these views independently cannot determine whether they describe the same action and object state. We introduce TRIWORLDBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating embodied world models through synchronized head, left-wrist, and right-wrist videos. It contains 500 episodes across 50 bimanual manipulation tasks and uses 19 metrics to assess tri-view consistency, task alignment, physical and 3D coherence, motion quality, temporal consistency, and visual quality. By combining cross-view checks with measurements tailored to each camera, the benchmark evaluates whether plausible individual videos also form a consistent prediction of the intended task. We summarize overall performance with TWB-Score and retain per-view results to identify where predictions fail. This extends world-model evaluation beyond single-view visual quality. Code, data, and metric definitions are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [194] [ArborSplat: Online Semantic Gaussian Splatting SLAM for Orchards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26315)
*Alessandro Masini,Matteo Frosi,Mirko Usuelli,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArborSplat：基于LiDAR里程计与语义3DGS的果园在线SLAM系统，通过类高度带约束与多视角标签融合，实现细结构语义建图，精度与速度均优于SGS-SLAM/GS3LAM/SemGauss-SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园机器人需要保留树干、棚架、果实等细小但语义关键结构的地图。3DGS SLAM虽具备高光度保真度，但其优化以表观驱动为主，将图像语义迁移到3D点对细薄结构不可靠——像素可能从背景表面获得深度。

Method: ArborSplat采用LiDAR里程计跟踪，直接在Gaussian地图上优化语义；利用每个关键帧立体点云拟合地平面，施加类特定的高度带约束；在线融合多视角证据为语义点云，并剔除与局部地面或单目深度不一致的标签；类约束精修将高斯容量留给欠代表结构。

Result: 在苹果园与梨园的休眠期、花期、采摘期评估。全路线12次遍历ATE均低于0.5 m；在共享301帧片段上，训练视角mIoU超SGS-SLAM和GS3LAM 0.23–0.50、留出集mIoU超0.15–0.36，速度提升1.7–7.5倍；SemGauss-SLAM在全部六组上GPU显存溢出。

Conclusion: 面向农业果园的在线语义3DGS SLAM系统，通过地平面高度带先验与多视角一致性过滤，解决了细薄结构语义迁移不可靠问题，在真实果园多季节场景中以更低计算代价取得更高语义建图精度。

Abstract: Orchard robots need maps that preserve small but semantically important structures such as trunks, trellises, and fruit. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM achieves high photometric fidelity. However, its optimization remains appearance-driven, and transferring image semantics to 3D points is unreliable for thin structures, whose pixels may receive depth from background surfaces. We present ArborSplat, an online semantic 3DGS SLAM system that tracks with LiDAR odometry and optimizes semantics directly on the Gaussian map, constrained by class-specific height bands above a ground plane fitted to each keyframe's stereo point cloud, and fuses multi-view evidence into a semantic point cloud online while rejecting labels inconsistent with the local ground surface or with monocular depth. Class-constrained refinement reserves Gaussian capacity for underrepresented structures and, under reduced budgets, increases training-view accuracy on tree classes. We evaluate the approach on apple and pear orchards during dormancy, flowering, and harvesting. On full routes, it keeps ATE below 0.5 m on all 12 traversals. On shared 301-frame segments, it exceeds SGS-SLAM and GS3LAM by 0.23 to 0.50 training-view and 0.15 to 0.36 held-out mIoU while running 1.7 to 7.5 times faster, whereas SemGauss-SLAM runs out of GPU memory on all six.

</details>


### [195] [Hierarchical Floorplan-Guided Vision-Language Exploration for Embodied Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26360)
*Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HFLEX-EQA 提出分层具身问答框架，结合在线分层场景图、VLM 规划、语义前沿探索与楼层平面图先验，在 OpenEQA/ExploreEQA 及四足机器人上验证结构化先验对 EQA 的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EQA 方法虽用 VLM 配合语义地图或场景图引导探索，但探索多依赖局部观测，环境的结构性先验（如楼层平面图）基本未被利用，导致探索效率与语义相关性不足。

Method: 从 RGB-D 观测增量构建分层场景图与开放词表占据地图；VLM 联合推理场景图、任务相关视觉观测、探索历史与估计的拓扑楼层平面图；并提出房间发现策略，利用楼层平面图与开放词表前沿语义，将探索导向语义相关但尚未观测的房间类型。

Result: 在 OpenEQA 与 ExploreEQA 基准上评测，并在真实室内环境的四足机器人上完成部署；结果显示结合 VLM 分层规划与结构先验（楼层平面图）对 EQA 任务有明确收益。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 分层规划与楼层平面图结构先验结合，可提升具身问答中的探索与推理表现，并具备真实机器人部署可行性。

Abstract: Embodied Question Answering (EQA) requires an agent to explore a previously unseen environment, gather relevant information, and answer questions about the scene. Recent approaches leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) together with semantic maps or scene graphs to guide exploration. However, exploration is typically driven only by local observations, while structural priors about the environment remain largely unused. We propose HFLEX-EQA, a hierarchical EQA framework that combines online scene graph construction, VLM- based planning, semantic frontier exploration, and floorplan priors. The system incrementally builds a hierarchical scene graph and an open-vocabulary occupancy map from RGB-D observations, enabling a VLM to jointly reason over the scene graph, task-relevant visual observations, exploration history, and an estimated topological floorplan. Furthermore, we introduce a room-discovery strategy that leverages the floorplan and open-vocabulary frontier semantics to guide exploration toward semantically relevant yet currently unobserved room types. We evaluate HFLEX-EQA on the OpenEQA and ExploreEQA benchmarks and demonstrate deployment on a quadruped robot in real indoor environments. Our results demonstrate the benefit of combining VLM-based hierarchical planning with structural floorplan priors for the EQA task.

</details>


### [196] [MAVP: Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26378)
*Jinhe Tang,Ruixiao Dai,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAVP通过从遥操作演示重建静态地图，将演示的基座轨迹统一表达在共享地图坐标系中，为策略提供显式基座位姿目标预测，并用定位反馈进行低层跟踪，从而提升移动操作任务的执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需要基座与机械臂协同运动并保持精确的空间定位。然而仅由演示训练的策略往往无法可靠实现预期基座运动，导致空间错位，进而引发操作失败。作者希望解决这一执行层面的可靠性问题。

Method: MAVP（Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies）框架：1）从遥操作演示中重建静态地图，并将演示的基座轨迹表达在共享地图坐标系中，从而提供跨演示一致的空间监督；2）执行时策略输入RGB观测、关节状态和机器人当前地图坐标系下的基座位姿，联合预测目标基座位姿、机械臂动作和夹爪动作；3）低层控制器通过前馈运动与位姿误差反馈跟踪预测的基座目标以纠正执行偏差；4）训练时使用位姿噪声增强以提升策略对位姿输入误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 在六个真实世界操作任务和三种策略家族上，MAVP在全部任务中均取得了高于无锚定速度控制（unanchored velocity control）的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 将基座运动显式锚定在共享地图坐标系并辅以定位反馈跟踪，可有效缓解演示训练策略的基座运动不可靠问题，提升移动操作的整体成功率，且该方法对不同策略族具有通用性。

Abstract: Successful mobile manipulation requires coordinated base and arm motion while maintaining accurate spatial positioning. However, demonstration-trained policies can struggle to realise the intended base motion reliably, leading to spatial misalignment and subsequent manipulation failures. We present MAVP (Map-Aware Visuomotor Policies), a framework that improves execution reliability by predicting explicit base-pose targets and tracking them using localisation feedback. MAVP reconstructs a static map from teleoperated demonstrations and expresses demonstrated base trajectories in a shared map frame, providing consistent spatial supervision across demonstrations. At execution time, the policy receives RGB observations, joint states, and the robot's current map-frame base pose, and jointly predicts target base poses, arm actions, and gripper actions. A low-level controller tracks the predicted base targets using feedforward motion and pose error feedback, enabling correction of execution deviations. We additionally use pose-noise augmentation during training to improve robustness to errors in the policy's pose input. Across six real-world manipulation tasks and three policy families, MAVP achieves higher task success rates than unanchored velocity control in all tasks. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [197] [SparseNav: Instruction-conditioned Sparse Semantic Perception for Training-Free Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26408)
*Quanhua Chen,Juhan Kang,Runfeng Lin,ZiFei Zhang,Enquang Feng,Chunran Zheng,Xiwang Dong,Jiarong Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SparseNav：一种免训练、按需感知的语义VLN框架，用轻量BEV几何地图+稀疏地标记忆，按当前子指令决定何时调用开放词汇分割，并支持VLM在混合前沿/局部航点候选中选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地图式VLN依赖持续空间表示，但超出当前指令需求的语义获取会带来不必要的感知开销与无关标注，且持续累积无关物体不仅浪费算力，还会污染VLM规划器所消费的视觉-空间表示。

Method: 训练-free框架，遵循"less-is-more"：持续维护轻量几何BEV地图与稀疏地标记忆；指令管理器跟踪导航进度并识别活跃地标查询；指令条件感知机制仅当被查询地标可见且其度量位置能影响下一步决策时才触发开放词汇分割；所得地标记忆支持VLM在混合前沿与局部方向航点候选间选择。

Result: 无需额外训练即在Val-Unseen上取得R2R-CE 42.8%与RxR-CE 40.7%成功率；进行了语义感知策略与各组件贡献的受控消融；在无预建地图条件下成功部署于搭载Intel RealSense D455 RGB-D（几何建图与地标定位）与Livox MID-360 LiDAR（定位）的Unitree Go2四足机器人，并在多个室内环境验证了指令条件航点导航的有效性。

Conclusion: 按需、指令条件的稀疏语义感知可在不训练的前提下提升连续环境VLN的成功率，并在真实四足平台上实现无预建地图的部署效果。

Abstract: Map-based vision-language navigation (VLN) relies on persistent spatial representations to connect language understanding with geometric planning. However, acquiring semantics beyond the needs of the current instruction can introduce unnecessary perception cost and irrelevant annotations. Continuously accumulating unrelated objects may not only waste computation, but also clutter the visual-spatial representation consumed by the vision-language model (VLM) planner. To address this problem, we present SparseNav, a training-free framework that follows a less-is-more principle for semantic navigation. SparseNav persistently maintains a lightweight geometric bird's-eye-view (BEV) map and sparse landmark memory, acquiring new semantics on demand using the active sub-instruction to decide what is worth grounding. An instruction manager first tracks navigation progress and identifies the active landmark query. An instruction-conditioned perception mechanism then invokes open-vocabulary segmentation when the queried landmark is visible and its metric location can inform the next decision. The resulting landmark memory supports VLM selection among hybrid frontier and local directional waypoint candidates. Without any additional training, SparseNav achieves success rates of 42.8% on R2R-CE and 40.7% on RxR-CE, both on the Val-Unseen splits. Controlled ablations examine semantic perception strategies and the contributions of individual framework components. Furthermore, we successfully deployed SparseNav on a Unitree Go2 quadruped equipped with an Intel RealSense D455 RGB-D camera for geometric mapping and landmark grounding and a Livox MID-360 LiDAR for localization, without a prebuilt map. We validated its effectiveness across multiple indoor environments using instruction-conditioned waypoint navigation.

</details>


### [198] [Sample, Simulate, Select: Physics-in-the-Loop Text-to-Motion for Humanoids Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26420)
*Raphael Memmesheimer,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S³ 用冻结的文本到动作生成模型采样 N 条动作，经方向匹配 IK 重定向到 Unitree G1，再用预训练 SONIC 跟踪策略在刚体动力学中仿真，直接选取执行最好的候选，实现无需训练的"文本→人形机器人"部署闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到动作模型生成的动作虽合理但不满足机器人动力学约束，全身跟踪控制器能可靠执行参考动作却无法重规划不可行动作；近期语言到人形系统靠大规模训练弥合此差距。作者希望量化：仅把部署控制器置于回路（无任何训练）能弥合多少差距，以及差距究竟在哪。

Method: Sample-simulate-select（S³）：每 prompt 从冻结文本到动作模型采样 N=8 条动作；用方向匹配逆运动学重定向到 Unitree G1；用预训练 SONIC 跟踪策略在全刚体动力学下 rollout；按策略执行质量选最优候选。验证器即确定性仿真器，故构造上即达到 any-of-N 上限。另对比运动学验证器（预测跌倒 AUROC 0.90）、与 GMR 重定向器消融，并用标准文本-动作评估器度量执行动作语义保真度（带真实动捕对照）。

Result: 200 条分层 HumanML3D 测试 prompt（N=8）：直立执行率 83.5%→89.5%，硬件门通过 33→85；完整测试集 4184 条：80.5%→89.5%。运动学验证器仅挽回约四分之一增益——对同一 prompt 的候选排序比群体分类更难。选择无法修复的一类：降低骨盆的 prompt，需用重定向机器人数据训练的生成器才可执行。与 GMR 消融后 any-of-8 上限升至 95.0%（失败互补）。177 条门选片段全部在真实 G1 上完成站立，硬件跟踪误差与仿真一致（r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 无需训练、仅将确定性仿真-执行控制器置于回路即可显著缩小文本到动作模型与人形机器人部署间的差距，但存在天花板与结构性失败模式（降低骨盆类 prompt 无法靠选择修复），指向上限位置及选择机制的局限。

Abstract: Text-to-motion models generate plausible human motion but do not model a robot's dynamics; whole-body tracking controllers execute robot references reliably but cannot replan an infeasible one. Recent language-to-humanoid systems bridge this gap by training. We measure how much of the gap closes with no training at all, by putting the deployment controller itself in the loop. Sample-simulate-select (S$^3$) draws $N$ motions per prompt from a frozen text-to-motion model, retargets each to a Unitree G1 by direction-matching inverse kinematics, rolls all of them out under full rigid-body dynamics with the pretrained SONIC tracking policy, and keeps the candidate the policy executed best. Because the verifier is the deterministic simulator itself, S$^3$ attains the any-of-$N$ ceiling by construction; what we measure is where that ceiling lies and what falls short of it. On 200 stratified HumanML3D test prompts with $N=8$, upright execution rises from 83.5% to 89.5% and hardware-gate passes from 33 to 85; on the complete test split (4,184 prompts) it rises from 80.5% to 89.5%. A kinematic verifier that predicts falls well (AUROC 0.90) recovers only a quarter of this gain: ranking a prompt's own candidates is harder than classifying the population. What selection cannot fix is one class, prompts that lower the pelvis, which a generator trained on retargeted robot data does execute. We further score the semantic fidelity of the executed motion with the standard text-motion evaluator, with a real-mocap control that attributes the loss to the robot projection, ablate the retargeter against GMR (complementary failures: the any-of-8 ceiling rises to 95.0% over both), and execute all 177 gate-selected clips on the real G1: every one completes standing, with hardware tracking error matching simulation ($r=0.94$).

</details>


### [199] [Dr-LiSA: Direct Radar-Lidar Scan Alignment for $SE(3)$ Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26423)
*Alex Zhang,Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Dr-LiSA，首个在SE(3)下将2D旋转雷达强度测量直接定位到3D激光雷达地图的直接法，通过学习式前向模型跨模态预测雷达扫描并做光度对齐，性能超越既有雷达-激光方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达对恶劣天气鲁棒，激光雷达在良好条件下提供高保真3D地图，二者互补；但现有雷达-激光定位仅限平面SE(2)，精度普遍不如激光-激光甚至雷达-雷达系统。核心难点在于雷达与激光观测和表征场景结构的方式存在巨大模态鸿沟。

Method: 提出Dr-LiSA直接定位方法：用学习的前向模型从候选位姿处的激光子图预测雷达测量，从而在SE(3)下对预测与实测雷达扫描做直接光度对齐。

Result: 在超过90 km道路数据上，SE(2)设置下优于先前雷达-激光方法，平面精度可与最先进雷达-雷达定位相竞争。

Conclusion: 学习式跨模态前向建模可有效弥合雷达-激光模态鸿沟，实现SE(3)直接雷达-激光定位，为多模态鲁棒定位提供新路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-LiSA, a first-of-its-kind direct method for localizing 2D spinning radar intensity measurements in $SE(3)$ against 3D lidar maps. Radar-lidar localization combines the complementary strengths of the two sensing modalities: radar is robust to adverse weather and precipitation, while lidar provides high-fidelity 3D maps in favourable conditions. However, existing radar-lidar localization methods are restricted to planar $SE(2)$ localization and have generally fallen short of the accuracy achieved by lidar-lidar and even radar-radar systems. A key challenge is the substantial sensing-modality gap between radar and lidar, which observe and represent scene structure in fundamentally different ways. Dr-LiSA bridges this gap using a learned forward model that predicts radar measurements from a lidar submap at a candidate pose, enabling direct photometric alignment of predicted and observed radar scans in $SE(3)$. Dr-LiSA outperforms prior radar-lidar approaches in $SE(2)$ while achieving planar accuracy competitive with state-of-the-art radar-radar localization across more than 90 km of on-road data.

</details>


### [200] [RouteRLT: Learning When and Which RL Specialist Should Control a Vision-Language-Action Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26467)
*Chongyu Zhu,Jaden Hinds,Hyegang Kim,Juan Sebastian Rojas,Ramy Elmallah,Chi-Guhn Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RouteRLT：为预训练 VLA 学习"何时"以及"切换哪个"RL 专家（针对单个精度关键阶段训练的 RL 策略），通过阶段选择器、切换稳定器和动作边界管理器实现分块策略间的平滑接管；在 LIBERO 与真实机器人线缆抓取+插端口任务上验证，可在部署时不依赖特权阶段边界地逼近有边界路由的性能，并保留通用能力与失败恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用 VLA 模型在接触密集的工业精密阶段（如连接器插入、线缆整理）表现不佳；常用做法是用 RL 微调预训练 VLA 以提升任务专精，但有两个未解问题：(1) 微调后如何保留其通用行为能力，(2) 如何判断何时需要 RL 精调、以及应由哪个专用策略接管。即"保留通用性 + 自动路由到专家"的双重需求。

Method: 提出 RouteRLT 路由框架：以一个通用 VLA 为主干，配以若干"RL 专家"（每个专家仅针对一个精度关键阶段训练）。框架包含三个组件——阶段选择器（识别当前应由哪个控制器激活）、稳定器（抑制频繁切换产生的瞬态抖动）、动作边界管理器（处理分块动作输出之间的衔接与边界一致性问题）。训练上属于"学习式路由"而非依赖人工特权阶段标签，部署时不需要阶段边界信息。

Result: 仿真：在多物体 pick-and-place 的 LIBERO 任务上，学习到的路由优于基础 VLA，并在不使用特权阶段边界的情况下达到与"特权边界路由"相当的水平。真机：在多阶段精度关键的"线缆拾取 + 端口插入"任务上，验证了在操作员对齐的交接协议下可自动路由到拾取与插入两个专家。同时观察到路由策略保留了通用 VLA 行为，并支持失败执行后的恢复。

Conclusion: 学习式路由能把 RL 专家控制精确施加在最有价值的精度关键阶段，同时保留通用 VLA 的泛化行为与失败恢复能力，是"通用 VLA + 阶段化 RL 专家"混合控制的一种可行范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide broad manipulation competence, but often struggle during the precision-critical stages that dominate contact-rich industrial tasks such as connector insertion and cable management. A common remedy is to refine a pretrained VLA with reinforcement learning (RL), enabling task-specific improvement beyond behavior cloning. However, how to preserve its generalist behavior while deciding when RL refinement is needed and which specialized policy should act remains an open question. In this work, we present RouteRLT, a routing framework that learns when and which RL specialist, an RL policy trained for a single precision-critical phase, should take control from a generalist VLA. A phase selector identifies the active controller, a stabilizer suppresses transient switches, and an action-boundary manager handles transitions between chunked policy outputs. We evaluate RouteRLT on multi-object pick-and-place tasks in LIBERO, as well as on a real-world cable pickup and port-insertion task with multiple precision-critical stages. In simulation, the learned routing improves over the base VLA and matches routing with privileged phase boundaries, without accessing those boundaries at deployment. The real-robot evaluation validates automatic routing to both the pickup and insertion specialists under an operator-aligned handoff protocol. Altogether, these results show that learned routing applies RL specialist control where precise adaptation is most valuable while preserving generalist VLA behavior, including recovery from failed execution attempts.

</details>


### [201] [Benchmarking Robots for Everyday Environments: From Lab Experiments to Real-World Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26490)
*Raphael Memmesheimer,Martina Overbeck,Dominik Beyer,Björn Kral,Sabine Bellmann,Sven Schneider,Jan Zimmermann,Anna-Maria Meer,Medina Klicic,Simone Roth,Carolin Straßmann,Alexander Arntz,Marlene Wessels,Johannes Kraus,Paul Schweidler,Tristan Schnell,Christoph Zimmermann,Benedikt Pulver,Wilhelm Stork,Martin Gersch,Sven Behnke,Arne Rönnau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出跨学科机器人公开环境基准框架，经三年七次评测（三类真实场景）验证从实验室原型到落地系统的转化关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实验室指标难以反映机器人在非结构化、以人为中心的公共环境中的真实部署需求，存在评估缺口。

Method: 构建跨学科评估框架，覆盖户外公园清洁、人行地下通道清洁、图书馆交互导览三类用例；三年（2023-2025）七次基准活动，含一次共识研讨会与六次现场评估（每用例两次），在真实室内外测试环境评测技术性能与部署影响；由机器人、人机交互、安全与经济领域专家组成专家组，迭代细化为从原型到运行系统的评估概念。

Result: 识别出任务完成度、交互质量、安全性与经济可行性等成功部署的关键因素，并给出评估概念的迭代结果。

Conclusion: 为研究者与从业者提供可操作洞见，帮助弥合机器人创新与真实世界可用性之间的差距。

Abstract: This study introduces an interdisciplinary framework for benchmarking robots deployed in public environments, addressing the gap between traditional laboratory metrics and real-world benchmarking requirements. We evaluate three distinct robots across diverse use cases - outdoor park cleaning, pedestrian underpass cleaning, and interactive library assistance - each representing unique challenges in public daily life. Over a three-year benchmarking process (2023-2025) comprising seven benchmarking events, a consensus workshop and six on-site evaluations (two per use case), we utilized realistic indoor and outdoor test environments to assess not only technical performance but also the broader implications of deploying robots in unstructured, human-centric settings. An expert panel, spanning robotics, human-robot interaction, safety, and economics, systematically developed and refined an evaluation concept to analyze the transition from laboratory prototypes to operational systems. Our findings highlight critical factors for successful deployment, including task fulfillment, interaction quality, safety, and economic feasibility. This work provides actionable insights for researchers and practitioners aiming to bridge the gap between robotic innovation and real-world applicability.

</details>


### [202] [Generalizing Manipulation Skills with a Local Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26499)
*Raman Talwar,Elias Nijs,Andreas Verleysen,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本地开放权重VLM（Qwen3-27B）通过代码智能体框架直接编写并执行代码控制UR3e机械臂，在儿童玩具构成的九类任务上实现零样本泛化，45次试验中30次成功，重复任务时耗时减少50%，展现自我改进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言驱动机器人多依赖固定动作接口或训练策略，泛化到新任务需额外工程或数据采集，成本高。作者希望验证本地开放权重VLM能否在无新编程、无新训练的前提下一次性泛化到任务新变体。

Method: 让开放权重VLM（Qwen3-27B）作为编码智能体，在提供运动学、安全限制与经典计算机视觉服务的底层机器人服务之上自主编写并运行代码，驱动UR3e机械臂。在九类基于儿童玩具设计、针对颜色/尺寸/形状及任务变异的泛化测试任务上各做五次试验，并测试成功完成任务后重做同一任务是否存在加速。

Result: 45次试验中30次观察到泛化成功，单次耗时3.4至67.5分钟，取决于任务复杂度；成功完成后重做同一任务耗时平均减少50%，表明存在自我改进；同时揭示了本地编码智能体的若干局限。

Conclusion: 本地开放权重VLM编码智能体可零样本泛化到未见任务变体，并具备任务内自我改进能力；解决其当前局限并进一步研究自我改进机制，是通向本地编码智能体实际部署的直接路径。

Abstract: Today, progress in open-weight language models enables systems capable of writing, executing and debugging code while still running on a single workstation. Most language-driven robots give the model a fixed action interface or a trained policy. Generalizing to a new task therefore means more engineering effort or more data collection, both time-consuming. We investigate whether a local open-weight vision-language model can control a robot and one-shot generalize to new variations of a task without new human programming or training. We let a local open-weight VLM, Qwen3.8-27B, drive a UR3e robotic arm from a coding-agent harness. It writes and runs its own code above a service that implements kinematics, safety limits and classic computer vision techniques. We investigate if this system is capable of generalizing to unseen tasks. Specifically, we test it on nine tasks built from children's toys designed to probe generalization capability across various object characteristics: color, size, shape, and task variation of those. With five trials for each task, we observe generalization in 30 out of 45 trials with durations ranging from 3.4 to 67.5 minutes depending on task complexity. We further test if there is a speedup when an agent is asked to redo the task after successful completion. This resulted in a 50% reduction in duration, indicating that there is self-improvement over time. Finally, we expose the limitations of a local coding agent. We believe that solving those limitations combined with further investigation of self-improvement over time points at a direct path toward real-world deployment of a local coding agent.

</details>


### [203] [MATE: Multi-Agent Virtual Teleoperation Platform for Humanoid Collaboration Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26520)
*Yichuan Yu,Youzhuo Wang,Yiming Ren,Di Feng,Yexuan Yang,Bingxi Yang,Shengxiao Gong,Yujing Sun,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MATE 是一个多智能体虚拟遥操作平台，允许多名异地操作员在同一物理仿真环境中协同控制多台全身人形机器人，采集了24.1小时、2500个联合episode、5类长时程任务的多机协作数据集，并提出执行对齐交互采样策略EAIS以提升模仿学习效果，实现从虚拟示教到真实人形机器人的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要多样化的具身经验来获得复杂移动操作与协作技能，但现有人形数据采集流程多针对单体智能体；物理多机器人协作因硬件昂贵、专用场地和反复复位而难以规模化。因此需要一种既能保留物理耦合交互、又能低成本大规模采集多机协作数据的手段。

Method: 提出 MATE——面向人形协作数据采集的多智能体虚拟遥操作平台：多名地理分布的操作员在共享的物理仿真环境中同时控制多台全身人形机器人，去除多台实体机器人与同地操作的需求，同时保留人形—物体—环境之间的物理耦合交互。基于该平台构建包含24.1小时协调行为、2500个联合episode、5类长时程任务（物体交接、接力递送、环境交互、协同搬运）的数据集。进一步提出 EAIS（Execution-Aligned Interaction Sampling）——在执行对齐前缀内计算采样信号，优先采样推进任务进度与交互关键的行为。

Result: 在多种协作任务上以代表性模仿学习与视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略进行评估，实验显示数据采集高效、策略学习有效，并可从虚拟示教零样本迁移到真实人形机器人，无需真实世界微调。

Conclusion: MATE 通过虚拟多智能体遥操作绕开物理多机器人协作的数据采集瓶颈，结合 EAIS 采样策略产出高质量协作示教，验证了仿真协作数据到真实人形机器人的可迁移性，为多机具身协作学习提供了可扩展的数据与训练路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots require diverse embodied experiences to acquire complex loco-manipulation and collaborative skills. However, existing humanoid data pipelines primarily focus on individual agents, while physical multi-robot collaboration remains difficult to scale due to costly hardware, dedicated spaces, and repeated resets. In this work, we introduce MATE, a Multi-Agent virtual TEleoperation platform for humanoid collaboration data collection that enables multiple geographically distributed operators to simultaneously control whole-body humanoids in a shared physics-based environment. MATE removes the need for multiple physical robots and co-located operation while preserving physically coupled interactions among humanoids, objects, and environments. Using MATE, we construct a multi-humanoid collaboration dataset comprising 24.1 hours of coordinated behavior across 2,500 joint episodes and five long-horizon tasks, including object handover, relay delivery, environment interaction, and cooperative transport. To improve learning from these interaction-rich demonstrations, we introduce EAIS, an Execution-Aligned Interaction Sampling strategy that computes sampling signals within an execution-aligned prefix and prioritizes task-progressing and interaction-critical behaviors. We evaluate MATE with representative imitation learning and vision-language-action policies across diverse collaboration tasks. Experiments demonstrate efficient data collection, effective policy learning, and zero-shot transfer from virtual demonstrations to a physical humanoid without real-world fine-tuning. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [204] [Learning Air-Ground Motion Control with Temporal Mode Switching and Cross-Terrain Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26564)
*Ruitian Pang,Mingrui Li,Xuanting Liu,Tiancheng Lai,Juncheng Chen,Xiangyu Li,Ruibin Zhang,Qishao Wang,Jin Yu,Haiyin Piao,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对被动轮式陆空两栖机器人，提出学习式陆空运动控制框架：用历史单点ToF测距与状态加未来参考训练模式选择器，用强化学习策略做轨迹跟踪，仿真与真机验证了受限感知下的可靠模式切换与多地形鲁棒跟踪（101m航迹RMSE 0.08m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮式TABV兼具飞行机动性与地面低能耗，但在机载感知有限的条件下，空中-地面模式切换可靠性不足，且跨多样地形的地面轨迹跟踪鲁棒性差，面向实际应用仍具挑战。

Method: 1) 学习式模式选择器：输入历史单点ToF测距、机器人状态及未来参考信息，输出当前应激活的运动模式；2) 强化学习轨迹跟踪策略：融合本体感知观测与未来参考信息以预判轨迹变化，地面运动阶段采用多地形训练与动力学随机化提升跨地形鲁棒性。

Result: 仿真与真机实验中实现受限感知下可靠陆空切换与多地形精确地面跟踪；学习式选择器在困难切换场景优于规则式选择器；地面控制器在所有测试条件下位置RMSE低于PID，并在NMPC失效处仍保持可用跟踪；系统完成101m陆空轨迹、多次自主模式切换，位置RMSE为0.08m。

Conclusion: 学习式框架可有效解决被动轮式TABV的自主模式切换与跨地形跟踪问题，具备实机部署潜力，为陆空两栖平台在真实场景中的自主运动提供可行方案。

Abstract: Passive-wheeled terrestrial-aerial bimodal vehicles (TABVs) combine aerial mobility with energy-efficient ground locomotion. However, reliable air-ground mode switching under limited onboard perception and robust ground trajectory tracking across diverse terrains remain challenging when targeting real-world applications. In this work, we propose a learning-based air-ground motion control framework for passive-wheeled TABVs: 1) a learned mode selector for autonomous air-ground motion mode switching. The selector uses historical single-point time-of-flight (ToF) measurements and robot states together with future reference information to determine the active locomotion mode. 2) a reinforcement learning control policy for trajectory tracking. The policy combines proprioceptive observations with future reference information to anticipate trajectory changes. For ground locomotion, multi-terrain training and dynamics randomization enable robust tracking across different terrains. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate reliable air-ground switching under limited perception and accurate ground tracking across diverse terrain conditions. The learned selector outperforms a rule-based mode selector in challenging transitions, while the ground controller achieves lower position RMSE than PID across all tested conditions and maintains decent tracking where NMPC fails. With these capabilities integrated, the system tracks a 101m air-ground trajectory through multiple autonomous mode transitions with a position RMSE of 0.08m.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond End-Task Success: How to Audit Visual Experience Retrieval in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26567)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"审计"方法论：在多个操作任务、复用机制与库规模（K=3/10/50）下穷举执行每个存储经验于每个查询场景，从而将复用规则的得分拆解为"每场景选择质量"与"经验库质量"两部分，并指出常见基于视觉相似度的选择规则得分被库中单个可迁移经验"抬高"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人通过复用过去经验而非重训练来适应新场景，但现有系统多以视觉相似度选择经验，评估也只报告被选中经验的成功率。该指标无法反映选择规则本身的好坏——一条规则可以靠反复使用某个广泛可迁移的经验获得高分，也可能因其偏好经验本身较弱而低分。由于得分混淆了"规则"与"经验库"两个因素，会误导领域后续的构建方向，因此需要一个能区分二者的审计方法。

Method: 提出穷举式审计方法：在2个操作任务、3种复用机制、K=3/10/50的经验库规模下，将每个存储经验在每一个查询场景中实际执行，从而获知所有候选结果的完整真值。被审计的选择规则基于5种视觉嵌入（从原始像素到CLIP）的最近邻距离。由于已知每个备选经验的结果，可把分数归因到每场景选择质量或经验库质量。并进一步将规则与"保持选择频率但随机配对场景"的shuffle基线比较，同时用AUROC分别评估视觉距离在"跨场景配对的成败预测"与"单场景内候选排序"两种能力上的表现。结论上提炼为两个廉价报告：所选经验的分布，以及事后最优单经验的成功率。

Result: (1) 事后选定的单一固定经验即可捕获随机选择与oracle之间30-58%的差距，每场景选择仅争夺剩余0.07-0.15的成功率。(2) 当K≥10时，视觉规则的集中度是oracle的1.5-3倍，其得分随后跟随该经验的质量。(3) 只要规则显著偏离保持选择率但随机配对的shuffle，规则就更差，且对所有学习型图像策略成立。(4) 视觉距离能较好预测某配对是否成功（AUROC最高0.96），但在K=50时，对5种嵌入中的4种，其单场景内候选排序能力不超过随机水平（AUROC 0.45-0.52）。

Conclusion: 现有基于视觉相似度的经验选择评估存在系统性误导：规则得分很大程度上反映经验库质量而非选择能力，且视觉距离擅长判断"能否成功"却不擅长在同场景内排序候选。由于穷举执行通常不可行，作者建议任何研究只需给出两个廉价报告——所选经验的分布与事后最优单经验的成功率——即可揭示选择的真实质量。

Abstract: Robots that store past experiences must select which one to reuse in a new scene. Most systems select by visual similarity, and most evaluations report only the success of the selected experience. That number does not show whether the selection was good: a rule can score well by repeatedly using one broadly transferable experience, or poorly because its preferred experience is weak. Since robots increasingly adapt by reuse rather than retraining, a score that describes the library rather than the rule misleads what the field builds next. We contribute an audit methodology: execute every stored experience in every query scene, over two manipulation tasks, three reuse mechanisms, and libraries of $K=3$, $10$, and $50$. Because every alternative's outcome is known, a score can be traced to per-scene selection or to library quality. The audited rules select by nearest-neighbor distance in five visual embeddings, from raw pixels to CLIP. (1) One fixed experience, chosen with hindsight, captures 30-58% of the gap between random selection and an oracle; per-scene selection competes for the remaining 0.07-0.15 in success rate. (2) At $K\ge10$, visual rules concentrate on one experience 1.5-3 times more than the oracle does, and their scores then follow that experience's quality. (3) Wherever a rule differs significantly from a shuffle that keeps its selection rates but pairs them with scenes at random, the rule is worse, for every learned image policy. (4) Visual distance predicts well whether a given pair will succeed (AUROC up to 0.96), yet ranks the candidates within one scene no better than chance for four of five embeddings at $K=50$ (AUROC 0.45-0.52). Exhaustive execution is usually infeasible, so the audit reduces to two cheap reports any study can give: the distribution of selected experiences, and the success of the best single experience in hindsight.

</details>


### [206] [Wheel-loader V-Cycle Automation with Deep Koopman MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26580)
*Armin Abdolmohammadi,Navid Mojahed,Dinesh Kumar,Bahram Ravani,Shima Nazari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层框架，将长时域几何规划与数据驱动预测控制结合，实现轮式装载机自主土方作业：用降阶铰接运动学模型生成前进/倒车联合优化的V型作业几何，用高保真仿真数据训练前后向深度双线性Koopman模型，嵌入MPC实现50ms实时轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式装载机土方作业中反复的前进-倒车动作天然适合自动化，但铰接式车辆的非线性动力学与复杂的车辆-地形交互使传统基于模型的方法效果受限，需要兼顾全局作业几何与局部非线性动力学的分层方案。

Method: 分层框架：上层用降阶铰接运动学模型生成机动几何，通过共享中间状态对前进与倒车轨迹联合优化；下层基于Algoryx Dynamics高保真仿真数据，分别学习前进与倒车两个数据驱动的深度双线性Koopman模型以捕捉车辆动力学，并将其嵌入计算高效的MPC中进行轨迹跟踪。

Result: 控制器在50ms执行回路内实时运行；高保真仿真结果表明，端到端框架可准确且计算高效地完成轮式装载机V-cycle作业机动。

Conclusion: 该工作表明几何规划与Koopman-MPC相结合的分层架构是铰接式重型机械自主作业的可行路径，未来需向真实机器部署与更复杂地形工况拓展。

Abstract: The repeated forward-reverse maneuvers performed by wheel loaders during earthmoving operations make them well suited for automation. However, the nonlinear dynamics of articulated vehicles and complex vehicle-terrain interactions limit the effectiveness of conventional model-based approaches. This paper presents a hierarchical framework that combines long-horizon geometric planning with data-driven predictive control for autonomous wheel-loader operation. A reduced-order articulated kinematic model is used to generate the maneuver geometry, where the forward and reverse trajectories are jointly optimized through a shared intermediate state. To capture the vehicle dynamics, two data-driven deep bilinear Koopman models are learned for the forward and reverse motions using data generated from high-fidelity simulations in Algoryx Dynamics. The learned Koopman representations are subsequently incorporated into a computationally efficient model predictive control (MPC) formulation for trajectory tracking. The resulting controller operates in real time within a 50-ms execution loop. High-fidelity simulation results demonstrate that the proposed end-to-end framework enables accurate and computationally efficient execution of wheel-loader V-cycle maneuvers, providing a promising approach toward autonomous operation of articulated heavy-duty machinery.

</details>


### [207] [NavSafe-$\infty$: Benchmarking Closed-Loop Driving Safety in Photorealistic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26618)
*Yuxin Bao,Hongwei Ruan,Luobin Wang,Seth Z. Zhao,Ziyang Leng,Zihan Zhang,Yu Zeng,Rowan McAllister,Henrik Christensen,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NavSafe-∞闭环基准（280场景、28类事件、按安全分类法给出四类能力分数），评测20个端到端驾驶策略，发现开环收益无法可靠迁移到闭环安全，并揭示示范扰动与开环RL微调的局限与奖励劫持问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环基准无法反映策略对累积误差的鲁棒性、故障恢复与交互安全性，E2E驾驶策略的评测存在盲区。

Method: 构建照片级真实感闭环基准NavSafe-∞，覆盖280个场景、28种事件类型，每类含基于结构化交通安全分类法定义的成功/失败判据，可输出交通碰撞、弱势道路使用者碰撞、交通违规与交通事故事件四类能力分数；并评测20个E2E策略，另对两种常见改进手段（被动示范扰动、开环RL微调）进行分析，配套可扩展工具箱支持事件定制与策略诊断。

Result: 开环指标提升不能可靠转化为闭环安全性；示范扰动仅在闭环轨迹接近扰动训练状态时有效；开环RL微调出现奖励劫持，用安全裕度换取自我推进，闭环反馈将其放大为累积性安全关键错误。

Conclusion: 开环基准存在预示闭环安全成功的盲区，需以闭环安全分类学评测E2E驾驶策略；基准与工具箱将开源维护以推动后续研究。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) driving policies have progressed rapidly on open-loop (OL) benchmarks, yet OL evaluation cannot reveal whether a policy withstands compounding errors, recovers from failures, or interacts safely with surrounding actors. We introduce NavSafe-$\infty$, a photorealistic closed-loop (CL) benchmark of 280 scenarios spanning 28 event types, each with success and failure criteria defined within a structured traffic-safety taxonomy, which yields category-level capability scores for Traffic Crashes, Vulnerable Road User Crashes, Traffic Violations, and Traffic Incidents. Evaluating 20 E2E policies, we find that OL gains do not reliably transfer to CL safety. Analyzing two common remedies further shows that passive demonstration perturbation helps mainly when CL rollouts stay near its perturbed training states, and that OL reinforcement-learning fine-tuning exhibits reward hacking by trading safety margin for ego progress, which CL feedback amplifies into compounding safety-critical errors. Together, these results demonstrate the blind spot of OL benchmarks indicating CL safety success. The benchmark and an extensible toolbox for customizable event curation and policy diagnosis will be open-sourced and maintained to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [208] [Imperfection for Precision: Upcycling Imperfect Data for High-Precision Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26672)
*Hao Wei,Yang Liu,Chao Tang,Shengbao Li,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Zhaofeng Cao,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ε4P 方法，通过按流匹配轨迹噪声水平分段利用低精度目标任务数据与高精度非匹配任务数据，减少高精度 VLA 操作对昂贵遥操作数据的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度操作 VLA 训练依赖任务专用高质量数据（如遥操作），采集慢且昂贵；而低精度目标任务数据与高精度跨任务数据通常被丢弃。

Method: ε4P 在流匹配（flow matching）轨迹上控制各数据源的贡献位置：高噪声段使用低精度、目标任务数据以保留高层任务语境，低噪声段使用高精度、任务不匹配数据以迁移底层动作精度，而非简单混合共训练。

Result: 在真实机器人上对亚毫米级高精度任务与粗粒度任务实验：利用额外不完美数据可提升策略性能最多 31.7 个百分点；用等量非匹配数据替代任务专用高质量数据时平均性能仅下降 4.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: ε4P 提供了一种可扩展的高精度操作范式，可系统性地“升级利用”异构不完美数据，降低对昂贵任务专用高质量数据的依赖。

Abstract: Training vision-language-action (VLA) models for high-precision manipulation typically requires task-specific, high-quality data (e.g., teleoperation), which is slow and expensive to collect. To reduce this burden without compromising manipulation precision, we propose $\varepsilon$4P (Imperfection for Precision), a simple yet effective method that "upcycles" two otherwise discarded data sources: (1) low-precision data from the target task and (2) high-precision data from mismatched tasks. Rather than naively mixing these imperfect data sources throughout co-training, $\varepsilon$4P controls where each source contributes along the flow-matching trajectory. Specifically, low-precision, target-task data is used at high noise to preserve high-level task context and high-precision, task-mismatched data is used at low noise to transfer low-level action precision. Through real-robot experiments on both sub-millimeter, high-precision tasks and coarse-grained tasks, we demonstrate that the proposed method (1) effectively leverages additional imperfect data to improve policy performance by up to 31.7 percentage points, and (2) can replace an equal amount of task-specific, high-quality data with an average performance drop of only 4.2 percentage points. Overall, $\varepsilon$4P points toward a scalable paradigm for high-precision manipulation, in which heterogeneous, imperfect data can be systematically repurposed to reduce reliance on costly task-specific, high-quality data. More details are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [209] [Underwater Navigation in Unsteady Flows Using Measurement Histories from a Single Sensing Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26753)
*Linhao Jin,Qimin Feng,Peter Gunnarson,Qiang Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用因果观测器从单点流速历史估计侧向流速，为固定导航控制器提供虚拟传感，在圆柱尾流训练后可跨Re与部分几何泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下导航依赖空间流场测量，但机器人尺寸与传感器布局限制了分布式传感；希望用单点测量历史替代多点空间采样。

Method: 构建因果观测器（flow-history observer），输入为单一传感单元的历史测量（体坐标系环境流速），输出当前侧向流速估计，并接入固定导航控制器；在二维尾流仿真中评估，仅用圆柱尾流Re=100训练。

Result: 在未重训练的Re=205/240上成功率84.4%/80.6%，比直接空间传感低7.4/4.6个百分点，比只含当前时刻的观测器高30个百分点以上；方形棱柱尾流接近直接传感，三角棱柱尾流性能下降；消融表明侧向速度差为控制关键量，并对误差持续性敏感。

Conclusion: 在给定观测模型下，单点流速历史的闭环虚拟传感具有可行性，但泛化受几何与误差动态限制。

Abstract: Spatial flow measurements support underwater navigation, but distributed sensing is constrained by robot size and sensor layout.
